import Link from "next/link"

export default async function SignInPage() {
  const user = await getSessionUser()
  if (user) redirect("/")

  return (
    <AuthShell
      title="Bienvenido de nuevo"
      subtitle="IngresÃ¡ a tu cuenta para gestionar tus turnos."
      footer={
        <>
          Â¿No tenÃ©s cuenta?{" "}
          <Link
            href="/sign-up"
            className="font-medium text-primary hover:underline"
          >
            Crear cuenta
          </Link>
        </>
      }
    >
      <AuthForm mode="sign-in" />
      <DemoCredentials />
    </AuthShell>
  )
}

===== .\src\app\layout.tsx =====

import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next"
import { Inter, Geist_Mono } from "next/font/google"
import { cookies } from "next/headers"

import "./globals.css"
import ThemeProvider from "@/components/ThemeProvider"
import { TooltipProvider } from "@/components/ui/tooltip"
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/sonner"
import { THEME_COOKIE, normalizeTheme, themeClassName } from "@/lib/theme"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const inter = Inter({ subsets: ["latin"], variable: "--font-sans" })

const fontMono = Geist_Mono({
  subsets: ["latin"],
  variable: "--font-mono",
})

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "SaludPlus Â· GestiÃ³n de Turnos MÃ©dicos",
  description:
    "Portal integral para reservar y gestionar turnos mÃ©dicos: pacientes, profesionales y administraciÃ³n en un solo lugar.",
  generator: "v0.app",
}

export const viewport: Viewport = {
  themeColor: "#2563eb",
}

export default async function RootLayout({
  children,
}: Readonly<{
  children: React.ReactNode
}>) {
  // El tema se decide acÃ¡, en el servidor, antes de generar el HTML: asÃ­ la
  // pÃ¡gina sale directamente con el color correcto y no hace falta ningÃºn
  // script que la corrija antes del primer pintado. Cuando el valor es
  // "system" no se escribe ninguna clase y el color lo resuelve el
  // `prefers-color-scheme` del variante `dark` en globals.css.
  const theme = normalizeTheme((await cookies()).get(THEME_COOKIE)?.value)

  return (
    <html
      lang="es"
      className={cn(
        "bg-background antialiased",
        fontMono.variable,
        "font-sans",
        inter.variable,
        themeClassName(theme)
      )}
    >
      <body>
        <ThemeProvider initialTheme={theme}>
          <TooltipProvider>{children}</TooltipProvider>
          <Toaster richColors position="top-center" />
        </ThemeProvider>
      </body>
    </html>
  )
}

===== .\src\app\page.tsx =====

import { redirect } from "next/navigation"
import AdminView from "@/components/admin/AdminView"
import DoctorView from "@/components/doctor/DoctorView"
import Navbar from "@/components/Navbar"
import PatientView from "@/components/patient/PatientView"
import { getSessionUser } from "@/lib/auth/session"
import { getAppointments } from "@/routes/AppointmentRoutes"
import { getMedics, getMyMedicProfile } from "@/routes/MedicRoutes"
import { getMyPatientProfile, getPatients } from "@/routes/PatientRoutes"
import { getProfileSummary } from "@/routes/ProfileRoutes"
import {
  getActiveBranches,
  getBranches,
} from "@/routes/BranchRoutes"
import { getMyReviewedAppointmentIds } from "@/routes/ReviewRoutes"
import { getSpecialties } from "@/routes/SpecialtyRoutes"
import { getUsers } from "@/routes/UserRoutes"

export default async function Page() {
  const user = await getSessionUser()
  if (!user) redirect("/sign-in")

  const profile = await getProfileSummary()

  return (
    <div className="min-h-svh bg-background">
      <Navbar user={user} profile={profile} />
      <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 sm:py-8">
        <div className="animate-in duration-500 fade-in-50 slide-in-from-bottom-2">
          {user.role === "patient" && <PatientDashboard />}
          {user.role === "doctor" && <DoctorDashboard />}
          {user.role === "admin" && <AdminDashboard />}
        </div>
      </main>
      <footer className="border-t border-border/70 py-6">
        <p className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground sm:px-6">
          SaludPlus Â· Sistema de GestiÃ³n de Turnos MÃ©dicos Â· Proyecto acadÃ©mico
          de Desarrollo de Software
        </p>
      </footer>
    </div>
  )
}

async function PatientDashboard() {
  const [profile, appointments, specialties, medics, branches, reviewed] =
    await Promise.all([
      getMyPatientProfile(),
      getAppointments(),
      getSpecialties(),
      getMedics(),
      // Solo sedes en servicio: no tiene sentido ofrecer una dada de baja.
      getActiveBranches(),
      getMyReviewedAppointmentIds(),
    ])

  return (
    <PatientView
      patientName={profile?.firstName ?? "Paciente"}
      appointments={appointments}
      specialties={specialties}
      medics={medics}
      branches={branches}
      reviewedAppointmentIds={reviewed}
    />
  )
}

async function DoctorDashboard() {
  const [profile, appointments, patients] = await Promise.all([
    getMyMedicProfile(),
    getAppointments(),
    getPatients(),
  ])

  return (
    <DoctorView
      doctorName={
        profile ? `${profile.firstName} ${profile.lastName}` : "Profesional"
      }
      specialtyName={profile?.specialtyName ?? ""}
      appointments={appointments}
      patients={patients}
    />
  )
}

async function AdminDashboard() {
  const [users, patients, medics, specialties, branches] = await Promise.all([
    getUsers(),
    getPatients(),
    getMedics(),
    getSpecialties(),
    // El panel ve todas, incluidas las inactivas, para poder reactivarlas.
    getBranches(),
  ])

  return (
    <AdminView
      users={users}
      patients={patients}
      medics={medics}
      specialties={specialties}
      branches={branches}
    />
  )
}

===== .\src\components\admin\AdminView.tsx =====

"use client"

import { useMemo, useState, useTransition } from "react"
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation"
import {
  HeartPulse,
  MapPin,
  Plus,
  Search,
  Stethoscope,
  UserRound,
  Users,
} from "lucide-react"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { normalizeDni } from "@/lib/format/dni"
import type { ActionResult } from "@/lib/actionResult"
import type { BranchDto } from "@/models/Branch"
import type { MedicDto } from "@/models/Medic"
import type { PatientDto } from "@/models/Patient"
import type { SpecialtyDto } from "@/models/Specialty"
import type { UserDto } from "@/models/User"
import { deleteBranch, saveBranch } from "@/routes/BranchRoutes"
import { deleteMedic, saveMedic } from "@/routes/MedicRoutes"
import { deletePatient, savePatient } from "@/routes/PatientRoutes"
import { deleteSpecialty, saveSpecialty } from "@/routes/SpecialtyRoutes"
import { deleteUser, saveUser } from "@/routes/UserRoutes"
import ConfirmDialog from "@/components/ConfirmDialog"
import BranchesTable from "@/components/admin/BranchesTable"
import MedicsTable from "@/components/admin/MedicsTable"
import PatientsTable from "@/components/admin/PatientsTable"
import RecordFormDialog, {
  newRecordLabels,
  type AdminRecord,
  type EditableEntity,
  type RecordFormValues,
} from "@/components/admin/RecordFormDialog"
import SpecialtiesGrid from "@/components/admin/SpecialtiesGrid"
import UsersTable from "@/components/admin/UsersTable"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs"

interface DeleteTarget {
  id: string
  name: string
}

function matches(query: string, ...fields: (string | undefined)[]): boolean {
  if (!query.trim()) return true
  const needle = query.toLowerCase()
  return fields.some((field) => field?.toLowerCase().includes(needle))
}

export default function AdminView({
  users: initialUsers,
  patients: initialPatients,
  medics: initialMedics,
  specialties: initialSpecialties,
  branches: initialBranches,
}: {
  users: UserDto[]
  patients: PatientDto[]
  medics: MedicDto[]
  specialties: SpecialtyDto[]
  branches: BranchDto[]
}) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<EditableEntity>("users")
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("")
  const [dialogOpen, setDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<AdminRecord | null>(null)
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [toDelete, setToDelete] = useState<DeleteTarget | null>(null)
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition()

  const totalRecords =
    initialUsers.length +
    initialPatients.length +
    initialMedics.length +
    initialSpecialties.length +
    initialBranches.length

  const filteredUsers = useMemo(
    () =>
      initialUsers.filter((user) => matches(query, user.name, user.email)),
    [initialUsers, query]
  )
  const filteredPatients = useMemo(() => {
    // El DNI puede estar guardado con puntos o sin ellos, y la persona puede
    // escribirlo de cualquiera de las dos formas: se comparan solo los dÃ­gitos.
    const dniQuery = normalizeDni(query)

    return initialPatients.filter(
      (patient) =>
        matches(
          query,
          patient.firstName,
          patient.lastName,
          patient.email,
          patient.dni
        ) ||
        (dniQuery.length > 0 && normalizeDni(patient.dni).includes(dniQuery))
    )
  }, [initialPatients, query])
  const filteredMedics = useMemo(
    () =>
      initialMedics.filter((medic) =>
        matches(
          query,
          medic.firstName,
          medic.lastName,
          medic.email,
          medic.specialtyName
        )
      ),
    [initialMedics, query]
  )
  const filteredSpecialties = useMemo(
    () =>
      initialSpecialties.filter((specialty) =>
        matches(query, specialty.name, specialty.description)
      ),
    [initialSpecialties, query]
  )

  const filteredBranches = useMemo(
    () =>
      initialBranches.filter((branch) =>
        matches(query, branch.name, branch.address, branch.phone)
      ),
    [initialBranches, query]
  )

  function handleTabChange(value: string) {
    setTab(value as EditableEntity)
    setQuery("")
  }

  function openCreate() {
    setEditing(null)
    setFormError(null)
    setDialogOpen(true)
  }

  function openEdit(record: AdminRecord) {
    setEditing(record)
    setFormError(null)
    setDialogOpen(true)
  }

  /**
   * Mutations return the failure as a value instead of throwing, so the message
   * written in the validation layer is the one the admin actually reads.
   */
  function runSave(operation: () => Promise<ActionResult<unknown>>) {
    setFormError(null)
    startTransition(async () => {
      const result = await operation()
      if (!result.ok) {
        setFormError(result.error)
        return
      }
      toast.success(editing ? "Registro actualizado" : "Registro creado")
      setDialogOpen(false)
      setEditing(null)
      router.refresh()
    })
  }

  function handleSubmit(values: RecordFormValues) {
    const id = editing?.id

    if (tab === "patients") {
      const record = editing as PatientDto | null
      runSave(() =>
        savePatient({
          id,
          firstName: values.firstName,
          lastName: values.lastName,
          email: values.email,
          phone: values.phone,
          dni: values.dni,
          birthDate: values.birthDate,
          bloodType: record?.bloodType,
        })
      )
      return
    }

    if (tab === "doctors") {
      const record = editing as MedicDto | null
      runSave(() =>
        saveMedic({
          id,
          firstName: values.firstName,
          lastName: values.lastName,
          email: values.email,
          phone: values.phone,
          specialtyId: values.specialtyId,
          branchId: values.branchId,
          office: values.office,
          license: values.license,
          avatar: record?.avatar,
          // `rating` no se manda: es el promedio de las reseÃ±as, no un dato
          // que el administrador pueda escribir.
          yearsExperience: values.yearsExperience,
          workDays: values.workDays,
          startTime: values.startTime,
          endTime: values.endTime,
          breakStart: values.breakStart,
          breakEnd: values.breakEnd,
          slotMinutes: values.slotMinutes,
        })
      )
      return
    }

    if (tab === "branches") {
      runSave(() =>
        saveBranch({
          id,
          name: values.name,
          address: values.address,
          phone: values.phone,
          active: values.active,
        })
      )
      return
    }

    if (tab === "specialties") {
      runSave(() =>
        saveSpecialty({
          id,
          name: values.name,
          description: values.description,
          icon: values.icon,
        })
      )
      return
    }

    runSave(() =>
      saveUser(
        {
          id,
          name: values.name,
          email: values.email,
          role: values.role,
        },
        values.password
      )
    )
  }

  function confirmDelete() {
    if (!toDelete) return
    const target = toDelete
    setToDelete(null)

    startTransition(async () => {
      const remove =
        tab === "patients"
          ? deletePatient
          : tab === "doctors"
            ? deleteMedic
            : tab === "specialties"
              ? deleteSpecialty
              : tab === "branches"
                ? deleteBranch
                : deleteUser

      const result = await remove(target.id)
      if (!result.ok) {
        // The row is still there, so nothing is removed from the UI either.
        toast.error(result.error)
        return
      }
      toast.success(`${target.name} eliminado`)
      router.refresh()
    })
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight sm:text-3xl">
          Panel de administraciÃ³n
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">
          GestionÃ¡ las tablas maestras del sistema Â· {totalRecords} registros.
        </p>
      </div>

      <Tabs value={tab} onValueChange={handleTabChange} className="gap-5">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <TabsList>
            <TabsTrigger value="users">
              <Users data-icon="inline-start" />
              Usuarios
            </TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="patients">
              <UserRound data-icon="inline-start" />
              Pacientes
            </TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="doctors">
              <Stethoscope data-icon="inline-start" />
              MÃ©dicos
            </TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="specialties">
              <HeartPulse data-icon="inline-start" />
              Especialidades
            </TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="branches">
              <MapPin data-icon="inline-start" />
              Sedes
            </TabsTrigger>
          </TabsList>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="relative flex-1 sm:w-64">
              <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 left-3 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
              <Input
                value={query}
                onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Buscar..."
                className="pl-9"
                aria-label="Buscar registros"
              />
            </div>
            <Button onClick={openCreate}>
              <Plus data-icon="inline-start" />
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">{newRecordLabels[tab]}</span>
              <span className="sm:hidden">Nuevo</span>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <TabsContent value="users">
          <UsersTable
            users={filteredUsers}
            onEdit={openEdit}
            onDelete={(user) => setToDelete({ id: user.id, name: user.name })}
          />
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="patients">
          <PatientsTable
            patients={filteredPatients}
            onEdit={openEdit}
            onDelete={(patient) =>
              setToDelete({
                id: patient.id,
                name: `${patient.firstName} ${patient.lastName}`,
              })
            }
          />
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="doctors">
          <MedicsTable
            medics={filteredMedics}
            onEdit={openEdit}
            onDelete={(medic) =>
              setToDelete({
                id: medic.id,
                name: `${medic.firstName} ${medic.lastName}`,
              })
            }
          />
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="specialties">
          <SpecialtiesGrid
            specialties={filteredSpecialties}
            onEdit={openEdit}
            onDelete={(specialty) =>
              setToDelete({ id: specialty.id, name: specialty.name })
            }
          />
        </TabsContent>
        <TabsContent value="branches">
          <BranchesTable
            branches={filteredBranches}
            onEdit={openEdit}
            onDelete={(branch) =>
              setToDelete({ id: branch.id, name: branch.name })
            }
          />
        </TabsContent>
      </Tabs>

      <RecordFormDialog
        open={dialogOpen}
        onOpenChange={(open) => {
          setDialogOpen(open)
          if (!open) {
            setEditing(null)
            setFormError(null)
          }
        }}
        entity={tab}
        record={editing}
        specialties={initialSpecialties}
        branches={initialBranches}
        submitting={pending}
        error={formError}
        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
      />

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={!!toDelete}
        onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setToDelete(null)}
        title="Â¿Eliminar registro?"
        description={
          <>
            EstÃ¡s por eliminar a{" "}
            <span className="font-medium text-foreground">
              {toDelete?.name}
            </span>
            . Esta acciÃ³n no se puede deshacer.
          </>
        }
        cancelLabel="Cancelar"
        confirmLabel="Eliminar"
        onConfirm={confirmDelete}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

===== .\src\components\admin\BranchesTable.tsx =====

"use client"

import { MapPin } from "lucide-react"
import type { BranchDto } from "@/models/Branch"
import RowActions from "@/components/admin/RowActions"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card"
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table"

export default function BranchesTable({
  branches,
  onEdit,
  onDelete,
}: {
  branches: BranchDto[]
  onEdit: (branch: BranchDto) => void
  onDelete: (branch: BranchDto) => void
}) {
  return (
    <Card className="overflow-hidden py-0">
      <Table>
        <TableHeader>
          <TableRow className="bg-muted/50 hover:bg-muted/50">
            <TableHead>Sede</TableHead>
            <TableHead className="hidden lg:table-cell">TelÃ©fono</TableHead>
            <TableHead>Profesionales</TableHead>
            <TableHead className="hidden sm:table-cell">Estado</TableHead>
            <TableHead className="text-right">Acciones</TableHead>
          </TableRow>
        </TableHeader>
        <TableBody>
          {branches.map((branch) => (
            <TableRow key={branch.id}>
              <TableCell>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <span className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-accent text-accent-foreground">
                    <MapPin className="size-4" />
                  </span>
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="font-medium">{branch.name}</p>
                    <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {branch.address}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell className="hidden lg:table-cell text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                {branch.phone || "â€”"}
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell>
                <Badge
                  variant={branch.doctorCount ? "secondary" : "outline"}
                  className="font-normal"
                >
                  {branch.doctorCount}{" "}
                  {branch.doctorCount === 1 ? "mÃ©dico" : "mÃ©dicos"}
                </Badge>
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell className="hidden sm:table-cell">
                <Badge
                  variant={branch.active ? "secondary" : "outline"}
                  className="font-normal"
                >
                  {branch.active ? "Activa" : "Inactiva"}
                </Badge>
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell>
                <RowActions
                  onEdit={() => onEdit(branch)}
                  onDelete={() => onDelete(branch)}
                  deleteDisabled={branch.doctorCount > 0}
                  deleteHint={
                    branch.doctorCount > 0
                      ? "Tiene mÃ©dicos asignados: reasignalos o desactivÃ¡ la sede"
                      : "Eliminar"
                  }
                />
              </TableCell>
            </TableRow>
          ))}
          {!branches.length && (
            <TableRow className="hover:bg-transparent">
              <TableCell
                colSpan={5}
                className="py-10 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground"
              >
                No se encontraron registros.
              </TableCell>
            </TableRow>
          )}
        </TableBody>
      </Table>
    </Card>
  )
}

===== .\src\components\admin\MedicsTable.tsx =====

"use client"

import { useState } from "react"

import { getInitials } from "@/lib/format/initials"
import type { MedicDto } from "@/models/Medic"
import RowActions from "@/components/admin/RowActions"
import DoctorReviewsDialog from "@/components/DoctorReviewsDialog"
import RatingBadge from "@/components/RatingBadge"
import { Avatar, AvatarFallback, AvatarImage } from "@/components/ui/avatar"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card"
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table"

export default function MedicsTable({
  medics,
  onEdit,
  onDelete,
}: {
  medics: MedicDto[]
  onEdit: (medic: MedicDto) => void
  onDelete: (medic: MedicDto) => void
}) {
  const [reviewsFor, setReviewsFor] = useState<MedicDto | null>(null)

  return (
    <Card className="overflow-hidden py-0">
      <Table>
        <TableHeader>
          <TableRow className="bg-muted/50 hover:bg-muted/50">
            <TableHead>Profesional</TableHead>
            <TableHead>Especialidad</TableHead>
            <TableHead className="hidden md:table-cell">Sede</TableHead>
            <TableHead>CalificaciÃ³n</TableHead>
            <TableHead className="hidden lg:table-cell">Contacto</TableHead>
            <TableHead className="hidden xl:table-cell">MatrÃ­cula</TableHead>
            <TableHead className="text-right">Acciones</TableHead>
          </TableRow>
        </TableHeader>
        <TableBody>
          {medics.map((medic) => (
            <TableRow key={medic.id}>
              <TableCell>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <Avatar className="size-9">
                    <AvatarImage src={medic.avatar} alt="" />
                    <AvatarFallback className="bg-accent text-xs font-semibold text-accent-foreground">
                      {getInitials(medic.displayName)}
                    </AvatarFallback>
                  </Avatar>
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="font-medium">
                      {medic.firstName} {medic.lastName}
                    </p>
                    <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground lg:hidden">
                      {medic.email}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell>
                <Badge
                  variant={medic.specialtyName ? "secondary" : "outline"}
                  className="font-normal"
                >
                  {medic.specialtyName || "Sin asignar"}
                </Badge>
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell className="hidden md:table-cell">
                {medic.branchName ? (
                  <>
                    <p className="text-sm">{medic.branchName}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {medic.office
                        ? `Consultorio ${medic.office}`
                        : "Sin consultorio"}
                    </p>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    Sin asignar
                  </span>
                )}
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell>
                {/* Abre la ficha con las opiniones. El promedio de la fila
                    sale de la tabla `reviews`, no de un nÃºmero cargado a
                    mano: por eso un profesional nuevo no aparece con 5.0. */}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setReviewsFor(medic)}
                  className="rounded-md px-1.5 py-1 text-sm transition-colors hover:bg-accent focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:outline-none"
                  aria-label={`Ver las reseÃ±as de ${medic.displayName}`}
                >
                  <RatingBadge
                    rating={medic.rating}
                    reviewCount={medic.reviewCount}
                  />
                </button>
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell className="hidden lg:table-cell">
                <p className="text-sm">{medic.email}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{medic.phone}</p>
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell className="hidden font-mono text-sm text-muted-foreground xl:table-cell">
                {medic.license}
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell>
                <RowActions
                  onEdit={() => onEdit(medic)}
                  onDelete={() => onDelete(medic)}
                />
              </TableCell>
            </TableRow>
          ))}
          {!medics.length && (
            <TableRow className="hover:bg-transparent">
              <TableCell
                colSpan={7}
                className="py-10 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground"
              >
                No se encontraron registros.
              </TableCell>
            </TableRow>
          )}
        </TableBody>
      </Table>

      <DoctorReviewsDialog
        medic={reviewsFor}
        open={reviewsFor !== null}
        onOpenChange={(open) => {
          if (!open) setReviewsFor(null)
        }}
      />
    </Card>
  )
}

              </TableCell>

===== .\src\components\admin\RecordFormDialog.tsx =====

"use client"

import type React from "react"
import { useState } from "react"
import type { BranchDto } from "@/models/Branch"
import type { MedicDto } from "@/models/Medic"
import type { PatientDto } from "@/models/Patient"
import type { SpecialtyDto } from "@/models/Specialty"
import type { Role, UserDto } from "@/models/User"
import {
  DEFAULT_SPECIALTY_ICON,
  resolveSpecialtyIcon,
  specialtyIconOptions,
} from "@/lib/specialtyIcons"
import { calculateAge, todayISODate } from "@/lib/format/date"
import { DEFAULT_SCHEDULE, generateTimeSlots } from "@/lib/bookingSlots"
import { DNI_MAX_DIGITS, formatDni, normalizeDni } from "@/lib/format/dni"
import { MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH } from "@/validations/UserValidation"
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogClose,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogBody,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog"
import {
  Field,
  FieldDescription,
  FieldGroup,
  FieldLabel,
} from "@/components/ui/field"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectGroup,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select"
import { Spinner } from "@/components/ui/spinner"
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea"

export type EditableEntity =
  | "users"
  | "patients"
  | "doctors"
  | "specialties"
  | "branches"

export type AdminRecord =
  | PatientDto
  | MedicDto
  | SpecialtyDto
  | UserDto
  | BranchDto

export const editableEntityLabels: Record<EditableEntity, string> = {
  users: "usuario",
  patients: "paciente",
  doctors: "mÃ©dico",
  specialties: "especialidad",
  branches: "sede",
}

/** Spelled out per entity because the article is gendered in Spanish. */
export const newRecordLabels: Record<EditableEntity, string> = {
  users: "Nuevo usuario",
  patients: "Nuevo paciente",
  doctors: "Nuevo mÃ©dico",
  specialties: "Nueva especialidad",
  branches: "Nueva sede",
}

/** Valor del select de sede cuando el profesional todavÃ­a no tiene una. */
const NO_BRANCH = "__none__"

/**
 * One flat bag of fields shared by the entities. Each form renders only its own
 * subset, and the caller reads only the keys it sent.
 */
export interface RecordFormValues {
  firstName: string
  lastName: string
  email: string
  phone: string
  dni: string
  birthDate: string
  specialtyId: string
  branchId: string
  office: string
  license: string
  yearsExperience: number
  workDays: number[]
  startTime: string
  endTime: string
  breakStart: string
  breakEnd: string
  slotMinutes: number
  name: string
  description: string
  icon: string
  address: string
  active: boolean
  role: Role
  password: string
}

const emptyValues: RecordFormValues = {
  firstName: "",
  lastName: "",
  email: "",
  phone: "",
  dni: "",
  birthDate: "",
  specialtyId: "",
  branchId: "",
  office: "",
  license: "",
  yearsExperience: 1,
  workDays: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5],
  startTime: DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.startTime,
  endTime: DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.endTime,
  breakStart: DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.breakStart,
  breakEnd: DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.breakEnd,
  slotMinutes: DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.slotMinutes,
  name: "",
  description: "",
  icon: DEFAULT_SPECIALTY_ICON,
  address: "",
  active: true,
  role: "patient",
  password: "",
}

/** Ãndice = getDay() de JavaScript: 0 es domingo. */
const weekdayLabels = ["D", "L", "M", "M", "J", "V", "S"]
const weekdayNames = [
  "domingo",
  "lunes",
  "martes",
  "miÃ©rcoles",
  "jueves",
  "viernes",
  "sÃ¡bado",
]

const roleLabels: Record<Role, string> = {
  patient: "Paciente",
  doctor: "MÃ©dico",
  admin: "Administrador",
}

function initialValues(
  entity: EditableEntity,
  record: AdminRecord | null
): RecordFormValues {
  if (!record) return emptyValues

  if (entity === "patients") {
    const patient = record as PatientDto
    return {
      ...emptyValues,
      firstName: patient.firstName,
      lastName: patient.lastName,
      email: patient.email,
      phone: patient.phone,
      dni: formatDni(patient.dni),
      birthDate: patient.birthDate,
    }
  }

  if (entity === "doctors") {
    const medic = record as MedicDto
    return {
      ...emptyValues,
      firstName: medic.firstName,
      lastName: medic.lastName,
      email: medic.email,
      phone: medic.phone,
      specialtyId: medic.specialtyId,
      branchId: medic.branchId,
      office: medic.office,
      license: medic.license,
      yearsExperience: medic.yearsExperience,
      workDays: medic.workDays,
      startTime: medic.startTime,
      endTime: medic.endTime,
      breakStart: medic.breakStart,
      breakEnd: medic.breakEnd,
      slotMinutes: medic.slotMinutes,
    }
  }

  if (entity === "specialties") {
    const specialty = record as SpecialtyDto
    return {
      ...emptyValues,
      name: specialty.name,
      description: specialty.description,
      icon: specialty.icon || DEFAULT_SPECIALTY_ICON,
    }
  }

  if (entity === "branches") {
    const branch = record as BranchDto
    return {
      ...emptyValues,
      name: branch.name,
      address: branch.address,
      phone: branch.phone,
      active: branch.active,
    }
  }

  const user = record as UserDto
  return {
    ...emptyValues,
    name: user.name,
    email: user.email,
    role: user.role,
  }
}

export default function RecordFormDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  entity,
  record,
  specialties,
  branches,
  submitting,
  error,
  onSubmit,
}: {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  entity: EditableEntity
  record: AdminRecord | null
  specialties: SpecialtyDto[]
  branches: BranchDto[]
  submitting: boolean
  error: string | null
  onSubmit: (values: RecordFormValues) => void
}) {
  const label = editableEntityLabels[entity]

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>
            {record ? "Editar" : "Nuevo"} {label}
          </DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            {record
              ? "ModificÃ¡ los datos y guardÃ¡ los cambios."
              : `CompletÃ¡ el formulario para dar de alta un ${label}.`}
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        {/* Remounting on entity/record change resets the fields without an
            effect that fights the user's typing. */}
        <RecordForm
          key={`${entity}:${record ? record.id : "new"}`}
          entity={entity}
          record={record}
          specialties={specialties}
          branches={branches}
          submitting={submitting}
          error={error}
          onSubmit={onSubmit}
        />
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}

function RecordForm({
  entity,
  record,
  specialties,
  branches,
  submitting,
  error,
  onSubmit,
}: {
  entity: EditableEntity
  record: AdminRecord | null
  specialties: SpecialtyDto[]
  branches: BranchDto[]
  submitting: boolean
  error: string | null
  onSubmit: (values: RecordFormValues) => void
}) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState(() => initialValues(entity, record))

  function set<K extends keyof RecordFormValues>(
    key: K,
    value: RecordFormValues[K]
  ) {
    setValues((previous) => ({ ...previous, [key]: value }))
  }

  function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault()
    onSubmit(values)
  }

  const isPerson = entity === "patients" || entity === "doctors"

  return (
    // `min-h-0` para que el cuerpo pueda achicarse y aparezca el scroll.
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">
      <DialogBody>
        <FieldGroup className="py-4">
          {isPerson && (
            <>
              <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <Field>
                  <FieldLabel htmlFor="firstName">Nombre</FieldLabel>
                  <Input
                    id="firstName"
                    required
                    value={values.firstName}
                    onChange={(event) => set("firstName", event.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Ej. Juan"
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field>
                  <FieldLabel htmlFor="lastName">Apellido</FieldLabel>
                  <Input
                    id="lastName"
                    required
                    value={values.lastName}
                    onChange={(event) => set("lastName", event.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Ej. PÃ©rez"
                  />
                </Field>
              </div>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="email">Email</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  id="email"
                  type="email"
                  required
                  value={values.email}
                  onChange={(event) => set("email", event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="correo@ejemplo.com"
                />
              </Field>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="phone">TelÃ©fono</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  id="phone"
                  value={values.phone}
                  onChange={(event) => set("phone", event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="+54 11 0000-0000"
                />
              </Field>
            </>
          )}

          {entity === "patients" && (
            <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="dni">DNI</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  id="dni"
                  required
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  value={values.dni}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    // Se puntea mientras se escribe, pero se guarda sin puntos.
                    set(
                      "dni",
                      formatDni(
                        normalizeDni(event.target.value).slice(0, DNI_MAX_DIGITS)
                      )
                    )
                  }
                  placeholder="00.000.000"
                />
                <FieldDescription>
                  Entre 7 y {DNI_MAX_DIGITS} dÃ­gitos, sin letras.
                </FieldDescription>
              </Field>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="birthDate">Fecha de nacimiento</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  id="birthDate"
                  type="date"
                  required
                  max={todayISODate()}
                  value={values.birthDate}
                  onChange={(event) => set("birthDate", event.target.value)}
                />
                <FieldDescription>
                  {values.birthDate
                    ? `Edad: ${calculateAge(values.birthDate)} aÃ±os`
                    : "La edad se calcula a partir de esta fecha."}
                </FieldDescription>
              </Field>
            </div>
          )}

          {entity === "doctors" && (
            <>
              <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <Field>
                  <FieldLabel htmlFor="specialtyId">Especialidad</FieldLabel>
                  {/* Se elige del catÃ¡logo, nunca se tipea: un nombre libre que
                      no coincidÃ­a dejaba al mÃ©dico sin especialidad asociada. */}
                  <Select
                    value={values.specialtyId}
                    onValueChange={(value) => set("specialtyId", value ?? "")}
                  >
                    <SelectTrigger id="specialtyId" className="w-full">
                      <SelectValue>
                        {specialties.find(
                          (specialty) => specialty.id === values.specialtyId
                        )?.name ?? "Sin asignar"}
                      </SelectValue>
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      <SelectGroup>
                        {specialties.map((specialty) => (
                          <SelectItem key={specialty.id} value={specialty.id}>
                            {specialty.name}
                          </SelectItem>
                        ))}
                      </SelectGroup>
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </Field>
                <Field>
                  <FieldLabel htmlFor="license">MatrÃ­cula</FieldLabel>
                  <Input
                    id="license"
                    value={values.license}
                    onChange={(event) => set("license", event.target.value)}
                    placeholder="MN 00000"
                  />
                </Field>
              </div>

              <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <Field>
                  <FieldLabel htmlFor="branchId">Sede</FieldLabel>
                  <Select
                    value={values.branchId || NO_BRANCH}
                    onValueChange={(value) =>
                      set("branchId", value === NO_BRANCH ? "" : (value ?? ""))
                    }
                  >
                    <SelectTrigger id="branchId" className="w-full">
                      <SelectValue>
                        {branches.find((branch) => branch.id === values.branchId)
                          ?.name ?? "Sin asignar"}
                      </SelectValue>
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      <SelectGroup>
                        <SelectItem value={NO_BRANCH}>Sin asignar</SelectItem>
                        {branches.map((branch) => (
                          <SelectItem key={branch.id} value={branch.id}>
                            {branch.name}
                            {!branch.active && " (inactiva)"}
                          </SelectItem>
                        ))}
                      </SelectGroup>
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </Field>
                <Field>
                  <FieldLabel htmlFor="yearsExperience">
                    AÃ±os de experiencia
                  </FieldLabel>
                  <Input
                    id="yearsExperience"
                    type="number"
                    min={0}
                    max={70}
                    value={values.yearsExperience}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      set("yearsExperience", Number(event.target.value))
                    }
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field>
                  <FieldLabel htmlFor="office">Consultorio</FieldLabel>
                  <Input
                    id="office"
                    value={values.office}
                    onChange={(event) => set("office", event.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Ej. 101"
                    disabled={!values.branchId}
                  />
                  <FieldDescription>
                    {values.branchId
                      ? "El nÃºmero de consultorio dentro de esa sede."
                      : "ElegÃ­ una sede para poder asignar consultorio."}
                  </FieldDescription>
                </Field>
              </div>

              <Field>
                <FieldLabel>DÃ­as de atenciÃ³n</FieldLabel>
                {/* Ya no es decorativo: el calendario de reserva bloquea los dÃ­as
                    que no estÃ©n marcados acÃ¡. */}
                <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                  {weekdayLabels.map((label, day) => {
                    const active = values.workDays.includes(day)
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={day}
                        type="button"
                        aria-pressed={active}
                        aria-label={weekdayNames[day]}
                        onClick={() =>
                          set(
                            "workDays",
                            active
                              ? values.workDays.filter((d) => d !== day)
                              : [...values.workDays, day].sort()
                          )
                        }
                        className={
                          active
                            ? "size-9 rounded-lg border border-primary bg-primary text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground"
                            : "size-9 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary/40"
                        }
                      >
                        {label}
                      </button>
                    )
                  })}
                </div>
                <FieldDescription>
                  {values.workDays.length
                    ? "Los pacientes solo van a poder reservar en estos dÃ­as."
                    : "Sin dÃ­as marcados nadie puede reservar con este profesional."}
                </FieldDescription>
              </Field>

              <Field>
                <FieldLabel>Jornada de atenciÃ³n</FieldLabel>
                <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                    <span className="w-16 text-muted-foreground">Abre</span>
                    <Input
                      type="time"
                      value={values.startTime}
                      onChange={(event) => set("startTime", event.target.value)}
                    />
                  </label>
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                    <span className="w-16 text-muted-foreground">Cierra</span>
                    <Input
                      type="time"
                      value={values.endTime}
                      onChange={(event) => set("endTime", event.target.value)}
                    />
                  </label>
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                    <span className="w-16 text-muted-foreground">Corte</span>
                    <Input
                      type="time"
                      value={values.breakStart}
                      onChange={(event) => set("breakStart", event.target.value)}
                    />
                  </label>
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                    <span className="w-16 text-muted-foreground">Retoma</span>
                    <Input
                      type="time"
                      value={values.breakEnd}
                      onChange={(event) => set("breakEnd", event.target.value)}
                    />
                  </label>
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                    <span className="w-16 text-muted-foreground">Consulta</span>
                    <Input
                      type="number"
                      min={5}
                      max={240}
                      step={5}
                      value={values.slotMinutes}
                      onChange={(event) =>
                        set("slotMinutes", Number(event.target.value))
                      }
                    />
                    <span className="text-muted-foreground">min</span>
                  </label>
                </div>
                <FieldDescription>
                  {/* La cuenta se muestra en vivo: es la forma mÃ¡s directa de ver
                      que una jornada mal cargada deja al profesional sin turnos. */}
                  {(() => {
                    const total = generateTimeSlots(values).length
                    if (total === 0) {
                      return "Con esta jornada no entra ningÃºn turno: revisÃ¡ los horarios."
                    }
                    return `${total} ${total === 1 ? "turno" : "turnos"} por dÃ­a de atenciÃ³n. DejÃ¡ el corte vacÃ­o para jornada continua.`
                  })()}
                </FieldDescription>
              </Field>
            </>
          )}

          {entity === "specialties" && (
            <>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="name">Nombre</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  id="name"
                  required
                  value={values.name}
                  onChange={(event) => set("name", event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Ej. CardiologÃ­a"
                />
              </Field>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="description">DescripciÃ³n</FieldLabel>
                <Textarea
                  id="description"
                  required
                  rows={3}
                  value={values.description}
                  onChange={(event) => set("description", event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="AtenciÃ³n integral del corazÃ³n y el sistema circulatorio."
                />
              </Field>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="icon">Ãcono</FieldLabel>
                <Select
                  value={values.icon}
                  onValueChange={(value) =>
                    set("icon", value ?? DEFAULT_SPECIALTY_ICON)
                  }
                >
                  <SelectTrigger id="icon" className="w-full">
                    <SelectValue>{values.icon}</SelectValue>
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    <SelectGroup>
                      {specialtyIconOptions.map((option) => {
                        const Icon = resolveSpecialtyIcon(option)
                        return (
                          <SelectItem key={option} value={option}>
                            <Icon className="size-4" />
                            {option}
                          </SelectItem>
                        )
                      })}
                    </SelectGroup>
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </Field>
            </>
          )}

          {entity === "branches" && (
            <>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="name">Nombre</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  id="name"
                  required
                  value={values.name}
                  onChange={(event) => set("name", event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Ej. Sede Central"
                />
              </Field>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="address">DirecciÃ³n</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  id="address"
                  required
                  value={values.address}
                  onChange={(event) => set("address", event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Av. Corrientes 1234, CABA"
                />
              </Field>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="phone">TelÃ©fono</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  id="phone"
                  value={values.phone}
                  onChange={(event) => set("phone", event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="+54 11 0000-0000"
                />
              </Field>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="active">Estado</FieldLabel>
                <Select
                  value={values.active ? "active" : "inactive"}
                  onValueChange={(value) => set("active", value === "active")}
                >
                  <SelectTrigger id="active" className="w-full">
                    <SelectValue>
                      {values.active ? "Activa" : "Inactiva"}
                    </SelectValue>
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    <SelectGroup>
                      <SelectItem value="active">Activa</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="inactive">Inactiva</SelectItem>
                    </SelectGroup>
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
                <FieldDescription>
                  Una sede inactiva deja de ofrecerse al reservar, pero conserva
                  los turnos que ya se atendieron ahÃ­.
                </FieldDescription>
              </Field>
            </>
          )}

          {entity === "users" && (
            <>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="name">Nombre completo</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  id="name"
                  required
                  value={values.name}
                  onChange={(event) => set("name", event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Ej. Juan PÃ©rez"
                />
              </Field>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="email">Email</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  id="email"
                  type="email"
                  required
                  value={values.email}
                  onChange={(event) => set("email", event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="correo@ejemplo.com"
                />
              </Field>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="role">Rol</FieldLabel>
                <Select
                  value={values.role}
                  onValueChange={(value) =>
                    set("role", (value as Role) ?? "patient")
                  }
                >
                  <SelectTrigger id="role" className="w-full">
                    <SelectValue>{roleLabels[values.role]}</SelectValue>
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    <SelectGroup>
                      {(Object.keys(roleLabels) as Role[]).map((value) => (
                        <SelectItem key={value} value={value}>
                          {roleLabels[value]}
                        </SelectItem>
                      ))}
                    </SelectGroup>
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </Field>
              {!record && (
                <Field>
                  <FieldLabel htmlFor="password">ContraseÃ±a inicial</FieldLabel>
                  <Input
                    id="password"
                    type="password"
                    required
                    minLength={MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH}
                    value={values.password}
                    onChange={(event) => set("password", event.target.value)}
                    placeholder="â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢"
                  />
                </Field>
              )}
            </>
          )}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive">
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}
        </FieldGroup>
      </DialogBody>

      <DialogFooter>
        <DialogClose
          render={
            <Button type="button" variant="outline" disabled={submitting}>
              Cancelar
            </Button>
          }
        />
        <Button type="submit" disabled={submitting}>
          {submitting && <Spinner data-icon="inline-start" />}
          {record ? "Guardar cambios" : "Crear registro"}
        </Button>
      </DialogFooter>
    </form>
  )
}

===== .\src\components\admin\RowActions.tsx =====

"use client"

import { Pencil, Trash2 } from "lucide-react"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import {
  Tooltip,
  TooltipContent,
  TooltipTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/tooltip"

export default function RowActions({
  onEdit,
  onDelete,
  deleteDisabled = false,
  deleteHint = "Eliminar",
}: {
  onEdit: () => void
  onDelete: () => void
  /** Set for rows the rules forbid removing, such as your own account. */
  deleteDisabled?: boolean
  deleteHint?: string
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-1">
      <Tooltip>
        <TooltipTrigger
          render={
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="icon"
              onClick={onEdit}
              aria-label="Editar"
            />
          }
        >
          <Pencil />
        </TooltipTrigger>
        <TooltipContent>Editar</TooltipContent>
      </Tooltip>
      <Tooltip>
        <TooltipTrigger
          render={
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="icon"
              onClick={onDelete}
              disabled={deleteDisabled}
              className="text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
              aria-label={deleteHint}
            />
          }
        >
          <Trash2 />
        </TooltipTrigger>
        <TooltipContent>{deleteHint}</TooltipContent>
      </Tooltip>
    </div>
  )
}

"use client"

import { Pencil, Trash2 } from "lucide-react"
import { resolveSpecialtyIcon } from "@/lib/specialtyIcons"
import type { SpecialtyDto } from "@/models/Specialty"
import Reveal, { staggerDelay } from "@/components/Reveal"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card"
import {
  Tooltip,
  TooltipContent,
  TooltipTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/tooltip"

export default function SpecialtiesGrid({
  specialties,
  onEdit,
  onDelete,
}: {
  specialties: SpecialtyDto[]
  onEdit: (specialty: SpecialtyDto) => void
  onDelete: (specialty: SpecialtyDto) => void
}) {
  if (!specialties.length) {
    return (
      <p className="py-10 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        No se encontraron especialidades.
      </p>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
      {specialties.map((specialty, index) => {
        const Icon = resolveSpecialtyIcon(specialty.icon)
        return (
          <Reveal key={specialty.id} delay={staggerDelay(index, 50)} className="h-full">
            <Card className="lift-on-hover h-full hover:shadow-md">
              <CardHeader>
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                  <span className="flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-accent text-accent-foreground">
                    <Icon className="size-5" />
                  </span>
                  <Badge variant="secondary" className="font-normal">
                    {specialty.activeDoctors}{" "}
                    {specialty.activeDoctors === 1 ? "mÃ©dico" : "mÃ©dicos"}
                  </Badge>
                </div>
                <CardTitle className="mt-2">{specialty.name}</CardTitle>
                <CardDescription>{specialty.description}</CardDescription>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent className="mt-auto flex gap-2">
                <Button
                  variant="outline"
                  size="sm"
                  className="flex-1"
                  onClick={() => onEdit(specialty)}
                >
                  <Pencil data-icon="inline-start" />
                  Editar
                </Button>
                <Tooltip>
                  <TooltipTrigger
                    render={
                      <Button
                        variant="outline"
                        size="icon"
                        className="text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
                        aria-label={`Eliminar ${specialty.name}`}
                        onClick={() => onDelete(specialty)}
                      />
                    }
                  >
                    <Trash2 />
                  </TooltipTrigger>
                  <TooltipContent>Eliminar especialidad</TooltipContent>
                </Tooltip>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </Reveal>
        )
      })}
    </div>
  )
}

===== .\src\components\auth\AuthForm.tsx =====

"use client"

import type React from "react"
import { useState } from "react"
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation"
import { ShieldCheck, Stethoscope, User } from "lucide-react"
import { authClient } from "@/lib/auth/authClient"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import type { Role } from "@/models/User"
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import {
  Field,
  FieldDescription,
  FieldGroup,
  FieldLabel,
} from "@/components/ui/field"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Spinner } from "@/components/ui/spinner"
import { ToggleGroup, ToggleGroupItem } from "@/components/ui/toggle-group"

type Mode = "sign-in" | "sign-up"

/**
 * Aplica el rol elegido al crear la cuenta.
 *
 * Antes el resultado de este pedido se descartaba. Si fallaba â€”tÃ­picamente
 * porque la cookie de sesiÃ³n todavÃ­a no estaba escrita cuando saliÃ³ el
 * fetchâ€”, la cuenta quedaba con el rol "patient" por defecto de la columna
 * pero sin su ficha asociada, y el problema reciÃ©n aparecÃ­a al intentar
 * reservar un turno. Se reintenta una vez para cubrir esa carrera y, si
 * igual falla, se avisa en lugar de seguir como si nada.
 */
async function assignRole(role: Role): Promise<void> {
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
    if (attempt > 0) {
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 400))
    }

    try {
      const response = await fetch("/api/set-role", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ role }),
      })
      if (response.ok) return
    } catch {
      // Se reintenta abajo.
    }
  }

  throw new Error(
    "La cuenta se creÃ³, pero no se pudo asignar el tipo de cuenta. " +
      "IniciÃ¡ sesiÃ³n y pedile a un administrador que lo configure."
  )
}

function RoleOption({
  value,
  icon: Icon,
  label,
  active,
}: {
  value: string
  icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>
  label: string
  active: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <ToggleGroupItem
      value={value}
      className={cn(
        "flex h-auto flex-col gap-1.5 rounded-lg border py-3 data-[pressed]:border-primary data-[pressed]:bg-accent",
        active && "border-primary bg-accent"
      )}
    >
      <Icon className="size-5" />
      <span className="text-xs font-medium">{label}</span>
    </ToggleGroupItem>
  )
}

export default function AuthForm({ mode }: { mode: Mode }) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("")
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("")
  const [name, setName] = useState("")
  const [role, setRole] = useState<Role>("patient")
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault()
    setError(null)
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      if (mode === "sign-up") {
        const result = await authClient.signUp.email({ email, password, name })
        if (result.error) {
          throw new Error(result.error.message ?? "No se pudo crear la cuenta")
        }
        await assignRole(role)
      } else {
        const result = await authClient.signIn.email({ email, password })
        if (result.error) {
          throw new Error(result.error.message ?? "Credenciales invÃ¡lidas")
        }
      }
      router.push("/")
      router.refresh()
    } catch (submitError) {
      setError((submitError as Error).message)
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
      <FieldGroup>
        {mode === "sign-up" && (
          <Field>
            <FieldLabel htmlFor="name">Nombre completo</FieldLabel>
            <Input
              id="name"
              value={name}
              onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Ej: Juan PÃ©rez"
              required
            />
          </Field>
        )}

        <Field>
          <FieldLabel htmlFor="email">Correo electrÃ³nico</FieldLabel>
          <Input
            id="email"
            type="email"
            value={email}
            onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="tu@email.com"
            autoComplete="email"
            required
          />
        </Field>

        <Field>
          <FieldLabel htmlFor="password">ContraseÃ±a</FieldLabel>
          <Input
            id="password"
            type="password"
            value={password}
            onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢"
            autoComplete={
              mode === "sign-in" ? "current-password" : "new-password"
            }
            minLength={8}
            required
          />
          {mode === "sign-up" && (
            <FieldDescription>MÃ­nimo 8 caracteres.</FieldDescription>
          )}
        </Field>

        {mode === "sign-up" && (
          <Field>
            <FieldLabel>Tipo de cuenta</FieldLabel>
            <ToggleGroup
              value={[role]}
              onValueChange={(values) => {
                const next = values.find((value) => value !== role) ?? values[0]
                if (next) setRole(next as Role)
              }}
              className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2"
            >
              <RoleOption
                value="patient"
                icon={User}
                label="Paciente"
                active={role === "patient"}
              />
              <RoleOption
                value="doctor"
                icon={Stethoscope}
                label="MÃ©dico"
                active={role === "doctor"}
              />
              <RoleOption
                value="admin"
                icon={ShieldCheck}
                label="Admin"
                active={role === "admin"}
              />
            </ToggleGroup>
          </Field>
        )}

        {error && (
          <Alert variant="destructive">
            <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        )}

        <Button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="w-full">
          {loading && <Spinner data-icon="inline-start" />}
          {mode === "sign-in" ? "Ingresar" : "Crear cuenta"}
        </Button>
      </FieldGroup>
    </form>
  )
}

===== .\src\components\auth\DemoCredentials.tsx =====

"use client"

import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation"
import { ShieldCheck, Stethoscope, User } from "lucide-react"
import { authClient } from "@/lib/auth/authClient"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Spinner } from "@/components/ui/spinner"

const demoAccounts = [
  {
    role: "Paciente",
    email: "paciente@saludplus.com",
    password: "paciente123",
    icon: User,
  },
  {
    role: "MÃ©dico",
    email: "medico@saludplus.com",
    password: "medico123",
    icon: Stethoscope,
  },
  {
    role: "Admin",
    email: "admin@saludplus.com",
    password: "admin123",
    icon: ShieldCheck,
  },
]

export default function DemoCredentials() {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [seeding, setSeeding] = useState(true)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<string | null>(null)

  // Creates the demo accounts the first time the sign-in page is opened.
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true
    fetch("/api/seed")
      .catch(() => {})
      .finally(() => {
        if (active) setSeeding(false)
      })
    return () => {
      active = false
    }
  }, [])

  async function signInAs(email: string, password: string) {
    setPending(email)
    const { error } = await authClient.signIn.email({ email, password })
    if (error) {
      setPending(null)
      return
    }
    router.push("/")
    router.refresh()
  }

  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed bg-muted/40 p-4">
      <p className="mb-3 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
        Cuentas de prueba (un clic para ingresar)
      </p>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        {demoAccounts.map((account) => (
          <Button
            key={account.email}
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            className="h-auto justify-start gap-3 py-2"
            disabled={seeding || pending !== null}
            onClick={() => signInAs(account.email, account.password)}
          >
            {pending === account.email ? (
              <Spinner />
            ) : (
              <account.icon className="size-4 text-primary" />
            )}
            <span className="flex flex-col items-start">
              <span className="text-sm font-medium">{account.role}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                {account.email}
              </span>
            </span>
          </Button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {seeding && (
        <p className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          <Spinner className="size-3" />
          Preparando cuentas de prueba...
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

===== .\src\components\doctor\DoctorView.tsx =====

"use client"

import type React from "react"
import { useMemo, useState } from "react"
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation"
import {
  CalendarDays,
  CheckCircle2,
  ClipboardList,
  Clock,
  UserX,
  Users,
} from "lucide-react"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { useServerState } from "@/hooks/useServerState"
import { appointmentStatusConfig } from "@/lib/appointmentStatus"
import { formatLongDate, todayISODate } from "@/lib/format/date"
import { getInitials } from "@/lib/format/initials"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import type { AppointmentDto, AppointmentStatus } from "@/models/Appointment"
import type { PatientDto } from "@/models/Patient"
import {
  saveAppointmentNotes,
  setAppointmentStatus,
} from "@/routes/AppointmentRoutes"
import PatientDetailSheet from "@/components/doctor/PatientDetailSheet"
import { Avatar, AvatarFallback } from "@/components/ui/avatar"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card"

const toneStyles: Record<string, string> = {
  primary: "bg-primary/10 text-primary",
  success: "bg-success/15 text-success",
  accent: "bg-warning/15 text-warning",
  destructive: "bg-destructive/12 text-destructive",
}

const statusToastLabels: Partial<Record<AppointmentStatus, string>> = {
  completed: "Paciente marcado como atendido",
  absent: "Paciente marcado como ausente",
}

function KpiCard({
  icon,
  value,
  label,
  tone,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode
  value: number
  label: string
  tone: keyof typeof toneStyles
}) {
  return (
    <Card>
      <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-3 py-5">
        <span
          className={cn(
            "flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg",
            toneStyles[tone]
          )}
        >
          {icon}
        </span>
        <div>
          <p className="text-3xl leading-none font-semibold tracking-tight">
            {value}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{label}</p>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  )
}

export default function DoctorView({
  doctorName,
  specialtyName,
  appointments: initialAppointments,
  patients,
}: {
  doctorName: string
  specialtyName: string
  appointments: AppointmentDto[]
  patients: PatientDto[]
}) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useServerState(initialAppointments)
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<AppointmentDto | null>(null)
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("")

  const stats = useMemo(
    () => ({
      total: appointments.length,
      completed: appointments.filter(
        (appointment) => appointment.status === "completed"
      ).length,
      pending: appointments.filter(
        (appointment) => appointment.status === "scheduled"
      ).length,
      absent: appointments.filter(
        (appointment) => appointment.status === "absent"
      ).length,
    }),
    [appointments]
  )

  async function updateStatus(id: string, status: AppointmentStatus) {
    setAppointments((previous) =>
      previous.map((appointment) =>
        appointment.id === id ? { ...appointment, status } : appointment
      )
    )
    try {
      await setAppointmentStatus(id, status)
      toast.success(statusToastLabels[status] ?? "Turno actualizado")
    } catch {
      toast.error("No se pudo actualizar el turno")
    }
    router.refresh()
  }

  function openDetail(appointment: AppointmentDto) {
    setSelected(appointment)
    setNotes(appointment.notes ?? "")
  }

  async function saveNotes() {
    if (!selected) return
    const id = selected.id
    setAppointments((previous) =>
      previous.map((appointment) =>
        appointment.id === id ? { ...appointment, notes } : appointment
      )
    )
    setSelected(null)
    try {
      await saveAppointmentNotes(id, notes)
      toast.success("Observaciones guardadas")
    } catch {
      toast.error("No se pudieron guardar las observaciones")
    }
    router.refresh()
  }

  const selectedPatient = selected
    ? patients.find((patient) => patient.id === selected.patientId)
    : undefined

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          <CalendarDays className="size-4" />
          {formatLongDate(todayISODate())}
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight sm:text-3xl">
          Agenda del dÃ­a
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">
          {doctorName}
          {specialtyName ? ` Â· ${specialtyName}` : ""}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-4">
        <KpiCard
          icon={<Users className="size-5" />}
          value={stats.total}
          label="Turnos de hoy"
          tone="primary"
        />
        <KpiCard
          icon={<CheckCircle2 className="size-5" />}
          value={stats.completed}
          label="Atendidos"
          tone="success"
        />
        <KpiCard
          icon={<Clock className="size-5" />}
          value={stats.pending}
          label="Pendientes"
          tone="accent"
        />
        <KpiCard
          icon={<UserX className="size-5" />}
          value={stats.absent}
          label="Ausentes"
          tone="destructive"
        />
      </div>

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <ClipboardList className="size-5 text-primary" />
            Pacientes citados
          </CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>
            GestionÃ¡ el estado de cada consulta a lo largo del dÃ­a.
          </CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {appointments.map((appointment) => {
            const status = appointmentStatusConfig[appointment.status]
            const isPending = appointment.status === "scheduled"
            return (
              <div
                key={appointment.id}
                className={cn(
                  "flex flex-col gap-4 rounded-xl border p-4 transition-colors sm:flex-row sm:items-center",
                  isPending
                    ? "border-border bg-card"
                    : "border-transparent bg-muted/40"
                )}
              >
                <div className="flex w-16 shrink-0 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 border-border sm:border-r sm:pr-4">
                  <Clock className="size-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                  <span className="text-sm font-semibold">
                    {appointment.time}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => openDetail(appointment)}
                  className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-3 text-left"
                >
                  <Avatar className="size-11">
                    <AvatarFallback className="bg-accent font-semibold text-accent-foreground">
                      {getInitials(appointment.patientName)}
                    </AvatarFallback>
                  </Avatar>
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      <p className="font-medium">{appointment.patientName}</p>
                      <Badge
                        variant="outline"
                        className={cn("font-normal", status.className)}
                      >
                        {status.label}
                      </Badge>
                    </div>
                    <p className="truncate text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                      {appointment.reason}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </button>

                {isPending ? (
                  <div className="flex shrink-0 gap-2">
                    <Button
                      size="sm"
                      className="bg-success text-success-foreground hover:bg-success/90"
                      onClick={() => updateStatus(appointment.id, "completed")}
                    >
                      <CheckCircle2 data-icon="inline-start" />
                      Atendido
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      size="sm"
                      variant="outline"
                      className="text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
                      onClick={() => updateStatus(appointment.id, "absent")}
                    >
                      <UserX data-icon="inline-start" />
                      Ausente
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <Button
                    size="sm"
                    variant="ghost"
                    className="shrink-0 text-muted-foreground"
                    onClick={() => openDetail(appointment)}
                  >
                    Ver detalle
                  </Button>
                )}
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <PatientDetailSheet
        appointment={selected}
        patient={selectedPatient}
        notes={notes}
        onNotesChange={setNotes}
        onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setSelected(null)}
        onSave={saveNotes}
      />
    </div>
  )
}
