===== .\src\components\patient\AppointmentCard.tsx =====

"use client"

import {
  CalendarDays,
  CalendarPlus,
  Download,
  MapPin,
  Star,
  X,
} from "lucide-react"
import { appointmentStatusConfig } from "@/lib/appointmentStatus"
import { formatShortMonth, parseISODate } from "@/lib/format/date"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import type { AppointmentDto } from "@/models/Appointment"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card"
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu"
import { buildGoogleCalendarUrl } from "@/lib/calendar/googleCalendar"

export default function AppointmentCard({
  appointment,
  onCancel,
  onReschedule,
  onReview,
  reviewed = false,
}: {
  appointment: AppointmentDto
  onCancel?: () => void
  /** Solo tiene sentido en turnos todavÃ­a agendados. */
  onReschedule?: () => void
  /** Se ofrece solo en turnos atendidos que todavÃ­a no fueron calificados. */
  onReview?: () => void
  reviewed?: boolean
}) {
  const status = appointmentStatusConfig[appointment.status]
  const canCancel = appointment.status === "scheduled" && onCancel
  const canReschedule = appointment.status === "scheduled" && onReschedule
  const canReview =
    appointment.status === "completed" && onReview && !reviewed

  // La sede se guarda al reservar; los turnos viejos, de antes de que existiera
  // el concepto, no la tienen y por eso simplemente no se muestra.
  const place = [
    appointment.branchName,
    appointment.office && `Consultorio ${appointment.office}`,
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(" Â· ")

  return (
    <Card className="transition-shadow hover:shadow-md">
      <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-4 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="flex size-12 shrink-0 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-accent text-accent-foreground">
            <span className="text-base leading-none font-semibold">
              {parseISODate(appointment.date).getDate()}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] uppercase">
              {formatShortMonth(appointment.date)}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <p className="font-medium">{appointment.doctorName}</p>
              <Badge
                variant="outline"
                className={cn("font-normal", status.className)}
              >
                {status.label}
              </Badge>
            </div>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              {appointment.specialtyName} Â· {appointment.time} hs
            </p>
            {place && (
              <p className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                <MapPin className="size-3.5 shrink-0" />
                {place}
              </p>
            )}
            {appointment.reason && (
              <p className="mt-0.5 truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                {appointment.reason}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-start sm:self-center">
          {reviewed && appointment.status === "completed" && (
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              <Star className="size-3.5 fill-current text-warning" />
              Calificado
            </span>
          )}
          {canReview && (
            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={onReview}>
              <Star data-icon="inline-start" />
              Calificar
            </Button>
          )}
          {appointment.status === "scheduled" && (
            <DropdownMenu>
              <DropdownMenuTrigger
                render={
                  <Button
                    variant="ghost"
                    size="sm"
                    aria-label="Agregar el turno a mi calendario"
                  />
                }
              >
                <>
                  <CalendarPlus data-icon="inline-start" />
                  <span className="hidden sm:inline">Agendar</span>
                </>
              </DropdownMenuTrigger>
              <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="w-60">
                {/* Abre Google Calendar con el turno cargado; queda guardado
                    al confirmar, y los avisos los manda Google. */}
                <DropdownMenuItem
                  render={
                    <a
                      href={buildGoogleCalendarUrl(appointment)}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noopener noreferrer"
                    />
                  }
                >
                  <CalendarDays />
                  Google Calendar
                </DropdownMenuItem>
                {/* El .ics es un enlace y no un botÃ³n porque la descarga la
                    tiene que manejar el navegador. Sirve para Outlook, Apple
                    y el calendario del telÃ©fono. */}
                <DropdownMenuItem
                  render={
                    <a
                      href={`/api/appointments/${appointment.id}/calendar`}
                    />
                  }
                >
                  <Download />
                  Descargar (.ics)
                </DropdownMenuItem>
              </DropdownMenuContent>
            </DropdownMenu>
          )}
          {canReschedule && (
            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={onReschedule}>
              <CalendarDays data-icon="inline-start" />
              Reprogramar
            </Button>
          )}
          {canCancel && (
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="sm"
              className="text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
              onClick={onCancel}
            >
              <X data-icon="inline-start" />
              Cancelar
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  )
}

===== .\src\components\patient\BookingStepper.tsx =====

"use client"

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react"
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation"
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  ArrowRight,
  CalendarDays,
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  HeartPulse,
  MapPin,
  PartyPopper,
  Star,
  Stethoscope,
} from "lucide-react"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { generateTimeSlots } from "@/lib/bookingSlots"
import { formatLongDate, toISODate } from "@/lib/format/date"
import { getInitials } from "@/lib/format/initials"
import { resolveSpecialtyIcon } from "@/lib/specialtyIcons"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import type { BranchDto } from "@/models/Branch"
import { medicSchedule, type MedicDto } from "@/models/Medic"
import type { SpecialtyDto } from "@/models/Specialty"
import {
  createAppointment,
  getTakenSlots,
} from "@/routes/AppointmentRoutes"
import RatingBadge from "@/components/RatingBadge"
import DoctorReviewsDialog from "@/components/DoctorReviewsDialog"
import Reveal, { staggerDelay } from "@/components/Reveal"
import { Avatar, AvatarFallback, AvatarImage } from "@/components/ui/avatar"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Calendar } from "@/components/ui/calendar"
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card"
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogClose,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog"
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectGroup,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select"

const steps = [
  { id: 1, label: "Sede", icon: MapPin },
  { id: 2, label: "Especialidad", icon: Stethoscope },
  { id: 3, label: "Profesional", icon: HeartPulse },
  { id: 4, label: "Fecha y hora", icon: CalendarDays },
]

export default function BookingStepper({
  branches,
  specialties,
  medics,
  onBooked,
}: {
  branches: BranchDto[]
  specialties: SpecialtyDto[]
  medics: MedicDto[]
  onBooked?: () => void
}) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [step, setStep] = useState(1)
  const [reviewsFor, setReviewsFor] = useState<MedicDto | null>(null)
  const [branchId, setBranchId] = useState("")
  const [specialtyId, setSpecialtyId] = useState("")
  const [medic, setMedic] = useState<MedicDto | null>(null)
  const [date, setDate] = useState<Date | undefined>(undefined)
  const [time, setTime] = useState("")
  const [confirmOpen, setConfirmOpen] = useState(false)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [taken, setTaken] = useState<string[]>([])
  const [loadingSlots, setLoadingSlots] = useState(false)

  // Los horarios ocupados se piden al elegir profesional y dÃ­a, para apagarlos
  // antes de que la persona los toque.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!medic || !date) {
      setTaken([])
      return
    }

    let active = true
    setLoadingSlots(true)

    getTakenSlots(medic.id, toISODate(date))
      .then((slots) => {
        if (active) setTaken(slots)
      })
      .catch(() => {
        // Si falla, se muestran todos: la reserva sigue validando en el servidor.
        if (active) setTaken([])
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (active) setLoadingSlots(false)
      })

    return () => {
      active = false
    }
  }, [medic, date])

  /** Profesionales que efectivamente atienden en la sede elegida. */
  const branchMedics = useMemo(
    () => medics.filter((candidate) => candidate.branchId === branchId),
    [medics, branchId]
  )

  /**
   * Solo se ofrecen las especialidades que tienen a alguien atendiendo en esa
   * sede: elegir una para despuÃ©s encontrar la lista vacÃ­a es una vÃ­a muerta.
   */
  const availableSpecialties = useMemo(() => {
    const ids = new Set(branchMedics.map((candidate) => candidate.specialtyId))
    return specialties.filter((specialty) => ids.has(specialty.id))
  }, [specialties, branchMedics])

  const filteredMedics = useMemo(
    () =>
      branchMedics.filter((candidate) => candidate.specialtyId === specialtyId),
    [branchMedics, specialtyId]
  )

  const branchName = branches.find((branch) => branch.id === branchId)?.name ?? ""

  /** Los horarios salen de la jornada del profesional, no de una lista fija. */
  const timeSlots = useMemo(
    () => (medic ? generateTimeSlots(medicSchedule(medic)) : []),
    [medic]
  )

  const specialtyName =
    specialties.find((specialty) => specialty.id === specialtyId)?.name ?? ""

  function selectBranch(value: string) {
    if (value === branchId) return
    // La especialidad y el profesional elegidos pueden no existir en la sede
    // nueva, asÃ­ que se descartan en vez de arrastrar una selecciÃ³n invÃ¡lida.
    setBranchId(value)
    setSpecialtyId("")
    setMedic(null)
  }

  function reset() {
    setStep(1)
    setBranchId("")
    setSpecialtyId("")
    setMedic(null)
    setDate(undefined)
    setTime("")
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (time && taken.includes(time)) setTime("")
  }, [taken, time])

  const canContinue =
    (step === 1 && branchId) ||
    (step === 2 && specialtyId) ||
    (step === 3 && medic) ||
    (step === 4 && date && time)

  async function handleContinue() {
    if (step < 4) {
      setStep((current) => current + 1)
      return
    }
    if (!medic || !date || !time) return

    setSaving(true)
    const result = await createAppointment({
      doctorId: medic.id,
      date: toISODate(date),
      time,
    })
    setSaving(false)

    if (!result.ok) {
      toast.error(result.error ?? "No se pudo reservar el turno")
      return
    }
    router.refresh()
    setConfirmOpen(true)
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
      <ol className="flex items-center">
        {steps.map((current, index) => {
          const done = step > current.id
          const active = step === current.id
          return (
            <li
              key={current.id}
              className="flex flex-1 items-center last:flex-none"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <span
                  className={cn(
                    "flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border text-sm font-semibold transition-colors",
                    done && "border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground",
                    active &&
                      "border-primary bg-primary/10 text-primary ring-4 ring-primary/10",
                    !done &&
                      !active &&
                      "border-border bg-card text-muted-foreground"
                  )}
                >
                  {done ? <Check className="size-4" /> : current.id}
                </span>
                <span
                  className={cn(
                    "hidden text-sm font-medium sm:block",
                    active ? "text-foreground" : "text-muted-foreground"
                  )}
                >
                  {current.label}
                </span>
              </div>
              {index < steps.length - 1 && (
                <span
                  className={cn(
                    "mx-2 h-0.5 flex-1 rounded-full transition-colors sm:mx-4",
                    done ? "bg-primary" : "bg-border"
                  )}
                />
              )}
            </li>
          )
        })}
      </ol>

      {step === 1 && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold">Â¿A quÃ© sede vas a ir?</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              ElegÃ­ dÃ³nde te queda cÃ³modo atenderte. DespuÃ©s vas a ver solo los
              profesionales que trabajan ahÃ­.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            {branches.map((branch, index) => {
              const selected = branchId === branch.id
              return (
                <Reveal key={branch.id} delay={staggerDelay(index, 50)}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => selectBranch(branch.id)}
                    className={cn(
                      "lift-on-hover flex w-full items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-card p-4 text-left hover:shadow-md",
                      selected
                        ? "border-primary ring-2 ring-primary/15"
                        : "border-border hover:border-primary/40"
                    )}
                  >
                    <span className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-accent text-accent-foreground">
                      <MapPin className="size-5" />
                    </span>
                    <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <span className="block text-sm font-semibold">
                        {branch.name}
                      </span>
                      <span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        {branch.address}
                      </span>
                      <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        {branch.doctorCount}{" "}
                        {branch.doctorCount === 1
                          ? "profesional"
                          : "profesionales"}
                      </span>
                    </span>
                    {selected && (
                      <CheckCircle2 className="size-5 shrink-0 text-primary" />
                    )}
                  </button>
                </Reveal>
              )
            })}
          </div>
          {!branches.length && (
            <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              No hay sedes disponibles en este momento.
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {step === 2 && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold">
              Â¿QuÃ© especialidad necesitÃ¡s?
            </h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              ElegÃ­ el Ã¡rea de atenciÃ³n para tu consulta.
            </p>
          </div>
          <Select
            value={specialtyId}
            onValueChange={(value) => setSpecialtyId(value ?? "")}
          >
            <SelectTrigger className="w-full sm:max-w-sm">
              <SelectValue>
                {specialtyName || "SeleccionÃ¡ una especialidad"}
              </SelectValue>
            </SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              <SelectGroup>
                {availableSpecialties.map((specialty) => (
                  <SelectItem key={specialty.id} value={specialty.id}>
                    {specialty.name}
                  </SelectItem>
                ))}
              </SelectGroup>
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
            {availableSpecialties.map((specialty) => {
              const Icon = resolveSpecialtyIcon(specialty.icon)
              const selected = specialtyId === specialty.id
              return (
                <button
                  key={specialty.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSpecialtyId(specialty.id)}
                  className={cn(
                    "group flex flex-col items-start gap-2 rounded-xl border bg-card p-4 text-left transition-all hover:shadow-md",
                    selected
                      ? "border-primary ring-2 ring-primary/15"
                      : "border-border hover:border-primary/40"
                  )}
                >
                  <span
                    className={cn(
                      "flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg transition-colors",
                      selected
                        ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground"
                        : "bg-accent text-accent-foreground"
                    )}
                  >
                    <Icon className="size-5" />
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-sm font-semibold">
                    {specialty.name}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {specialty.activeDoctors} profesionales
                  </span>
                </button>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {step === 3 && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold">ElegÃ­ un profesional</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              {specialtyName} en {branchName}.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            {filteredMedics.map((candidate, index) => {
              const selected = medic?.id === candidate.id
              return (
                <Reveal key={candidate.id} delay={staggerDelay(index, 50)}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setMedic(candidate)}
                    className={cn(
                      "lift-on-hover flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-card p-3.5 text-left hover:shadow-md",
                      selected
                        ? "border-primary ring-2 ring-primary/15"
                        : "border-border hover:border-primary/40"
                    )}
                  >
                    <Avatar className="size-14">
                      <AvatarImage
                        src={candidate.avatar}
                        alt={candidate.displayName}
                      />
                      <AvatarFallback>
                        {getInitials(candidate.displayName)}
                      </AvatarFallback>
                    </Avatar>
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold">
                        {candidate.displayName}
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        {candidate.specialtyName}
                      </p>
                      <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        <RatingBadge
                          rating={candidate.rating}
                          reviewCount={candidate.reviewCount}
                          showCount={false}
                        />
                        <span aria-hidden>Â·</span>
                        <span>{candidate.yearsExperience} aÃ±os exp.</span>
                      </div>
                      {candidate.office && (
                        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          Consultorio {candidate.office}
                        </p>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    {selected && (
                      <CheckCircle2 className="size-5 shrink-0 text-primary" />
                    )}
                  </button>

                  {/* Fuera del botÃ³n de selecciÃ³n: un botÃ³n dentro de otro
                      botÃ³n es HTML invÃ¡lido, y el navegador decide solo cuÃ¡l
                      de los dos recibe el clic. */}
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setReviewsFor(candidate)}
                    className="mt-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-1.5 py-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:outline-none"
                  >
                    <Star className="size-3.5" />
                    {candidate.reviewCount > 0
                      ? `Ver ${candidate.reviewCount} ${candidate.reviewCount === 1 ? "reseÃ±a" : "reseÃ±as"}`
                      : "Ver ficha"}
                  </button>
                </Reveal>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {step === 4 && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold">ElegÃ­ fecha y horario</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              Turnos disponibles con {medic?.displayName}.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[auto_1fr]">
            <div className="flex justify-center rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-2">
              <Calendar
                mode="single"
                selected={date}
                onSelect={setDate}
                disabled={(candidate) => {
                  const today = new Date()
                  today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
                  if (candidate < today) return true
                  // Antes se bloqueaba el domingo y nada mÃ¡s, asÃ­ que se podÃ­a
                  // reservar con alguien un dÃ­a que no atiende.
                  return !medic?.workDays.includes(candidate.getDay())
                }}
                className="bg-transparent p-0"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium">
                <Clock className="size-4 text-primary" />
                {date ? formatLongDate(toISODate(date)) : "SeleccionÃ¡ un dÃ­a"}
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4">
                {timeSlots.map((slot, index) => {
                  const selected = time === slot
                  const busy = taken.includes(slot)
                  return (
                    <Reveal key={slot} delay={staggerDelay(index, 20, 240)}>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={!date || busy || loadingSlots}
                        title={busy ? "Horario ya reservado" : undefined}
                        onClick={() => setTime(slot)}
                        className={cn(
                          "w-full rounded-lg border px-2 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-all disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40",
                          selected
                            ? "border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow-sm"
                            : "border-border bg-card hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-accent/50",
                          busy && "line-through"
                        )}
                      >
                        {slot}
                      </button>
                    </Reveal>
                  )
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-border pt-4">
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          onClick={() => setStep((current) => Math.max(1, current - 1))}
          disabled={step === 1}
        >
          <ArrowLeft data-icon="inline-start" />
          AtrÃ¡s
        </Button>
        <Button onClick={handleContinue} disabled={!canContinue || saving}>
          {step === 4
            ? saving
              ? "Confirmando..."
              : "Confirmar turno"
            : "Continuar"}
          {step === 4 ? (
            <Check data-icon="inline-end" />
          ) : (
            <ArrowRight data-icon="inline-end" />
          )}
        </Button>
      </div>

      <Dialog
        open={confirmOpen}
        onOpenChange={(open) => {
          setConfirmOpen(open)
          if (!open) {
            reset()
            onBooked?.()
          }
        }}
      >
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-md">
          <DialogHeader className="items-center text-center">
            <div className="mb-2 flex size-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-success/15 text-success">
              <PartyPopper className="size-7" />
            </div>
            <DialogTitle className="text-center">
              Â¡Turno confirmado!
            </DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription className="text-center">
              Te enviamos los detalles por correo electrÃ³nico. PodÃ©s verlo en{" "}
              <span className="font-medium text-foreground">Mis turnos</span>.
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>
          <Card className="border-primary/20 bg-primary/5">
            <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-3 py-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <Avatar className="size-11">
                  <AvatarImage src={medic?.avatar} alt="" />
                  <AvatarFallback>
                    {medic ? getInitials(medic.displayName) : ""}
                  </AvatarFallback>
                </Avatar>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{medic?.displayName}</p>
                  <Badge variant="secondary" className="mt-0.5 font-normal">
                    {specialtyName}
                  </Badge>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-sm">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <CalendarDays className="size-4 text-primary" />
                  {date ? formatLongDate(toISODate(date)) : ""}
                </span>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Clock className="size-4 text-primary" />
                  {time} hs
                </span>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
          <DialogFooter>
            <DialogClose render={<Button className="w-full">Listo</Button>} />
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>

      <DoctorReviewsDialog
        medic={reviewsFor}
        open={reviewsFor !== null}
        onOpenChange={(open) => {
          if (!open) setReviewsFor(null)
        }}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

===== .\src\components\patient\PatientView.tsx =====

"use client"

import type React from "react"
import { useMemo, useState } from "react"
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation"
import {
  CalendarCheck,
  CalendarPlus,
  Clock,
  History,
  LayoutDashboard,
  MapPin,
  Stethoscope,
} from "lucide-react"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { useServerState } from "@/hooks/useServerState"
import { daysUntil, formatLongDate, formatShortDate } from "@/lib/format/date"
import { getInitials } from "@/lib/format/initials"
import type { AppointmentDto } from "@/models/Appointment"
import type { BranchDto } from "@/models/Branch"
import type { MedicDto } from "@/models/Medic"
import type { SpecialtyDto } from "@/models/Specialty"
import {
  cancelAppointment,
  rescheduleAppointment,
} from "@/routes/AppointmentRoutes"
import { saveReview } from "@/routes/ReviewRoutes"
import ConfirmDialog from "@/components/ConfirmDialog"
import CountUp from "@/components/CountUp"
import Reveal, { staggerDelay } from "@/components/Reveal"
import AppointmentCard from "@/components/patient/AppointmentCard"
import BookingStepper from "@/components/patient/BookingStepper"
import RescheduleDialog from "@/components/patient/RescheduleDialog"
import ReviewDialog from "@/components/patient/ReviewDialog"
import { Avatar, AvatarFallback } from "@/components/ui/avatar"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card"
import {
  Empty,
  EmptyDescription,
  EmptyHeader,
  EmptyMedia,
  EmptyTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/empty"
import { Separator } from "@/components/ui/separator"
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs"

function isUpcoming(appointment: AppointmentDto): boolean {
  return appointment.status === "scheduled"
}

function formatCountdown(date: string): string {
  const days = daysUntil(date)
  if (days <= 0) return "hoy"
  if (days === 1) return "1 dÃ­a"
  return `${days} dÃ­as`
}

function StatMini({
  icon,
  value,
  label,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode
  value: number
  label: string
}) {
  return (
    <Card className="lift-on-hover">
      <CardContent className="flex items-center gap-3 py-4">
        <span className="flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-accent text-accent-foreground">
          {icon}
        </span>
        <div>
          <p className="text-2xl leading-none font-semibold">
            <CountUp value={value} />
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{label}</p>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  )
}

export default function PatientView({
  patientName,
  appointments: initialAppointments,
  specialties,
  medics,
  branches,
  reviewedAppointmentIds,
}: {
  patientName: string
  appointments: AppointmentDto[]
  specialties: SpecialtyDto[]
  medics: MedicDto[]
  branches: BranchDto[]
  /** Turnos que esta persona ya calificÃ³: no se vuelve a ofrecer el botÃ³n. */
  reviewedAppointmentIds: string[]
}) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("inicio")
  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useServerState(initialAppointments)
  const [toCancel, setToCancel] = useState<AppointmentDto | null>(null)
  const [toReview, setToReview] = useState<AppointmentDto | null>(null)
  const [toReschedule, setToReschedule] = useState<AppointmentDto | null>(null)
  const [rescheduleError, setRescheduleError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [savingReschedule, setSavingReschedule] = useState(false)

  const [reviewError, setReviewError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [savingReview, setSavingReview] = useState(false)
  const reviewed = useMemo(
    () => new Set(reviewedAppointmentIds),
    [reviewedAppointmentIds]
  )

  async function submitReschedule(date: string, time: string) {
    if (!toReschedule) return

    setSavingReschedule(true)
    setRescheduleError(null)
    const result = await rescheduleAppointment(toReschedule.id, date, time)
    setSavingReschedule(false)

    if (!result.ok) {
      setRescheduleError(result.error ?? "No se pudo reprogramar el turno")
      return
    }
    setToReschedule(null)
    toast.success("Turno reprogramado")
    router.refresh()
  }

  async function submitReview(score: number, comment: string) {
    if (!toReview) return

    setSavingReview(true)
    setReviewError(null)
    const result = await saveReview({
      appointmentId: toReview.id,
      score,
      comment: comment || undefined,
    })
    setSavingReview(false)

    if (!result.ok) {
      setReviewError(result.error)
      return
    }
    setToReview(null)
    toast.success("Â¡Gracias! Tu calificaciÃ³n fue registrada.")
    router.refresh()
  }

  const upcoming = useMemo(
    () =>
      appointments
        .filter(isUpcoming)
        .sort((left, right) => left.date.localeCompare(right.date)),
    [appointments]
  )
  const past = useMemo(
    () =>
      appointments
        .filter((appointment) => !isUpcoming(appointment))
        .sort((left, right) => right.date.localeCompare(left.date)),
    [appointments]
  )
  const nextAppointment = upcoming[0]

  async function confirmCancel() {
    if (!toCancel) return
    const target = toCancel
    setAppointments((previous) =>
      previous.map((appointment) =>
        appointment.id === target.id
          ? { ...appointment, status: "cancelled" }
          : appointment
      )
    )
    setToCancel(null)
    try {
      await cancelAppointment(target.id)
      toast.success("Turno cancelado", {
        description: `${target.specialtyName} Â· ${formatShortDate(target.date)}`,
      })
    } catch {
      toast.error("No se pudo cancelar el turno")
    }
    router.refresh()
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-balance sm:text-3xl">
          Hola, {patientName}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">
          GestionÃ¡ tus turnos mÃ©dicos de forma simple y rÃ¡pida.
        </p>
      </div>

      <Tabs value={tab} onValueChange={setTab} className="gap-6">
        <TabsList className="w-full max-w-md">
          <TabsTrigger value="inicio">
            <LayoutDashboard data-icon="inline-start" />
            Inicio
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="reservar">
            <CalendarPlus data-icon="inline-start" />
            Reservar
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="turnos">
            <History data-icon="inline-start" />
            Mis turnos
          </TabsTrigger>
        </TabsList>

        <TabsContent value="inicio" className="flex flex-col gap-6">
          <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3">
            <Card className="overflow-hidden border-primary/20 lg:col-span-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-primary/10 px-6 py-3">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary">
                  <CalendarCheck className="size-4" />
                  PrÃ³ximo turno
                </span>
                {nextAppointment && (
                  <Badge className="bg-primary text-primary-foreground">
                    En {formatCountdown(nextAppointment.date)}
                  </Badge>
                )}
              </div>
              <CardContent className="pt-5">
                {nextAppointment ? (
                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                      <Avatar className="size-14">
                        <AvatarFallback className="bg-accent font-semibold text-accent-foreground">
                          {getInitials(nextAppointment.doctorName)}
                        </AvatarFallback>
                      </Avatar>
                      <div>
                        <p className="font-semibold">
                          {nextAppointment.doctorName}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                          {nextAppointment.specialtyName}
                        </p>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                          {nextAppointment.reason}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-xl bg-muted/60 p-4 text-sm sm:text-right">
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-2 font-medium sm:justify-end">
                        <Clock className="size-4 text-primary" />
                        {formatLongDate(nextAppointment.date)}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">
                        {nextAppointment.time} hs
                      </span>
                      {(nextAppointment.branchName ||
                        nextAppointment.office) && (
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground sm:justify-end">
                          <MapPin className="size-3.5" />
                          {[
                            nextAppointment.branchName,
                            nextAppointment.office &&
                              `Consultorio ${nextAppointment.office}`,
                          ]
                            .filter(Boolean)
                            .join(" Â· ")}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <Empty className="py-6">
                    <EmptyHeader>
                      <EmptyMedia variant="icon">
                        <CalendarPlus />
                      </EmptyMedia>
                      <EmptyTitle>No tenÃ©s turnos prÃ³ximos</EmptyTitle>
                      <EmptyDescription>
                        ReservÃ¡ tu prÃ³xima consulta en segundos.
                      </EmptyDescription>
                    </EmptyHeader>
                    <Button onClick={() => setTab("reservar")}>
                      <CalendarPlus data-icon="inline-start" />
                      Reservar turno
                    </Button>
                  </Empty>
                )}
              </CardContent>
            </Card>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-1">
                <StatMini
                  icon={<CalendarCheck className="size-5" />}
                  value={upcoming.length}
                  label="Turnos prÃ³ximos"
                />
                <StatMini
                  icon={<Stethoscope className="size-5" />}
                  value={
                    past.filter(
                      (appointment) => appointment.status === "completed"
                    ).length
                  }
                  label="Consultas realizadas"
                />
              </div>
              <Card className="flex-1 border-dashed">
                <CardContent className="flex h-full flex-col items-start justify-center gap-3 py-6">
                  <p className="text-sm text-pretty text-muted-foreground">
                    Â¿NecesitÃ¡s ver a un especialista? ReservÃ¡ un nuevo turno
                    ahora.
                  </p>
                  <Button className="w-full" onClick={() => setTab("reservar")}>
                    <CalendarPlus data-icon="inline-start" />
                    Nueva reserva
                  </Button>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            </div>
          </div>
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="reservar">
          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle>Reservar un nuevo turno</CardTitle>
              <CardDescription>
                SeguÃ­ los pasos para agendar tu consulta mÃ©dica.
              </CardDescription>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent>
              <BookingStepper
                branches={branches}
                specialties={specialties}
                medics={medics}
                onBooked={() => setTab("turnos")}
              />
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="turnos" className="flex flex-col gap-6">
          <section className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">
              PrÃ³ximos ({upcoming.length})
            </h2>
            {upcoming.length ? (
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
                {upcoming.map((appointment, index) => (
                  <Reveal key={appointment.id} delay={staggerDelay(index)}>
                    <AppointmentCard
                      appointment={appointment}
                      onCancel={() => setToCancel(appointment)}
                      onReschedule={() => {
                        setRescheduleError(null)
                        setToReschedule(appointment)
                      }}
                      onReview={() => {
                        setReviewError(null)
                        setToReview(appointment)
                      }}
                      reviewed={reviewed.has(appointment.id)}
                    />
                  </Reveal>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <Card>
                <CardContent className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                  No tenÃ©s turnos prÃ³ximos agendados.
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            )}
          </section>

          <Separator />

          <section className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">
              Historial ({past.length})
            </h2>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              {past.map((appointment, index) => (
                <Reveal key={appointment.id} delay={staggerDelay(index)}>
                  <AppointmentCard
                    appointment={appointment}
                    onReview={() => {
                      setReviewError(null)
                      setToReview(appointment)
                    }}
                    reviewed={reviewed.has(appointment.id)}
                  />
                </Reveal>
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>
        </TabsContent>
      </Tabs>

      <RescheduleDialog
        appointment={toReschedule}
        medic={medics.find(
          (candidate) => candidate.id === toReschedule?.doctorId
        )}
        open={!!toReschedule}
        onOpenChange={(open) => {
          if (!open) {
            setToReschedule(null)
            setRescheduleError(null)
          }
        }}
        submitting={savingReschedule}
        error={rescheduleError}
        onSubmit={submitReschedule}
      />

      <ReviewDialog
        appointment={toReview}
        open={!!toReview}
        onOpenChange={(open) => {
          if (!open) {
            setToReview(null)
            setReviewError(null)
          }
        }}
        submitting={savingReview}
        error={reviewError}
        onSubmit={submitReview}
      />

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={!!toCancel}
        onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setToCancel(null)}
        title="Â¿Cancelar este turno?"
        description={
          toCancel
            ? `${toCancel.specialtyName} con ${toCancel.doctorName} el ${formatShortDate(toCancel.date)} a las ${toCancel.time} hs. Esta acciÃ³n no se puede deshacer.`
            : ""
        }
        cancelLabel="Volver"
        confirmLabel="SÃ­, cancelar turno"
        onConfirm={confirmCancel}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

===== .\src\components\patient\RescheduleDialog.tsx =====

"use client"

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react"
import { Clock } from "lucide-react"
import { generateTimeSlots } from "@/lib/bookingSlots"
import { formatLongDate, toISODate } from "@/lib/format/date"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import type { AppointmentDto } from "@/models/Appointment"
import { medicSchedule, type MedicDto } from "@/models/Medic"
import { getTakenSlots } from "@/routes/AppointmentRoutes"
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Calendar } from "@/components/ui/calendar"
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogBody,
  DialogClose,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog"
import { Spinner } from "@/components/ui/spinner"

export default function RescheduleDialog({
  appointment,
  medic,
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  submitting,
  error,
  onSubmit,
}: {
  appointment: AppointmentDto | null
  /** Aporta los dÃ­as que el profesional atiende; puede faltar en datos viejos. */
  medic: MedicDto | undefined
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  submitting: boolean
  error: string | null
  onSubmit: (date: string, time: string) => void
}) {
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-2xl">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>Reprogramar turno</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            {appointment
              ? `ElegÃ­ la nueva fecha y hora para tu consulta con ${appointment.doctorName}.`
              : "ElegÃ­ la nueva fecha y hora."}
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        {appointment && (
          // Remontar al cambiar de turno descarta la selecciÃ³n del anterior.
          <RescheduleForm
            key={appointment.id}
            appointment={appointment}
            medic={medic}
            submitting={submitting}
            error={error}
            onSubmit={onSubmit}
          />
        )}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}

function RescheduleForm({
  appointment,
  medic,
  submitting,
  error,
  onSubmit,
}: {
  appointment: AppointmentDto
  medic: MedicDto | undefined
  submitting: boolean
  error: string | null
  onSubmit: (date: string, time: string) => void
}) {
  const [date, setDate] = useState<Date | undefined>(undefined)
  const [time, setTime] = useState("")
  const [taken, setTaken] = useState<string[]>([])
  const [loadingSlots, setLoadingSlots] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!date) {
      setTaken([])
      return
    }

    let active = true
    setLoadingSlots(true)

    getTakenSlots(appointment.doctorId, toISODate(date))
      .then((slots) => {
        if (!active) return
        // El horario actual del propio turno no cuenta como ocupado: si se
        // cambia solo la fecha, se puede conservar la misma hora.
        setTaken(
          slots.filter(
            (slot) =>
              !(
                toISODate(date) === appointment.date && slot === appointment.time
              )
          )
        )
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) setTaken([])
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (active) setLoadingSlots(false)
      })

    return () => {
      active = false
    }
  }, [date, appointment])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (time && taken.includes(time)) setTime("")
  }, [taken, time])

  // Sin datos del profesional se usa la jornada por defecto: el servidor
  // valida igual, asÃ­ que en el peor caso se ofrece un horario que rechaza.
  const timeSlots = useMemo(
    () => generateTimeSlots(medic ? medicSchedule(medic) : undefined),
    [medic]
  )

  const unchanged =
    !!date && toISODate(date) === appointment.date && time === appointment.time

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={(event) => {
        event.preventDefault()
        if (!date || !time) return
        onSubmit(toISODate(date), time)
      }}
      className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col"
    >
      <DialogBody>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 py-4">
          <p className="rounded-lg bg-muted/60 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Turno actual:{" "}
            <span className="font-medium text-foreground">
              {formatLongDate(appointment.date)} a las {appointment.time} hs
            </span>
          </p>

          <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[auto_1fr]">
            <div className="flex justify-center rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-2">
              <Calendar
                mode="single"
                selected={date}
                onSelect={setDate}
                disabled={(candidate) => {
                  const today = new Date()
                  today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
                  if (candidate < today) return true
                  // Sin datos del profesional se permite cualquier dÃ­a y decide
                  // el servidor, que es quien tiene la informaciÃ³n completa.
                  if (!medic) return false
                  return !medic.workDays.includes(candidate.getDay())
                }}
                className="bg-transparent p-0"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium">
                <Clock className="size-4 text-primary" />
                {date ? formatLongDate(toISODate(date)) : "SeleccionÃ¡ un dÃ­a"}
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4">
                {timeSlots.map((slot) => {
                  const selected = time === slot
                  const busy = taken.includes(slot)
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={slot}
                      type="button"
                      disabled={!date || busy || loadingSlots}
                      title={busy ? "Horario ya reservado" : undefined}
                      onClick={() => setTime(slot)}
                      className={cn(
                        "rounded-lg border px-2 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-all disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40",
                        selected
                          ? "border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow-sm"
                          : "border-border bg-card hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-accent/50",
                        busy && "line-through"
                      )}
                    >
                      {slot}
                    </button>
                  )
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {unchanged && (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              Es el mismo horario que ya tenÃ­as: elegÃ­ otro para poder guardar.
            </p>
          )}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive">
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}
        </div>
      </DialogBody>

      <DialogFooter>
        <DialogClose
          render={
            <Button type="button" variant="outline" disabled={submitting}>
              Cancelar
            </Button>
          }
        />
        <Button type="submit" disabled={submitting || !date || !time || unchanged}>
          {submitting && <Spinner data-icon="inline-start" />}
          Confirmar cambio
        </Button>
      </DialogFooter>
    </form>
  )
}

===== .\src\components\patient\ReviewDialog.tsx =====

"use client"

import { useState } from "react"
import { Star } from "lucide-react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import type { AppointmentDto } from "@/models/Appointment"
import { MAX_SCORE, MIN_SCORE } from "@/models/Review"
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogClose,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog"
import { Field, FieldDescription, FieldLabel } from "@/components/ui/field"
import { Spinner } from "@/components/ui/spinner"
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea"

const scoreLabels: Record<number, string> = {
  1: "Muy malo",
  2: "Malo",
  3: "Regular",
  4: "Bueno",
  5: "Excelente",
}

const scores = Array.from(
  { length: MAX_SCORE - MIN_SCORE + 1 },
  (_, index) => MIN_SCORE + index
)

export default function ReviewDialog({
  appointment,
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  submitting,
  error,
  onSubmit,
}: {
  appointment: AppointmentDto | null
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  submitting: boolean
  error: string | null
  onSubmit: (score: number, comment: string) => void
}) {
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-md">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>Calificar la atenciÃ³n</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            {appointment
              ? `Tu consulta con ${appointment.doctorName}.`
              : "Contanos cÃ³mo fue tu consulta."}
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        {/* Remontar al cambiar de turno limpia la puntuaciÃ³n anterior. */}
        <ReviewForm
          key={appointment?.id ?? "none"}
          submitting={submitting}
          error={error}
          onSubmit={onSubmit}
        />
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}

function ReviewForm({
  submitting,
  error,
  onSubmit,
}: {
  submitting: boolean
  error: string | null
  onSubmit: (score: number, comment: string) => void
}) {
  const [score, setScore] = useState(0)
  const [hovered, setHovered] = useState(0)
  const [comment, setComment] = useState("")

  // Al pasar el mouse se previsualiza esa puntuaciÃ³n sin cambiar la elegida.
  const shown = hovered || score

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={(event) => {
        event.preventDefault()
        onSubmit(score, comment)
      }}
    >
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 py-4">
        <Field>
          <FieldLabel>Â¿CÃ³mo fue la atenciÃ³n?</FieldLabel>
          <div
            className="flex items-center gap-1"
            onMouseLeave={() => setHovered(0)}
          >
            {scores.map((value) => (
              <button
                key={value}
                type="button"
                aria-label={`${value} ${value === 1 ? "estrella" : "estrellas"}`}
                aria-pressed={score === value}
                onMouseEnter={() => setHovered(value)}
                onFocus={() => setHovered(value)}
                onBlur={() => setHovered(0)}
                onClick={() => setScore(value)}
                className="rounded-md p-1 transition-transform hover:scale-110 focus-visible:ring-3 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-none"
              >
                <Star
                  className={cn(
                    "size-7 transition-colors",
                    value <= shown
                      ? "fill-warning text-warning"
                      : "text-muted-foreground/40"
                  )}
                />
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <FieldDescription>
            {shown ? scoreLabels[shown] : "ElegÃ­ de 1 a 5 estrellas."}
          </FieldDescription>
        </Field>

        <Field>
          <FieldLabel htmlFor="comment">Comentario (opcional)</FieldLabel>
          <Textarea
            id="comment"
            rows={3}
            maxLength={500}
            value={comment}
            onChange={(event) => setComment(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="Contanos cÃ³mo fue tu experiencia."
          />
        </Field>

        {error && (
          <Alert variant="destructive">
            <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        )}
      </div>

      <DialogFooter>
        <DialogClose
          render={
            <Button type="button" variant="outline" disabled={submitting}>
              Cancelar
            </Button>
          }
        />
        <Button type="submit" disabled={submitting || score === 0}>
          {submitting && <Spinner data-icon="inline-start" />}
          Enviar calificaciÃ³n
        </Button>
      </DialogFooter>
    </form>
  )
}

===== .\src\components\ui\alert.tsx =====

import * as React from "react"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const alertVariants = cva(
  "group/alert relative grid w-full gap-0.5 rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-2 text-left text-sm has-data-[slot=alert-action]:relative has-data-[slot=alert-action]:pr-18 has-[>svg]:grid-cols-[auto_1fr] has-[>svg]:gap-x-2 *:[svg]:row-span-2 *:[svg]:translate-y-0.5 *:[svg]:text-current *:[svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-card text-card-foreground",
        destructive:
          "bg-card text-destructive *:data-[slot=alert-description]:text-destructive/90 *:[svg]:text-current",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

function Alert({
  className,
  variant,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & VariantProps<typeof alertVariants>) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert"
      role="alert"
      className={cn(alertVariants({ variant }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertTitle({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-title"
      className={cn(
        "font-medium group-has-[>svg]/alert:col-start-2 [&_a]:underline [&_a]:underline-offset-3 [&_a]:hover:text-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertDescription({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-description"
      className={cn(
        "text-sm text-balance text-muted-foreground md:text-pretty [&_a]:underline [&_a]:underline-offset-3 [&_a]:hover:text-foreground [&_p:not(:last-child)]:mb-4",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertAction({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-action"
      className={cn("absolute top-2 right-2", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Alert, AlertTitle, AlertDescription, AlertAction }

      {...props}
    />
  )
}

      data-slot="avatar-group"

        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AvatarGroupCount({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {

    <div
      data-slot="avatar-group-count"
      className={cn(

        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )

===== .\src\components\ui\badge.tsx =====

import { mergeProps } from "@base-ui/react/merge-props"
import { useRender } from "@base-ui/react/use-render"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const badgeVariants = cva(
  "group/badge inline-flex h-5 w-fit shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1 overflow-hidden rounded-4xl border border-transparent px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-all focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-[3px] focus-visible:ring-ring/50 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-1.5 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-1.5 aria-invalid:border-destructive aria-invalid:ring-destructive/20 dark:aria-invalid:ring-destructive/40 [&>svg]:pointer-events-none [&>svg]:size-3!",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-primary text-primary-foreground [a]:hover:bg-primary/80",
        secondary:
          "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground [a]:hover:bg-secondary/80",
        destructive:
          "bg-destructive/10 text-destructive focus-visible:ring-destructive/20 dark:bg-destructive/20 dark:focus-visible:ring-destructive/40 [a]:hover:bg-destructive/20",
        outline:
          "border-border text-foreground [a]:hover:bg-muted [a]:hover:text-muted-foreground",
        ghost:
          "hover:bg-muted hover:text-muted-foreground dark:hover:bg-muted/50",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

function Badge({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  render,
  ...props
}: useRender.ComponentProps<"span"> & VariantProps<typeof badgeVariants>) {
  return useRender({
    defaultTagName: "span",
    props: mergeProps<"span">(
      {
        className: cn(badgeVariants({ variant }), className),
      },
      props
    ),
    render,
    state: {
      slot: "badge",
      variant,
    },
  })
}

export { Badge, badgeVariants }

===== .\src\components\ui\button.tsx =====

import { Button as ButtonPrimitive } from "@base-ui/react/button"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "group/button inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-transparent bg-clip-padding text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-all outline-none select-none focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-3 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 active:not-aria-[haspopup]:translate-y-px disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 aria-invalid:border-destructive aria-invalid:ring-3 aria-invalid:ring-destructive/20 dark:aria-invalid:border-destructive/50 dark:aria-invalid:ring-destructive/40 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary/80",
        outline:
          "border-border bg-background hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground aria-expanded:bg-muted aria-expanded:text-foreground dark:border-input dark:bg-input/30 dark:hover:bg-input/50",
        secondary:
          "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground hover:bg-[color-mix(in_oklch,var(--secondary),var(--foreground)_5%)] aria-expanded:bg-secondary aria-expanded:text-secondary-foreground",
        ghost:
          "hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground aria-expanded:bg-muted aria-expanded:text-foreground dark:hover:bg-muted/50",
        destructive:
          "bg-destructive/10 text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/20 focus-visible:border-destructive/40 focus-visible:ring-destructive/20 dark:bg-destructive/20 dark:hover:bg-destructive/30 dark:focus-visible:ring-destructive/40",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
      size: {
        default:
          "h-8 gap-1.5 px-2.5 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-2 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-2",
        xs: "h-6 gap-1 rounded-[min(var(--radius-md),10px)] px-2 text-xs in-data-[slot=button-group]:rounded-lg has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-1.5 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-1.5 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3",
        sm: "h-7 gap-1 rounded-[min(var(--radius-md),12px)] px-2.5 text-[0.8rem] in-data-[slot=button-group]:rounded-lg has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-1.5 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-1.5 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3.5",
        lg: "h-9 gap-1.5 px-2.5 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-2 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-2",
        icon: "size-8",
        "icon-xs":
          "size-6 rounded-[min(var(--radius-md),10px)] in-data-[slot=button-group]:rounded-lg [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3",
        "icon-sm":
          "size-7 rounded-[min(var(--radius-md),12px)] in-data-[slot=button-group]:rounded-lg",
        "icon-lg": "size-9",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  }
)

function Button({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  size = "default",
  ...props
}: ButtonPrimitive.Props & VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants>) {
  return (
    <ButtonPrimitive
      data-slot="button"
      className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Button, buttonVariants }

"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import {
  DayPicker,
  getDefaultClassNames,
  type DayButton,
  type Locale,
} from "react-day-picker"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { Button, buttonVariants } from "@/components/ui/button"
import {
  ChevronLeftIcon,
  ChevronRightIcon,
  ChevronDownIcon,
} from "lucide-react"

function Calendar({
  className,
  classNames,
  showOutsideDays = true,
  captionLayout = "label",
  buttonVariant = "ghost",
  locale,
  formatters,
  components,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DayPicker> & {
  buttonVariant?: React.ComponentProps<typeof Button>["variant"]
}) {
  const defaultClassNames = getDefaultClassNames()

  return (
    <DayPicker
      showOutsideDays={showOutsideDays}
      className={cn(
        "group/calendar bg-background p-2 [--cell-radius:var(--radius-md)] [--cell-size:--spacing(7)] in-data-[slot=card-content]:bg-transparent in-data-[slot=popover-content]:bg-transparent",
        String.raw`rtl:**:[.rdp-button\_next>svg]:rotate-180`,
        String.raw`rtl:**:[.rdp-button\_previous>svg]:rotate-180`,
        className
      )}
      captionLayout={captionLayout}
      locale={locale}
      formatters={{
        formatMonthDropdown: (date) =>
          date.toLocaleString(locale?.code, { month: "short" }),
        ...formatters,
      }}
      classNames={{
        root: cn("w-fit", defaultClassNames.root),
        months: cn(
          "relative flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row",
          defaultClassNames.months
        ),
        month: cn("flex w-full flex-col gap-4", defaultClassNames.month),
        nav: cn(
          "absolute inset-x-0 top-0 flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-1",
          defaultClassNames.nav
        ),
        button_previous: cn(
          buttonVariants({ variant: buttonVariant }),
          "size-(--cell-size) p-0 select-none aria-disabled:opacity-50",
          defaultClassNames.button_previous
        ),
        button_next: cn(
          buttonVariants({ variant: buttonVariant }),
          "size-(--cell-size) p-0 select-none aria-disabled:opacity-50",
          defaultClassNames.button_next
        ),
        month_caption: cn(
          "flex h-(--cell-size) w-full items-center justify-center px-(--cell-size)",
          defaultClassNames.month_caption
        ),
        dropdowns: cn(
          "flex h-(--cell-size) w-full items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium",
          defaultClassNames.dropdowns
        ),
        dropdown_root: cn(
          "relative rounded-(--cell-radius)",
          defaultClassNames.dropdown_root
        ),
        dropdown: cn(
          "absolute inset-0 bg-popover opacity-0",
          defaultClassNames.dropdown
        ),
        caption_label: cn(
          "font-medium select-none",
          captionLayout === "label"
            ? "text-sm"
            : "flex items-center gap-1 rounded-(--cell-radius) text-sm [&>svg]:size-3.5 [&>svg]:text-muted-foreground",
          defaultClassNames.caption_label
        ),
        month_grid: cn("w-full border-collapse", defaultClassNames.month_grid),
        weekdays: cn("flex", defaultClassNames.weekdays),
        weekday: cn(
          "flex-1 rounded-(--cell-radius) text-[0.8rem] font-normal text-muted-foreground select-none",
          defaultClassNames.weekday
        ),
        week: cn("mt-2 flex w-full", defaultClassNames.week),
        week_number_header: cn(
          "w-(--cell-size) select-none",
          defaultClassNames.week_number_header
        ),
        week_number: cn(
          "text-[0.8rem] text-muted-foreground select-none",
          defaultClassNames.week_number
        ),
        day: cn(
          "group/day relative aspect-square h-full w-full rounded-(--cell-radius) p-0 text-center select-none [&:last-child[data-selected=true]_button]:rounded-r-(--cell-radius)",
          props.showWeekNumber
            ? "[&:nth-child(2)[data-selected=true]_button]:rounded-l-(--cell-radius)"
            : "[&:first-child[data-selected=true]_button]:rounded-l-(--cell-radius)",
          defaultClassNames.day
        ),
        range_start: cn(
          "relative isolate z-0 rounded-l-(--cell-radius) bg-muted after:absolute after:inset-y-0 after:right-0 after:w-4 after:bg-muted",
          defaultClassNames.range_start
        ),
        range_middle: cn("rounded-none", defaultClassNames.range_middle),
        range_end: cn(
          "relative isolate z-0 rounded-r-(--cell-radius) bg-muted after:absolute after:inset-y-0 after:left-0 after:w-4 after:bg-muted",
          defaultClassNames.range_end
        ),
        today: cn(
          "rounded-(--cell-radius) bg-muted text-foreground data-[selected=true]:rounded-none",
          defaultClassNames.today
        ),
        outside: cn(
          "text-muted-foreground aria-selected:text-muted-foreground",
          defaultClassNames.outside
        ),
        disabled: cn(
          "text-muted-foreground opacity-50",
          defaultClassNames.disabled
        ),
        hidden: cn("invisible", defaultClassNames.hidden),
        ...classNames,
      }}
      components={{
        Root: ({ className, rootRef, ...props }) => {
          return (
            <div
              data-slot="calendar"
              ref={rootRef}
              className={cn(className)}
              {...props}
            />
          )
        },
        Chevron: ({ className, orientation, ...props }) => {
          if (orientation === "left") {
            return (
              <ChevronLeftIcon className={cn("size-4", className)} {...props} />
            )
          }

          if (orientation === "right") {
            return (
              <ChevronRightIcon
                className={cn("size-4", className)}
                {...props}
              />
            )
          }

          return (
            <ChevronDownIcon className={cn("size-4", className)} {...props} />
          )
        },
        DayButton: ({ ...props }) => (
          <CalendarDayButton locale={locale} {...props} />
        ),
        WeekNumber: ({ children, ...props }) => {
          return (
            <td {...props}>
              <div className="flex size-(--cell-size) items-center justify-center text-center">
                {children}
              </div>
            </td>
          )
        },
        ...components,
      }}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function CalendarDayButton({
  className,
  day,
  modifiers,
  locale,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DayButton> & { locale?: Partial<Locale> }) {
  const defaultClassNames = getDefaultClassNames()

  const ref = React.useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (modifiers.focused) ref.current?.focus()
  }, [modifiers.focused])

  return (
    <Button
      variant="ghost"
      size="icon"
      data-day={day.date.toLocaleDateString(locale?.code)}
      data-selected-single={
        modifiers.selected &&
        !modifiers.range_start &&
        !modifiers.range_end &&
        !modifiers.range_middle
      }
      data-range-start={modifiers.range_start}
      data-range-end={modifiers.range_end}
      data-range-middle={modifiers.range_middle}
      className={cn(
        "relative isolate z-10 flex aspect-square size-auto w-full min-w-(--cell-size) flex-col gap-1 border-0 leading-none font-normal group-data-[focused=true]/day:relative group-data-[focused=true]/day:z-10 group-data-[focused=true]/day:border-ring group-data-[focused=true]/day:ring-[3px] group-data-[focused=true]/day:ring-ring/50 data-[range-end=true]:rounded-(--cell-radius) data-[range-end=true]:rounded-r-(--cell-radius) data-[range-end=true]:bg-primary data-[range-end=true]:text-primary-foreground data-[range-middle=true]:rounded-none data-[range-middle=true]:bg-muted data-[range-middle=true]:text-foreground data-[range-start=true]:rounded-(--cell-radius) data-[range-start=true]:rounded-l-(--cell-radius) data-[range-start=true]:bg-primary data-[range-start=true]:text-primary-foreground data-[selected-single=true]:bg-primary data-[selected-single=true]:text-primary-foreground dark:hover:text-foreground [&>span]:text-xs [&>span]:opacity-70",
        defaultClassNames.day,
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Calendar, CalendarDayButton }
