===== .\src\lib\auth\authServer.ts =====

import { betterAuth } from "better-auth"
import { createHash } from "crypto"
import { pool } from "@/lib/db"

/**
 * BETTER_AUTH_SECRET is preferred, but the preview environment does not always
 * persist it between syncs. Deriving a deterministic fallback from DATABASE_URL
 * keeps sessions valid across server reloads.
 */
function resolveAuthSecret(): string {
  if (process.env.BETTER_AUTH_SECRET) return process.env.BETTER_AUTH_SECRET
  const seed = process.env.DATABASE_URL ?? "saludplus-dev-seed"
  return createHash("sha256").update(`saludplus:${seed}`).digest("base64")
}

/**
 * Without an explicit baseURL Better Auth has to guess the origin from each
 * request, which breaks callback URLs in local development. The fallback chain
 * ends on localhost so `npm run dev` works with no extra configuration.
 */
function resolveBaseURL(): string | undefined {
  if (process.env.BETTER_AUTH_URL) return process.env.BETTER_AUTH_URL
  if (process.env.VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL) {
    return `https://${process.env.VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL}`
  }
  if (process.env.VERCEL_URL) return `https://${process.env.VERCEL_URL}`
  if (process.env.V0_RUNTIME_URL) return process.env.V0_RUNTIME_URL
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "development") return "http://localhost:3000"
  return undefined
}

/**
 * The v0 preview renders the app inside a cross-site iframe, where the session
 * cookie only survives with SameSite=None; Secure. Those same attributes make
 * browsers drop the cookie over plain http://localhost, so the override is
 * scoped to the preview rather than to NODE_ENV.
 */
const isCrossSitePreview = Boolean(process.env.V0_RUNTIME_URL)

export const auth = betterAuth({
  database: pool,
  secret: resolveAuthSecret(),
  baseURL: resolveBaseURL(),
  emailAndPassword: {
    enabled: true,
    autoSignIn: true,
  },
  user: {
    additionalFields: {
      role: {
        type: "string",
        required: false,
        defaultValue: "patient",
        input: false,
      },
    },
  },
  trustedOrigins: [
    ...(process.env.V0_RUNTIME_URL ? [process.env.V0_RUNTIME_URL] : []),
    ...(process.env.VERCEL_URL ? [`https://${process.env.VERCEL_URL}`] : []),
    ...(process.env.VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL
      ? [`https://${process.env.VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL}`]
      : []),
    ...(process.env.NODE_ENV === "development"
      ? ["http://localhost:3000", "http://127.0.0.1:3000"]
      : []),
  ],
  session: {
    expiresIn: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7,
    updateAge: 60 * 60 * 24,
  },
  ...(isCrossSitePreview
    ? {
        advanced: {
          defaultCookieAttributes: {
            sameSite: "none" as const,
            secure: true,
          },
        },
      }
    : {}),
})

===== .\src\lib\auth\session.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { headers } from "next/headers"
import { auth } from "@/lib/auth/authServer"
import type { Role, SessionUser } from "@/models/User"

export async function getSessionUser(): Promise<SessionUser | null> {
  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() })
  if (!session?.user) return null

  const sessionUser = session.user as typeof session.user & { role?: string }
  return {
    id: sessionUser.id,
    name: sessionUser.name,
    email: sessionUser.email,
    role: (sessionUser.role as Role) ?? "patient",
    image: sessionUser.image,
  }
}

export async function requireUser(): Promise<SessionUser> {
  const user = await getSessionUser()
  if (!user) throw new Error("Unauthorized")
  return user
}

export async function requireAdmin(): Promise<SessionUser> {
  const user = await requireUser()
  if (user.role !== "admin") throw new Error("Unauthorized")
  return user
}

===== .\src\lib\calendar\googleCalendar.ts =====

import type { AppointmentDto } from "@/models/Appointment"
import { toIcsUtcStamp } from "@/lib/calendar/ics"

/**
 * Enlace que abre Google Calendar con el turno ya cargado.
 *
 * Por quÃ© esto y no la API de Google Calendar: la API exige credenciales de
 * OAuth 2.0 â€”un client ID y un client secret que se sacan de la consola de
 * Google Cloud, con pantalla de consentimiento verificadaâ€” y guardar el token
 * de cada persona. Sin esas credenciales, una integraciÃ³n con la API no
 * funciona: se puede escribir el cÃ³digo, pero no se puede probar ni usar.
 *
 * Este enlace, en cambio, funciona hoy y sin nada configurado. Abre Google
 * Calendar con el evento completo; la persona toca "Guardar" y el turno queda
 * en su calendario, con los avisos que tenga configurados en Google.
 *
 * Lo que se pierde frente a la API: hace falta ese clic, y si despuÃ©s se
 * cancela o reprograma el turno acÃ¡, el evento en Google no se entera. EstÃ¡
 * anotado en el propio evento para que nadie se confÃ­e.
 *
 * Formato de `dates`: dos marcas UTC bÃ¡sicas separadas por "/", que es lo que
 * espera Google. Se reutiliza `toIcsUtcStamp` para no tener dos conversiones
 * de zona horaria que puedan discrepar.
 */
export function buildGoogleCalendarUrl(
  appointment: AppointmentDto,
  { durationMinutes = 30 }: { durationMinutes?: number } = {}
): string {
  const inicio = toIcsUtcStamp(appointment.date, appointment.time)
  const fin = toIcsUtcStamp(appointment.date, appointment.time, durationMinutes)

  const lugar = [appointment.branchName, appointment.office && `Consultorio ${appointment.office}`]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(" Â· ")

  const detalle = [
    `Profesional: ${appointment.doctorName}`,
    `Especialidad: ${appointment.specialtyName}`,
    appointment.reason ? `Motivo: ${appointment.reason}` : "",
    "",
    "Turno reservado en SaludPlus.",
    "Si lo cancelÃ¡s o reprogramÃ¡s allÃ­, este evento no se actualiza solo.",
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join("\n")

  const params = new URLSearchParams({
    action: "TEMPLATE",
    text: `Turno Â· ${appointment.specialtyName} Â· ${appointment.doctorName}`,
    dates: `${inicio}/${fin}`,
    details: detalle,
  })

  // Google ignora un `location` vacÃ­o, pero mandarlo igual deja el enlace con
  // un parÃ¡metro de mÃ¡s sin valor. Mejor no incluirlo.
  if (lugar) params.set("location", lugar)

  return `https://calendar.google.com/calendar/render?${params.toString()}`
}

===== .\src\lib\calendar\ics.ts =====

import type { AppointmentDto } from "@/models/Appointment"

/**
 * Genera el archivo .ics de un turno (RFC 5545).
 *
 * Sin dependencias ni servicios externos: es texto plano. El navegador lo abre
 * en Google Calendar, Outlook o el calendario del telÃ©fono, que es lo que en la
 * prÃ¡ctica evita que la persona se olvide del turno.
 */

/**
 * Argentina estÃ¡ en UTC-3 todo el aÃ±o: no aplica horario de verano desde 2009.
 *
 * Se emiten horas absolutas en UTC en lugar de "hora flotante" porque asÃ­ el
 * turno cae en el momento correcto sin importar en quÃ© zona horaria estÃ©
 * configurado el dispositivo de quien lo abre. Si alguna vez volviera a haber
 * horario de verano, este es el Ãºnico lugar a tocar.
 */
const UTC_OFFSET_HOURS = 3

/** DuraciÃ³n de la consulta. Cuando cada profesional tenga su agenda, saldrÃ¡ de ahÃ­. */
const DEFAULT_DURATION_MINUTES = 30

/**
 * Escapa los caracteres que en un valor TEXT del formato tienen significado
 * propio. Sin esto, un motivo de consulta con una coma parte el campo en dos.
 */
function escapeText(value: string): string {
  return value
    .replace(/\\/g, "\\\\")
    .replace(/;/g, "\\;")
    .replace(/,/g, "\\,")
    .replace(/\r?\n/g, "\\n")
}

/**
 * Pliega las lÃ­neas a 75 octetos, como exige el RFC.
 *
 * Se cuenta en bytes UTF-8, no en caracteres: una descripciÃ³n con acentos
 * llega al lÃ­mite antes de lo que sugiere su longitud, y cortar en medio de un
 * carÃ¡cter multibyte produce un archivo que algunos clientes rechazan.
 */
function foldLine(line: string): string {
  const encoder = new TextEncoder()
  if (encoder.encode(line).length <= 75) return line

  const partes: string[] = []
  let actual = ""
  let bytes = 0
  // La primera lÃ­nea admite 75 octetos; las continuaciones llevan un espacio
  // inicial que tambiÃ©n cuenta.
  let limite = 75

  for (const caracter of line) {
    const tamano = encoder.encode(caracter).length
    if (bytes + tamano > limite) {
      partes.push(actual)
      actual = ""
      bytes = 0
      limite = 74
    }
    actual += caracter
    bytes += tamano
  }
  if (actual) partes.push(actual)

  return partes.join("\r\n ")
}

/** "2026-06-10" + "10:00" â†’ "20260610T130000Z" */
export function toIcsUtcStamp(
  date: string,
  time: string,
  addMinutes = 0
): string {
  const [year, month, day] = date.split("-").map(Number)
  const [hour, minute] = time.split(":").map(Number)

  const instante = Date.UTC(
    year,
    month - 1,
    day,
    hour + UTC_OFFSET_HOURS,
    minute + addMinutes
  )
  return new Date(instante).toISOString().replace(/[-:]/g, "").slice(0, 15) + "Z"
}

function place(appointment: AppointmentDto): string {
  return [
    appointment.branchName,
    appointment.office && `Consultorio ${appointment.office}`,
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(", ")
}

export function buildAppointmentIcs(
  appointment: AppointmentDto,
  options: { now?: Date; durationMinutes?: number } = {}
): string {
  const duracion = options.durationMinutes ?? DEFAULT_DURATION_MINUTES
  const generadoEn = (options.now ?? new Date())
    .toISOString()
    .replace(/[-:]/g, "")
    .slice(0, 15)

  const descripcion = [
    appointment.specialtyName,
    appointment.reason && `Motivo: ${appointment.reason}`,
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(" â€” ")

  const lineas = [
    "BEGIN:VCALENDAR",
    "VERSION:2.0",
    "PRODID:-//SaludPlus//Turnos Medicos//ES",
    "CALSCALE:GREGORIAN",
    "METHOD:PUBLISH",
    "BEGIN:VEVENT",
    // El UID identifica el evento: reimportar el mismo turno actualiza el que
    // ya estÃ¡ en el calendario en lugar de agregar un duplicado.
    `UID:${appointment.id}@saludplus`,
    `DTSTAMP:${generadoEn}Z`,
    `DTSTART:${toIcsUtcStamp(appointment.date, appointment.time)}`,
    `DTEND:${toIcsUtcStamp(appointment.date, appointment.time, duracion)}`,
    `SUMMARY:${escapeText(`Consulta con ${appointment.doctorName}`)}`,
  ]

  if (descripcion) lineas.push(`DESCRIPTION:${escapeText(descripcion)}`)

  const lugar = place(appointment)
  if (lugar) lineas.push(`LOCATION:${escapeText(lugar)}`)

  lineas.push(
    appointment.status === "cancelled" ? "STATUS:CANCELLED" : "STATUS:CONFIRMED",
    // Aviso del propio calendario 24 horas antes, ademÃ¡s del de la campanita.
    "BEGIN:VALARM",
    "TRIGGER:-PT24H",
    "ACTION:DISPLAY",
    "DESCRIPTION:Recordatorio de turno medico",
    "END:VALARM",
    "END:VEVENT",
    "END:VCALENDAR"
  )

  // El RFC exige CRLF, y una lÃ­nea en blanco final.
  return lineas.map(foldLine).join("\r\n") + "\r\n"
}

/** Nombre sugerido de descarga: legible y sin caracteres problemÃ¡ticos. */
export function icsFileName(appointment: AppointmentDto): string {
  return `turno-${appointment.date}-${appointment.time.replace(":", "")}.ics`
}

===== .\src\lib\db\index.ts =====

import { drizzle } from "drizzle-orm/node-postgres"
import { Pool } from "pg"
import * as schema from "./schema"

/**
 * Managed providers (Neon, Supabase, RDS) require SSL, but a local PostgreSQL
 * almost never has it enabled. Appending `sslmode=require` unconditionally is
 * what makes `npm run dev` fail against localhost with
 * "The server does not support SSL connections", so the flag is only added for
 * remote hosts. `uselibpqcompat=true` keeps libpq semantics and silences the
 * deprecation warning emitted by pg.
 */
function isLocalHost(raw: string): boolean {
  try {
    const { hostname } = new URL(raw)
    return (
      hostname === "localhost" ||
      hostname === "127.0.0.1" ||
      hostname === "::1" ||
      hostname === "host.docker.internal"
    )
  } catch {
    // A malformed URL is reported by the Pool itself with a better message.
    return false
  }
}

function buildConnectionString(): string {
  const raw = process.env.DATABASE_URL?.trim() ?? ""

  if (!raw) {
    throw new Error(
      [
        "Falta la variable DATABASE_URL.",
        "CopiÃ¡ .env.local.example a .env.local y completÃ¡ la cadena de conexiÃ³n.",
        "Ejemplo local: postgresql://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/saludplus",
      ].join(" ")
    )
  }

  if (isLocalHost(raw)) return raw
  if (/[?&]uselibpqcompat=/.test(raw)) return raw

  const separator = raw.includes("?") ? "&" : "?"
  const extra = /[?&]sslmode=/.test(raw)
    ? "uselibpqcompat=true"
    : "uselibpqcompat=true&sslmode=require"
  return `${raw}${separator}${extra}`
}

export const pool = new Pool({ connectionString: buildConnectionString() })
export const db = drizzle(pool, { schema })

import {
  pgTable,
  text,
  timestamp,
  boolean,
  integer,
  real,
} from "drizzle-orm/pg-core"

// --- Better Auth required tables -------------------------------------------
// Column names are camelCase to match Better Auth's defaults. Do not rename.
// These live in the public schema (self-hosted Better Auth), separate from the
// managed `neon_auth` schema.

export const user = pgTable("user", {
  id: text("id").primaryKey(),
  name: text("name").notNull(),
  email: text("email").notNull().unique(),
  emailVerified: boolean("emailVerified").notNull().default(false),
  image: text("image"),
  // Application role: patient | doctor | admin
  role: text("role").notNull().default("patient"),
  createdAt: timestamp("createdAt").notNull().defaultNow(),
  updatedAt: timestamp("updatedAt").notNull().defaultNow(),
})

export const session = pgTable("session", {
  id: text("id").primaryKey(),
  expiresAt: timestamp("expiresAt").notNull(),
  token: text("token").notNull().unique(),
  createdAt: timestamp("createdAt").notNull().defaultNow(),
  updatedAt: timestamp("updatedAt").notNull().defaultNow(),
  ipAddress: text("ipAddress"),
  userAgent: text("userAgent"),
  userId: text("userId")
    .notNull()
    .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
})

export const account = pgTable("account", {
  id: text("id").primaryKey(),
  accountId: text("accountId").notNull(),
  providerId: text("providerId").notNull(),
  // Not part of Better Auth's account model: it never writes this column, so a
  // NOT NULL constraint here makes every sign-up fail on insert.
  issuer: text("issuer"),
  userId: text("userId")
    .notNull()
    .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
  accessToken: text("accessToken"),
  refreshToken: text("refreshToken"),
  idToken: text("idToken"),
  accessTokenExpiresAt: timestamp("accessTokenExpiresAt"),
  refreshTokenExpiresAt: timestamp("refreshTokenExpiresAt"),
  scope: text("scope"),
  password: text("password"),
  createdAt: timestamp("createdAt").notNull().defaultNow(),
  updatedAt: timestamp("updatedAt").notNull().defaultNow(),
})

export const verification = pgTable("verification", {
  id: text("id").primaryKey(),
  identifier: text("identifier").notNull(),
  value: text("value").notNull(),
  expiresAt: timestamp("expiresAt").notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp("createdAt").defaultNow(),
  updatedAt: timestamp("updatedAt").defaultNow(),
})

})

  id: text("id").primaryKey(),
  // UNIQUE en la base: una sola reseÃ±a por turno.
  appointmentId: text("appointmentId")
    .notNull()
    .references(() => appointments.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
  doctorId: text("doctorId")
    .notNull()
    .references(() => doctors.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
  patientId: text("patientId")
    .notNull()
    .references(() => patients.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
  // Restringido a 1..5 por un CHECK en la base.
  score: integer("score").notNull(),
  comment: text("comment"),
  createdAt: timestamp("createdAt").notNull().defaultNow(),
})

export const notifications = pgTable("notifications", {

    .notNull()
    .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),

  // appointment_status | review_received
  type: text("type").notNull(),
  title: text("title").notNull(),
  body: text("body").notNull().default(""),
  // UNIQUE junto con userId: permite reintentar la generaciÃ³n de avisos sin
  // duplicarlos.
  dedupeKey: text("dedupeKey"),
  readAt: timestamp("readAt"),
  createdAt: timestamp("createdAt").notNull().defaultNow(),
})

===== .\src\lib\format\date.ts =====

const monthNames = [
  "enero",
  "febrero",
  "marzo",
  "abril",
  "mayo",
  "junio",
  "julio",
  "agosto",
  "septiembre",
  "octubre",
  "noviembre",
  "diciembre",
]

const shortMonthNames = [
  "ene",
  "feb",
  "mar",
  "abr",
  "may",
  "jun",
  "jul",
  "ago",
  "sep",
  "oct",
  "nov",
  "dic",
]

const weekdayNames = [
  "domingo",
  "lunes",
  "martes",
  "miÃ©rcoles",
  "jueves",
  "viernes",
  "sÃ¡bado",
]

const millisecondsPerDay = 86400000

function capitalize(value: string): string {
  return value.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + value.slice(1)
}

export function toISODate(date: Date): string {
  const year = date.getFullYear()
  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")
  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")
  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`
}

export function todayISODate(): string {
  return toISODate(new Date())
}

/** Builds a local Date from an ISO date so the calendar day is not shifted by the timezone. */
export function parseISODate(iso: string): Date {
  const [year, month, day] = iso.slice(0, 10).split("-").map(Number)
  return new Date(year, month - 1, day)
}

export function formatLongDate(iso: string): string {
  const date = parseISODate(iso)
  return `${capitalize(weekdayNames[date.getDay()])} ${date.getDate()} de ${monthNames[date.getMonth()]}`
}

export function formatShortDate(iso: string): string {
  const date = parseISODate(iso)
  return `${date.getDate()} ${shortMonthNames[date.getMonth()]} ${date.getFullYear()}`
}

export function formatShortMonth(iso: string): string {
  return shortMonthNames[parseISODate(iso).getMonth()]
}

export function calculateAge(birthDate: string): number {
  const birth = parseISODate(birthDate)
  const now = new Date()
  const monthDelta = now.getMonth() - birth.getMonth()
  const hasHadBirthday =
    monthDelta > 0 || (monthDelta === 0 && now.getDate() >= birth.getDate())
  return now.getFullYear() - birth.getFullYear() - (hasHadBirthday ? 0 : 1)
}

export function daysUntil(iso: string): number {
  const target = parseISODate(iso)
  const today = new Date()
  today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
  return Math.round((target.getTime() - today.getTime()) / millisecondsPerDay)
}

===== .\src\lib\format\dni.ts =====

/**
 * El DNI se guarda como dÃ­gitos puros y se muestra con puntos.
 *
 * Los datos de carga inicial venÃ­an con formato ("35.812.443") y los usuarios
 * escriben de las dos formas, asÃ­ que se normaliza al entrar y se formatea al
 * salir. Guardar el formato harÃ­a fallar cualquier bÃºsqueda o comparaciÃ³n.
 */

export const DNI_MIN_DIGITS = 7
export const DNI_MAX_DIGITS = 8

/** Deja solo los dÃ­gitos: "35.812.443" y "35 812 443" dan "35812443". */
export function normalizeDni(value: string): string {
  return value.replace(/\D/g, "")
}

/** "35812443" â†’ "35.812.443". Devuelve el valor tal cual si no es un DNI vÃ¡lido. */
export function formatDni(value: string): string {
  const digits = normalizeDni(value)
  if (!digits) return ""
  return digits.replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ".")
}

===== .\src\lib\format\initials.ts =====

export function getInitials(name: string): string {
  return name
    .replace(/^(Dra?\.?)\s+/i, "")
    .split(" ")
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, 2)
    .map((part) => part[0]?.toUpperCase())
    .join("")
}

===== .\src\lib\actionResult.ts =====

/**
 * Server Actions that throw send the client a generic, redacted message in
 * production, so the validation text written in the `validations` layer never
 * reaches the user. Returning the failure as a value keeps it intact.
 *
 * Lives outside `src/routes` on purpose: a "use server" module may only export
 * async functions, so neither the type nor the helper could be declared there.
 */
export type ActionResult<T> =
  | { ok: true; data: T }
  | { ok: false; error: string }

export async function runAction<T>(
  operation: () => Promise<T>
): Promise<ActionResult<T>> {
  try {
    return { ok: true, data: await operation() }
  } catch (error) {
    const message =
      error instanceof Error && error.message
        ? error.message
        : "OcurriÃ³ un error inesperado."

    // "Unauthorized" is thrown by the session guards and is not worth showing
    // verbatim to the person sitting in front of the screen.
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        message === "Unauthorized"
          ? "No tenÃ©s permisos para realizar esta acciÃ³n."
          : message,
    }
  }
}

===== .\src\lib\bookingSlots.ts =====

/**
 * GeneraciÃ³n de horarios de atenciÃ³n.
 *
 * Antes esto era una constante: catorce horarios idÃ©nticos para todos los
 * profesionales. Un traumatÃ³logo con consultas de 40 minutos y un clÃ­nico con
 * consultas de 15 no pueden compartir la misma grilla, asÃ­ que cada agenda se
 * calcula a partir de la jornada del profesional.
 */

export interface Schedule {
  /** Inicio de la jornada, "HH:MM". */
  startTime: string
  /** Fin de la jornada. El Ãºltimo turno empieza antes de esta hora. */
  endTime: string
  /** Inicio del corte del mediodÃ­a, o vacÃ­o si no hay. */
  breakStart: string
  breakEnd: string
  /** DuraciÃ³n de cada consulta, en minutos. */
  slotMinutes: number
}

export const DEFAULT_SCHEDULE: Schedule = {
  startTime: "08:00",
  endTime: "18:00",
  breakStart: "12:00",
  breakEnd: "15:00",
  slotMinutes: 30,
}

const timePattern = /^([01]\d|2[0-3]):([0-5]\d)$/

/** "10:30" â†’ 630. Devuelve null si el formato no es vÃ¡lido. */
export function toMinutes(time: string): number | null {
  const match = timePattern.exec(time.trim())
  if (!match) return null
  return Number(match[1]) * 60 + Number(match[2])
}

/** 630 â†’ "10:30" */
export function toTimeString(minutes: number): string {
  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60)
  const rest = minutes % 60
  return `${String(hours).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(rest).padStart(2, "0")}`
}

/**
 * Devuelve los horarios en los que se puede reservar con este profesional.
 *
 * Un turno entra solo si termina dentro de la jornada y no se superpone con el
 * corte: ofrecer un horario que empieza cinco minutos antes del cierre es peor
 * que no ofrecerlo.
 */
export function generateTimeSlots(schedule: Partial<Schedule> = {}): string[] {
  const {
    startTime,
    endTime,
    breakStart,
    breakEnd,
    slotMinutes,
  } = { ...DEFAULT_SCHEDULE, ...schedule }

  const inicio = toMinutes(startTime)
  const fin = toMinutes(endTime)
  const duracion = Math.trunc(slotMinutes)

  // Una jornada mal cargada no debe colgar el bucle ni ofrecer horarios raros:
  // se prefiere una lista vacÃ­a, que la interfaz muestra como "sin horarios".
  if (inicio === null || fin === null) return []
  if (duracion <= 0 || duracion > 12 * 60) return []
  if (fin <= inicio) return []

  const corteInicio = breakStart ? toMinutes(breakStart) : null
  const corteFin = breakEnd ? toMinutes(breakEnd) : null
  const hayCorte =
    corteInicio !== null && corteFin !== null && corteFin > corteInicio

  const horarios: string[] = []
  for (let minuto = inicio; minuto + duracion <= fin; minuto += duracion) {
    const seSuperponeConElCorte =
      hayCorte && minuto < (corteFin as number) && minuto + duracion > (corteInicio as number)

    if (!seSuperponeConElCorte) horarios.push(toTimeString(minuto))
  }

  return horarios
}

function daysFromToday(delta: number): string {
  const date = new Date()
  date.setDate(date.getDate() + delta)
  return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10)
}

const today = daysFromToday(0)

===== .\src\lib\specialtyIcons.ts =====

import {
  Baby,
  Bone,
  Brain,
  Eye,
  HeartPulse,
  Sparkles,
  Stethoscope,
  type LucideIcon,
} from "lucide-react"

export {
  DEFAULT_SPECIALTY_ICON,
  specialtyIconOptions,
  type SpecialtyIconName,
} from "./specialtyIconNames"

/**
 * Shared by the admin grid, the booking stepper and the specialty form so the
 * catalogue offered when creating a specialty is exactly the one that can be
 * rendered afterwards.
 */
const specialtyIcons: Record<string, LucideIcon> = {
  Stethoscope,
  HeartPulse,
  Baby,
  Brain,
  Bone,
  Eye,
  Sparkles,
}

export function resolveSpecialtyIcon(icon: string): LucideIcon {
  return specialtyIcons[icon] ?? Stethoscope
}

===== .\src\lib\theme.ts =====

/**
 * LÃ³gica del tema claro/oscuro, sin React y sin dependencias.
 *
 * La preferencia se guarda en una **cookie**, no en `localStorage`.
 *
 * El motivo es concreto. Con `localStorage` solo el navegador sabe quÃ© tema
 * eligiÃ³ la persona, asÃ­ que el HTML que manda el servidor sale siempre en
 * claro y hay que corregirlo con un `<script>` antes del primer pintado para
 * que no haya destello. Ese script es justamente lo que React 19 no acepta
 * dentro del Ã¡rbol de componentes â€”avisa que un `<script>` renderizado del
 * lado del cliente nunca se ejecutaâ€” y el aviso salta igual desde un
 * componente de servidor, porque el layout tambiÃ©n se rerenderiza en el
 * cliente al navegar.
 *
 * Con una cookie, el servidor lee la preferencia antes de generar el HTML y
 * escribe la clase directamente en `<html>`. No hay script, no hay aviso, no
 * hay destello, y el modo "seguir al sistema" lo resuelve el CSS con
 * `prefers-color-scheme` sin que intervenga JavaScript en ningÃºn momento.
 */

export type Theme = "light" | "dark" | "system"
export type ResolvedTheme = "light" | "dark"

export const THEME_COOKIE = "theme"

/** Un aÃ±o. La preferencia visual no tiene por quÃ© caducar antes. */
export const THEME_COOKIE_MAX_AGE = 60 * 60 * 24 * 365

export const THEMES: readonly Theme[] = ["light", "dark", "system"]

export const DARK_MEDIA_QUERY = "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)"

export function isTheme(value: unknown): value is Theme {
  return value === "light" || value === "dark" || value === "system"
}

/**
 * Interpreta el valor de la cookie. Cualquier cosa que no sea un tema vÃ¡lido
 * â€”una cookie editada a mano, un valor de una versiÃ³n anterior, `undefined`â€”
 * cae en el respaldo en lugar de romper la pÃ¡gina.
 */
export function normalizeTheme(
  stored: string | null | undefined,
  fallback: Theme = "system"
): Theme {
  return isTheme(stored) ? stored : fallback
}

/**
 * Clase que va en `<html>`.
 *
 * "system" no lleva ninguna: la ausencia de clase es lo que habilita la rama
 * `prefers-color-scheme` del variante `dark` en `globals.css`. Y "light" sÃ­
 * lleva clase, aunque parezca redundante, porque es lo que bloquea esa rama
 * cuando alguien eligiÃ³ claro teniendo el sistema en oscuro.
 */
export function themeClassName(theme: Theme): "" | "light" | "dark" {
  return theme === "system" ? "" : theme
}

/** Traduce la preferencia a lo que realmente se ve. Solo lo necesita el
 *  cliente, para componentes como sonner que piden un tema concreto. */
export function resolveTheme(
  theme: Theme,
  systemPrefersDark: boolean
): ResolvedTheme {
  if (theme === "system") return systemPrefersDark ? "dark" : "light"
  return theme
}

/** El orden del botÃ³n que alterna: nunca vuelve a "system" solo. */
export function nextTheme(resolved: ResolvedTheme): Theme {
  return resolved === "dark" ? "light" : "dark"
}

/**
 * Aplica el tema al documento, para que el cambio se vea al instante sin
 * esperar a que el servidor devuelva la prÃ³xima navegaciÃ³n.
 *
 * Es la Ãºnica funciÃ³n que toca el DOM.
 */
export function applyTheme(root: HTMLElement, theme: Theme): void {
  root.classList.toggle("dark", theme === "dark")
  root.classList.toggle("light", theme === "light")
}

/** DuraciÃ³n de la transiciÃ³n al cambiar de tema. Tiene que coincidir con la
 *  de `.theme-transition` en globals.css; `test/theme-css.test.ts` lo verifica. */
export const THEME_TRANSITION_MS = 320

/**
 * Enciende la transiciÃ³n de colores mientras dura el cambio de tema.
 *
 * La clase se pone antes de cambiar las variables y se saca cuando la
 * animaciÃ³n terminÃ³. Se hace asÃ­, y no con una transiciÃ³n permanente sobre
 * `*`, porque eso obligarÃ­a al navegador a recalcular estilos en cada hover
 * de cada elemento de la pÃ¡gina.
 *
 * Devuelve la funciÃ³n que la apaga, para poder cancelarla si alguien vuelve a
 * cambiar el tema antes de que termine: si no, el temporizador viejo apagarÃ­a
 * la transiciÃ³n nueva a mitad de camino.
 */
export function startThemeTransition(
  root: HTMLElement,
  schedule: (fn: () => void, ms: number) => unknown = setTimeout
): () => void {
  root.classList.add("theme-transition")

  // Fuerza al navegador a resolver los estilos AHORA, con la transiciÃ³n ya
  // declarada y los colores todavÃ­a viejos. Sin esto, poner la clase de
  // transiciÃ³n y la del tema en el mismo bloque sÃ­ncrono deja las dos en el
  // mismo recÃ¡lculo: el navegador no ve un valor "de partida" y salta al
  // color nuevo sin animar. Se comprobÃ³ en Chromium: sin el reflow el cambio
  // era instantÃ¡neo unas veces y animado otras.
  void root.offsetHeight

  const timer = schedule(() => {
    root.classList.remove("theme-transition")
  }, THEME_TRANSITION_MS)

  return () => {
    clearTimeout(timer as ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>)
    root.classList.remove("theme-transition")
  }
}

/**
 * Cookie serializada. `SameSite=Lax` alcanza â€”no es un dato sensible y no se
 * usa para nada entre sitiosâ€” y no lleva `HttpOnly` a propÃ³sito: la escribe
 * el cliente cuando se toca el botÃ³n.
 */
export function themeCookie(theme: Theme): string {
  return `${THEME_COOKIE}=${theme}; path=/; max-age=${THEME_COOKIE_MAX_AGE}; samesite=lax`
}

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx"
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge"

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs))
}

===== .\src\models\Appointment.ts =====

import { appointments } from "@/lib/db/schema"

export type AppointmentRow = typeof appointments.$inferSelect

export { appointments as appointmentTable }

export type AppointmentStatus =
  | "scheduled"
  | "completed"
  | "cancelled"
  | "absent"

export interface AppointmentDto {
  id: string
  patientId: string
  patientName: string
  doctorId: string
  doctorName: string
  specialtyName: string
  branchName: string
  office: string
  date: string
  time: string
  status: AppointmentStatus
  reason?: string
  notes?: string
}

export interface AppointmentResult {
  ok: boolean
  error?: string
  appointment?: AppointmentDto
}

export interface AppointmentInput {
  doctorId: string
  date: string
  time: string
  reason?: string
}

/**
 * Es un objeto y no una lista de parÃ¡metros posicionales: la entidad ya tiene
 * catorce campos y varios son strings seguidos, donde un desorden no lo detecta
 * el compilador.
 */
export interface AppointmentAttributes {
  id: string
  patientId: string
  patientName: string
  doctorId: string
  doctorName: string
  specialtyName: string
  /** Sede y consultorio copiados del mÃ©dico al momento de reservar. */
  branchId?: string | null
  branchName?: string
  office?: string
  date: string
  time: string
  status?: AppointmentStatus
  reason?: string | null
  notes?: string | null
}

export class Appointment {
  id: string
  patientId: string
  patientName: string
  doctorId: string
  doctorName: string
  specialtyName: string
  branchId: string | null
  branchName: string
  office: string
  date: string
  time: string
  status: AppointmentStatus
  reason: string | null
  notes: string | null

  constructor(attributes: AppointmentAttributes) {
    this.id = attributes.id
    this.patientId = attributes.patientId
    this.patientName = attributes.patientName
    this.doctorId = attributes.doctorId
    this.doctorName = attributes.doctorName
    this.specialtyName = attributes.specialtyName
    this.branchId = attributes.branchId ?? null
    this.branchName = attributes.branchName ?? ""
    this.office = attributes.office ?? ""
    this.date = attributes.date
    this.time = attributes.time
    this.status = attributes.status ?? "scheduled"
    this.reason = attributes.reason ?? null
    this.notes = attributes.notes ?? null
  }

  static fromRow(row: AppointmentRow): Appointment {
    return new Appointment({
      id: row.id,
      patientId: row.patientId,
      patientName: row.patientName,
      doctorId: row.doctorId,
      doctorName: row.doctorName,
      specialtyName: row.specialtyName,
      branchId: row.branchId,
      branchName: row.branchName,
      office: row.office,
      date: row.date,
      time: row.time,
      status: row.status as AppointmentStatus,
      reason: row.reason,
      notes: row.notes,
    })
  }
}

export function toAppointmentDto(appointment: Appointment): AppointmentDto {
  return {
    id: appointment.id,
    patientId: appointment.patientId,
    patientName: appointment.patientName,
    doctorId: appointment.doctorId,
    doctorName: appointment.doctorName,
    specialtyName: appointment.specialtyName,
    branchName: appointment.branchName,
    office: appointment.office,
    date: appointment.date,
    time: appointment.time,
    status: appointment.status,
    reason: appointment.reason ?? undefined,
    notes: appointment.notes ?? undefined,
  }
}

===== .\src\models\Branch.ts =====

import { branches } from "@/lib/db/schema"

export type BranchRow = typeof branches.$inferSelect

export { branches as branchTable }

export interface BranchDto {
  id: string
  name: string
  address: string
  phone: string
  active: boolean
  /** CuÃ¡ntos profesionales atienden acÃ¡. Se calcula al leer. */
  doctorCount: number
}

export interface BranchInput {
  name: string
  address: string
  phone: string
  active: boolean
}

/** Payload del formulario de administraciÃ³n. */
export type BranchFormInput = Partial<BranchInput> & { id?: string }

export class Branch {
  id: string
  name: string
  address: string
  phone: string
  active: boolean
  /** Transitorio: no es una columna, se resuelve al consultar. */
  doctorCount: number

  constructor(
    id: string,
    name: string,
    address: string = "",
    phone: string = "",
    active: boolean = true,
    doctorCount: number = 0
  ) {
    this.id = id
    this.name = name
    this.address = address
    this.phone = phone
    this.active = active
    this.doctorCount = doctorCount
  }

  static fromRow(row: BranchRow, doctorCount = 0): Branch {
    return new Branch(
      row.id,
      row.name,
      row.address,
      row.phone,
      row.active,
      doctorCount
    )
  }
}

export function toBranchDto(branch: Branch): BranchDto {
  return {
    id: branch.id,
    name: branch.name,
    address: branch.address,
    phone: branch.phone,
    active: branch.active,
    doctorCount: branch.doctorCount,
  }
}

===== .\src\models\Medic.ts =====

import { doctors } from "@/lib/db/schema"
import { DEFAULT_SCHEDULE, type Schedule } from "@/lib/bookingSlots"

export type MedicRow = typeof doctors.$inferSelect

export { doctors as medicTable }

export interface MedicDto {
  id: string
  firstName: string
  lastName: string
  displayName: string
  email: string
  phone: string
  specialtyId: string
  specialtyName: string
  branchId: string
  branchName: string
  office: string
  avatar: string
  license: string
  /** Promedio de reseÃ±as. Solo tiene sentido si reviewCount > 0. */
  rating: number
  reviewCount: number
  yearsExperience: number
  workDays: number[]
  startTime: string
  endTime: string
  breakStart: string
  breakEnd: string
  slotMinutes: number
}

/**
 * Lo que el formulario de administraciÃ³n puede cargar.
 *
 * `rating` y `reviewCount` quedan afuera a propÃ³sito: son el resultado de las
 * reseÃ±as de los pacientes, no un dato que alguien escriba a mano.
 */
export interface MedicInput {
  firstName: string
  lastName: string
  email: string
  phone: string
  specialtyId: string
  specialtyName: string
  branchId: string
  office: string
  avatar: string
  license: string
  yearsExperience: number
  workDays: number[]
  startTime: string
  endTime: string
  breakStart: string
  breakEnd: string
  slotMinutes: number
}

/** Admin form payload: absent fields fall back to the validation defaults. */
export type MedicFormInput = Partial<MedicInput> & { id?: string }

/**
 * Atributos con los que se construye un Medic.
 *
 * Es un objeto y no una lista de parÃ¡metros posicionales porque la entidad ya
 * tiene quince campos: con posicionales, agregar uno en el medio desalinea en
 * silencio todos los que siguen.
 */
export interface MedicAttributes {
  id: string
  firstName: string
  lastName: string
  email: string
  phone?: string
  specialtyId?: string
  specialtyName?: string
  branchId?: string | null
  branchName?: string
  office?: string
  avatar?: string
  license?: string
  rating?: number
  reviewCount?: number
  yearsExperience?: number
  workDays?: number[]
  startTime?: string
  endTime?: string
  breakStart?: string
  breakEnd?: string
  slotMinutes?: number
  userId?: string | null
}

// The Spanish honorific is gendered but the schema only stores the given name,
// so it is inferred from the most common feminine names in the catalog.
const feminineFirstNames = new Set([
  "marÃ­a",
  "lucÃ­a",
  "ana",
  "sofÃ­a",
  "valentina",
  "laura",
  "carla",
  "julia",
  "paula",
  "marta",
])

export class Medic {
  id: string
  firstName: string
  lastName: string
  email: string
  phone: string
  specialtyId: string
  specialtyName: string
  branchId: string | null
  /** Transitorio: no es una columna, se resuelve al consultar la sede. */
  branchName: string
  office: string
  avatar: string
  license: string
  rating: number
  reviewCount: number
  yearsExperience: number
  workDays: number[]
  startTime: string
  endTime: string
  breakStart: string
  breakEnd: string
  slotMinutes: number
  userId?: string | null

  constructor(attributes: MedicAttributes) {
    this.id = attributes.id
    this.firstName = attributes.firstName
    this.lastName = attributes.lastName
    this.email = attributes.email
    this.phone = attributes.phone ?? ""
    this.specialtyId = attributes.specialtyId ?? ""
    this.specialtyName = attributes.specialtyName ?? ""
    this.branchId = attributes.branchId ?? null
    this.branchName = attributes.branchName ?? ""
    this.office = attributes.office ?? ""
    this.avatar = attributes.avatar ?? ""
    this.license = attributes.license ?? ""
    this.rating = attributes.rating ?? 0
    this.reviewCount = attributes.reviewCount ?? 0
    this.yearsExperience = attributes.yearsExperience ?? 1
    this.workDays = attributes.workDays ?? [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
    this.startTime = attributes.startTime ?? DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.startTime
    this.endTime = attributes.endTime ?? DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.endTime
    this.breakStart = attributes.breakStart ?? DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.breakStart
    this.breakEnd = attributes.breakEnd ?? DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.breakEnd
    this.slotMinutes = attributes.slotMinutes ?? DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.slotMinutes
    this.userId = attributes.userId ?? null
  }

  get title(): string {
    return feminineFirstNames.has(this.firstName.trim().toLowerCase())
      ? "Dra."
      : "Dr."
  }

  get displayName(): string {
    return `${this.title} ${this.firstName} ${this.lastName}`
  }

  static fromRow(row: MedicRow, branchName = ""): Medic {
    return new Medic({
      id: row.id,
      firstName: row.firstName,
      lastName: row.lastName,
      email: row.email,
      phone: row.phone,
      specialtyId: row.specialtyId,
      specialtyName: row.specialtyName,
      branchId: row.branchId,
      branchName,
      office: row.office,
      avatar: row.avatar,
      license: row.license,
      rating: row.rating,
      reviewCount: row.reviewCount,
      yearsExperience: row.yearsExperience,
      workDays: row.workDays
        .split(",")
        .map((day) => Number.parseInt(day, 10))
        .filter((day) => !Number.isNaN(day)),
      startTime: row.startTime,
      endTime: row.endTime,
      breakStart: row.breakStart,
      breakEnd: row.breakEnd,
      slotMinutes: row.slotMinutes,
      userId: row.userId,
    })
  }
}

export function toMedicDto(medic: Medic): MedicDto {
  return {
    id: medic.id,
    firstName: medic.firstName,
    lastName: medic.lastName,
    displayName: medic.displayName,
    email: medic.email,
    phone: medic.phone,
    specialtyId: medic.specialtyId,
    specialtyName: medic.specialtyName,
    branchId: medic.branchId ?? "",
    branchName: medic.branchName,
    office: medic.office,
    avatar: medic.avatar,
    license: medic.license,
    rating: medic.rating,
    reviewCount: medic.reviewCount,
    yearsExperience: medic.yearsExperience,
    workDays: medic.workDays,
    startTime: medic.startTime,
    endTime: medic.endTime,
    breakStart: medic.breakStart,
    breakEnd: medic.breakEnd,
    slotMinutes: medic.slotMinutes,
  }
}

/** La jornada del profesional, lista para generar sus horarios. */
export function medicSchedule(medic: MedicDto): Schedule {
  return {
    startTime: medic.startTime,
    endTime: medic.endTime,
    breakStart: medic.breakStart,
    breakEnd: medic.breakEnd,
    slotMinutes: medic.slotMinutes,
  }
}

===== .\src\models\Notification.ts =====

import { notifications } from "@/lib/db/schema"

export type NotificationRow = typeof notifications.$inferSelect

export { notifications as notificationTable }

export type NotificationType =
  | "appointment_booked"
  | "appointment_cancelled"
  | "appointment_rescheduled"
  | "appointment_reminder"
  | "appointment_status"
  | "review_received"

export interface NotificationDto {
  id: string
  type: NotificationType
  title: string
  body: string
  read: boolean
  /** ISO con fecha y hora: la campanita muestra "hace 2 h". */
  createdAt: string
}

export class Notification {
  id: string
  userId: string
  type: NotificationType
  title: string
  body: string
  /**
   * Identifica el hecho que originÃ³ el aviso. El Ã­ndice Ãºnico sobre
   * (userId, dedupeKey) es lo que hace que el generador de recordatorios
   * pueda correr mil veces sin duplicar nada.
   */
  dedupeKey: string | null
  readAt: Date | null
  createdAt: Date

  constructor(
    id: string,
    userId: string,
    type: NotificationType,
    title: string,
    body: string = "",
    dedupeKey: string | null = null,
    readAt: Date | null = null,
    createdAt: Date = new Date()
  ) {
    this.id = id
    this.userId = userId
    this.type = type
    this.title = title
    this.body = body
    this.dedupeKey = dedupeKey
    this.readAt = readAt
    this.createdAt = createdAt
  }

  static fromRow(row: NotificationRow): Notification {
    return new Notification(
      row.id,
      row.userId,
      row.type as NotificationType,
      row.title,
      row.body,
      row.dedupeKey,
      row.readAt,
      row.createdAt
    )
  }
}

export function toNotificationDto(item: Notification): NotificationDto {
  return {
    id: item.id,
    type: item.type,
    title: item.title,
    body: item.body,
    read: item.readAt !== null,
    createdAt: item.createdAt.toISOString(),
  }
}

import { reviews } from "@/lib/db/schema"

  score: number

export interface RatingSummary {
  average: number
  total: number

export class Review {
  id: string
  appointmentId: string
  doctorId: string
  patientId: string

  createdAt: Date

  constructor(
    id: string,
    appointmentId: string,

  ) {

/**
 * ReseÃ±a tal como se muestra a cualquiera, SIN identidad del paciente.
 *
 * En una aplicaciÃ³n de salud, publicar "Juan PÃ©rez calificÃ³ al Dr. X" revela
 * que Juan es paciente del Dr. X, y eso es informaciÃ³n mÃ©dica sobre una
 * persona concreta. La opiniÃ³n es Ãºtil; quiÃ©n la escribiÃ³ no hace falta.
 */
export interface PublicReviewDto {
  id: string
  score: number
  comment?: string
  createdAt: string

export function toPublicReviewDto(review: Review): PublicReviewDto {

    comment: review.comment ?? undefined,
    createdAt: review.createdAt.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  }
}

    id: review.id,
    appointmentId: review.appointmentId,
    doctorId: review.doctorId,
    patientId: review.patientId,
    score: review.score,
    comment: review.comment ?? undefined,

import { user } from "@/lib/db/schema"

  id: string
  name: string
  email: string
  role: Role
  image?: string | null
}

/** Shape sent to the admin panel. Never carries credentials. */
export interface UserDto {
  id: string
  name: string
  email: string
  role: Role
  emailVerified: boolean
  createdAt: string
  /** True for the account running the current session, which cannot self-delete. */
  isCurrent: boolean
}

  name: string
  email: string
  role: Role
}

  return {
    id: row.id,
    name: row.name,
    email: row.email,
    role: (row.role as Role) ?? "patient",
    emailVerified: row.emailVerified,
    createdAt: row.createdAt.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
    isCurrent: row.id === currentUserId,
  }
}

===== .\src\repository\AppointmentRepository.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { and, asc, desc, eq, ne } from "drizzle-orm"
import { db } from "@/lib/db"
import {
  Appointment,
  appointmentTable,
  type AppointmentStatus,
} from "@/models/Appointment"
import type { Repository } from "./BaseRepository"

export class AppointmentRepository implements Repository<Appointment> {
  async findAll(): Promise<Appointment[]> {
    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(appointmentTable)
      .orderBy(desc(appointmentTable.date), asc(appointmentTable.time))
    return rows.map(Appointment.fromRow)
  }

  async findOne(id: string): Promise<Appointment | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(appointmentTable)
      .where(eq(appointmentTable.id, id))
      .limit(1)
    return row ? Appointment.fromRow(row) : undefined
  }

  async findByDoctorId(doctorId: string): Promise<Appointment[]> {
    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(appointmentTable)
      .where(eq(appointmentTable.doctorId, doctorId))
      .orderBy(asc(appointmentTable.time))
    return rows.map(Appointment.fromRow)
  }

  async findByPatientId(patientId: string): Promise<Appointment[]> {
    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(appointmentTable)
      .where(eq(appointmentTable.patientId, patientId))
      .orderBy(desc(appointmentTable.date), asc(appointmentTable.time))
    return rows.map(Appointment.fromRow)
  }

  async findByDoctorAndDate(
    doctorId: string,
    date: string
  ): Promise<Appointment[]> {
    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(appointmentTable)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(appointmentTable.doctorId, doctorId),
          eq(appointmentTable.date, date)
        )
      )
    return rows.map(Appointment.fromRow)
  }

  /** Turnos agendados para una fecha. Base del recordatorio del dÃ­a previo. */
  async findScheduledOn(date: string): Promise<Appointment[]> {
    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(appointmentTable)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(appointmentTable.date, date),
          eq(appointmentTable.status, "scheduled")
        )
      )
      .orderBy(asc(appointmentTable.time))
    return rows.map(Appointment.fromRow)
  }

  /**
   * `excludeId` deja fuera al propio turno: al reprogramar, el registro que se
   * estÃ¡ moviendo no puede contarse como su propio conflicto.
   */
  async findClash(
    doctorId: string,
    date: string,
    time: string,
    excludeId?: string
  ): Promise<Appointment | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(appointmentTable)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(appointmentTable.doctorId, doctorId),
          eq(appointmentTable.date, date),
          eq(appointmentTable.time, time),
          // Un turno cancelado libera el horario, igual que en el Ã­ndice Ãºnico.
          ne(appointmentTable.status, "cancelled"),
          ...(excludeId ? [ne(appointmentTable.id, excludeId)] : [])
        )
      )
      .limit(1)
    return row ? Appointment.fromRow(row) : undefined
  }

  /**
   * Una persona no puede estar en dos consultas a la vez.
   *
   * Faltaba: habÃ­a guarda para la agenda del mÃ©dico y para el consultorio,
   * pero ninguna para el paciente, asÃ­ que se podÃ­a reservar cardiologÃ­a y
   * fonoaudiologÃ­a el mismo dÃ­a a las 08:00 con dos profesionales distintos.
   * Los dos turnos eran vÃ¡lidos por separado y ninguno de los dos chequeos
   * los veÃ­a.
   */
  async findPatientClash(
    patientId: string,
    date: string,
    time: string,
    excludeId?: string
  ): Promise<Appointment | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(appointmentTable)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(appointmentTable.patientId, patientId),
          eq(appointmentTable.date, date),
          eq(appointmentTable.time, time),
          ne(appointmentTable.status, "cancelled"),
          ...(excludeId ? [ne(appointmentTable.id, excludeId)] : [])
        )
      )
      .limit(1)
    return row ? Appointment.fromRow(row) : undefined
  }

  /** Horarios que el paciente ya tiene ocupados ese dÃ­a, con cualquier mÃ©dico. */
  async findByPatientAndDate(
    patientId: string,
    date: string
  ): Promise<Appointment[]> {
    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(appointmentTable)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(appointmentTable.patientId, patientId),
          eq(appointmentTable.date, date),
          ne(appointmentTable.status, "cancelled")
        )
      )
    return rows.map((row) => Appointment.fromRow(row))
  }

  /**
   * Un consultorio es un recurso fÃ­sico compartido: dos profesionales distintos
   * no pueden estar atendiendo en la misma sala, sede, dÃ­a y hora.
   */
  async findRoomClash(
    branchId: string,
    office: string,
    date: string,
    time: string,
    excludeId?: string
  ): Promise<Appointment | undefined> {
    if (!branchId || !office) return undefined

    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(appointmentTable)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(appointmentTable.branchId, branchId),
          eq(appointmentTable.office, office),
          eq(appointmentTable.date, date),
          eq(appointmentTable.time, time),
          ne(appointmentTable.status, "cancelled"),
          ...(excludeId ? [ne(appointmentTable.id, excludeId)] : [])
        )
      )
      .limit(1)
    return row ? Appointment.fromRow(row) : undefined
  }

  async create(item: Appointment): Promise<Appointment> {
    await db.insert(appointmentTable).values({
      id: item.id,
      patientId: item.patientId,
      patientName: item.patientName,
      doctorId: item.doctorId,
      doctorName: item.doctorName,
      specialtyName: item.specialtyName,
      branchId: item.branchId,
      branchName: item.branchName,
      office: item.office,
      date: item.date,
      time: item.time,
      status: item.status,
      reason: item.reason ?? null,
      notes: item.notes ?? null,
    })
    return item
  }

  async update(
    id: string,
    item: Partial<Appointment>
  ): Promise<Appointment | undefined> {
    await db
      .update(appointmentTable)
      .set(item)
      .where(eq(appointmentTable.id, id))
    return this.findOne(id)
  }

  async updateStatusScopedToPatient(
    id: string,
    patientId: string,
    status: AppointmentStatus
  ): Promise<void> {
    await db
      .update(appointmentTable)
      .set({ status })
      .where(
        and(
          eq(appointmentTable.id, id),
          eq(appointmentTable.patientId, patientId)
        )
      )
  }

  async delete(id: string): Promise<{ id: string } | undefined> {
    await db.delete(appointmentTable).where(eq(appointmentTable.id, id))
    return { id }
  }
}

===== .\src\repository\BaseRepository.ts =====

export interface Repository<T> {
  findAll(): Promise<T[]>
  findOne(id: string): Promise<T | undefined>
  create(item: T): Promise<T>
  update(id: string, item: Partial<T>): Promise<T | undefined>
  delete(id: string): Promise<{ id: string } | undefined>
}

===== .\src\repository\BranchRepository.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { and, asc, eq, ne, sql } from "drizzle-orm"
import { db } from "@/lib/db"
import { Branch, branchTable } from "@/models/Branch"
import { medicTable } from "@/models/Medic"
import type { Repository } from "./BaseRepository"

/** Subconsulta reutilizada: cuÃ¡ntos profesionales atienden en cada sede. */
const doctorCount = sql<number>`(
  SELECT count(*)::int FROM ${medicTable}
  WHERE ${medicTable.branchId} = ${branchTable.id}
)`

export class BranchRepository implements Repository<Branch> {
  async findAll(): Promise<Branch[]> {
    const rows = await db
      .select({ branch: branchTable, doctors: doctorCount })
      .from(branchTable)
      .orderBy(asc(branchTable.name))
    return rows.map((row) => Branch.fromRow(row.branch, row.doctors))
  }

  /** Solo las sedes que se pueden ofrecer al reservar un turno. */
  async findActive(): Promise<Branch[]> {
    const rows = await db
      .select({ branch: branchTable, doctors: doctorCount })
      .from(branchTable)
      .where(eq(branchTable.active, true))
      .orderBy(asc(branchTable.name))
    return rows.map((row) => Branch.fromRow(row.branch, row.doctors))
  }

  async findOne(id: string): Promise<Branch | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select({ branch: branchTable, doctors: doctorCount })
      .from(branchTable)
      .where(eq(branchTable.id, id))
      .limit(1)
    return row ? Branch.fromRow(row.branch, row.doctors) : undefined
  }

  async findByName(name: string): Promise<Branch | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(branchTable)
      .where(sql`lower(${branchTable.name}) = lower(${name})`)
      .limit(1)
    return row ? Branch.fromRow(row) : undefined
  }

  /** Evita chocar contra la restricciÃ³n Ãºnica con un mensaje entendible. */
  async findOtherByName(
    name: string,
    excludeId: string
  ): Promise<Branch | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(branchTable)
      .where(
        and(
          sql`lower(${branchTable.name}) = lower(${name})`,
          ne(branchTable.id, excludeId)
        )
      )
      .limit(1)
    return row ? Branch.fromRow(row) : undefined
  }

  async countDoctors(id: string): Promise<number> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select({ total: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
      .from(medicTable)
      .where(eq(medicTable.branchId, id))
    return row?.total ?? 0
  }

  async create(item: Branch): Promise<Branch> {
    await db.insert(branchTable).values({
      id: item.id,
      name: item.name,
      address: item.address,
      phone: item.phone,
      active: item.active,
    })
    return item
  }

  async createIfMissing(item: Branch): Promise<void> {
    await db
      .insert(branchTable)
      .values({
        id: item.id,
        name: item.name,
        address: item.address,
        phone: item.phone,
        active: item.active,
      })
      .onConflictDoNothing()
  }

  async update(id: string, item: Partial<Branch>): Promise<Branch | undefined> {
    // `doctorCount` es transitorio: no existe como columna.
    const { doctorCount: _ignored, ...columns } = item
    await db.update(branchTable).set(columns).where(eq(branchTable.id, id))
    return this.findOne(id)
  }

  async delete(id: string): Promise<{ id: string } | undefined> {
    await db.delete(branchTable).where(eq(branchTable.id, id))
    return { id }
  }
}

===== .\src\repository\MedicRepository.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { and, asc, eq, ne, sql } from "drizzle-orm"
import { db } from "@/lib/db"
import { branchTable } from "@/models/Branch"
import { Medic, medicTable } from "@/models/Medic"
import type { Repository } from "./BaseRepository"

/**
 * El nombre de la sede NO se copia a la fila del mÃ©dico: se resuelve con un
 * LEFT JOIN. `specialtyName` sÃ­ estÃ¡ desnormalizado y por eso hubo que propagar
 * a mano cada renombre de especialidad; no se repite ese error acÃ¡.
 */
function selectWithBranch() {
  return db
    .select({ medic: medicTable, branchName: branchTable.name })
    .from(medicTable)
    .leftJoin(branchTable, eq(medicTable.branchId, branchTable.id))
}

type JoinedRow = {
  medic: typeof medicTable.$inferSelect
  branchName: string | null
}

function toMedic(row: JoinedRow): Medic {
  return Medic.fromRow(row.medic, row.branchName ?? "")
}

export class MedicRepository implements Repository<Medic> {
  async findAll(): Promise<Medic[]> {
    const rows = await selectWithBranch().orderBy(asc(medicTable.lastName))
    return rows.map(toMedic)
  }

  async findOne(id: string): Promise<Medic | undefined> {
    const [row] = await selectWithBranch()
      .where(eq(medicTable.id, id))
      .limit(1)
    return row ? toMedic(row) : undefined
  }

  async findByUserId(userId: string): Promise<Medic | undefined> {
    const [row] = await selectWithBranch()
      .where(eq(medicTable.userId, userId))
      .limit(1)
    return row ? toMedic(row) : undefined
  }

  async findByEmail(email: string): Promise<Medic | undefined> {
    const [row] = await selectWithBranch()
      .where(sql`lower(${medicTable.email}) = lower(${email})`)
      .limit(1)
    return row ? toMedic(row) : undefined
  }

  /** Evita chocar contra la restricciÃ³n Ãºnica con un mensaje entendible. */
  async findOtherByEmail(
    email: string,
    excludeId: string
  ): Promise<Medic | undefined> {
    const [row] = await selectWithBranch()
      .where(
        and(
          sql`lower(${medicTable.email}) = lower(${email})`,
          ne(medicTable.id, excludeId)
        )
      )
      .limit(1)
    return row ? toMedic(row) : undefined
  }

  /**
   * `specialtyName` estÃ¡ desnormalizado en la fila del mÃ©dico, asÃ­ que renombrar
   * una especialidad hay que propagarlo o las tablas se contradicen.
   */
  async renameSpecialty(specialtyId: string, name: string): Promise<void> {
    await db
      .update(medicTable)
      .set({ specialtyName: name })
      .where(eq(medicTable.specialtyId, specialtyId))
  }

  async countBySpecialtyId(specialtyId: string): Promise<number> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select({ total: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
      .from(medicTable)
      .where(eq(medicTable.specialtyId, specialtyId))
    return row?.total ?? 0
  }

  /** Cachea el promedio de reseÃ±as para no recalcularlo en cada listado. */
  async setRating(id: string, average: number, total: number): Promise<void> {
    await db
      .update(medicTable)
      .set({ rating: average, reviewCount: total })
      .where(eq(medicTable.id, id))
  }

  async create(item: Medic): Promise<Medic> {
    await db.insert(medicTable).values(this.toColumns(item))
    return item
  }

  async createIfMissing(item: Medic): Promise<void> {
    await db.insert(medicTable).values(this.toColumns(item)).onConflictDoNothing()
  }

  async update(id: string, item: Partial<Medic>): Promise<Medic | undefined> {
    // `workDays` se guarda como texto separado por comas y `branchName` es
    // transitorio: ninguno de los dos se puede pasar tal cual al UPDATE.
    const { workDays, branchName: _ignored, ...rest } = item
    await db
      .update(medicTable)
      .set({ ...rest, ...(workDays ? { workDays: workDays.join(",") } : {}) })
      .where(eq(medicTable.id, id))
    return this.findOne(id)
  }

  async delete(id: string): Promise<{ id: string } | undefined> {
    await db.delete(medicTable).where(eq(medicTable.id, id))
    return { id }
  }

  private toColumns(item: Medic) {
    return {
      id: item.id,
      userId: item.userId ?? null,
      firstName: item.firstName,
      lastName: item.lastName,
      email: item.email,
      phone: item.phone,
      specialtyId: item.specialtyId,
      specialtyName: item.specialtyName,
      branchId: item.branchId,
      office: item.office,
      avatar: item.avatar,
      license: item.license,
      rating: item.rating,
      reviewCount: item.reviewCount,
      yearsExperience: item.yearsExperience,
      workDays: item.workDays.join(","),
      startTime: item.startTime,
      endTime: item.endTime,
      breakStart: item.breakStart,
      breakEnd: item.breakEnd,
      slotMinutes: item.slotMinutes,
    }
  }
}

===== .\src\repository\NotificationRepository.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { and, desc, eq, isNull, sql } from "drizzle-orm"
import { db } from "@/lib/db"
import { Notification, notificationTable } from "@/models/Notification"

/** Tope de la campanita: nadie baja mÃ¡s allÃ¡ de esto. */
const FEED_LIMIT = 30

export class NotificationRepository {
  async findByUserId(userId: string): Promise<Notification[]> {
    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(notificationTable)
      .where(eq(notificationTable.userId, userId))
      .orderBy(desc(notificationTable.createdAt))
      .limit(FEED_LIMIT)
    return rows.map(Notification.fromRow)
  }

  async countUnread(userId: string): Promise<number> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select({ total: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
      .from(notificationTable)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(notificationTable.userId, userId),
          isNull(notificationTable.readAt)
        )
      )
    return row?.total ?? 0
  }

  /**
   * Inserta ignorando los repetidos.
   *
   * El Ã­ndice Ãºnico sobre (userId, dedupeKey) convierte esto en una operaciÃ³n
   * segura de reintentar: el generador de recordatorios corre en cada visita y
   * solo el primer intento deja una fila.
   */
  async createIfMissing(item: Notification): Promise<boolean> {
    const inserted = await db
      .insert(notificationTable)
      .values({
        id: item.id,
        userId: item.userId,
        type: item.type,
        title: item.title,
        body: item.body,
        dedupeKey: item.dedupeKey,
      })
      .onConflictDoNothing()
      .returning({ id: notificationTable.id })

    return inserted.length > 0
  }

  async markAllRead(userId: string): Promise<void> {
    await db
      .update(notificationTable)
      .set({ readAt: new Date() })
      .where(
        and(
          eq(notificationTable.userId, userId),
          isNull(notificationTable.readAt)
        )
      )
  }

  async markRead(userId: string, id: string): Promise<void> {
    await db
      .update(notificationTable)
      .set({ readAt: new Date() })
      .where(
        and(
          eq(notificationTable.userId, userId),
          eq(notificationTable.id, id)
        )
      )
  }
}

import "server-only"
import { and, asc, eq, ne, sql } from "drizzle-orm"
import { db } from "@/lib/db"
import { Patient, patientTable } from "@/models/Patient"
import type { Repository } from "./BaseRepository"

export class PatientRepository implements Repository<Patient> {
  async findAll(): Promise<Patient[]> {
    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(patientTable)
      .orderBy(asc(patientTable.lastName))
    return rows.map(Patient.fromRow)
  }

  async findByEmail(email: string): Promise<Patient | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(patientTable)
      .where(sql`lower(${patientTable.email}) = lower(${email})`)
      .limit(1)
    return row ? Patient.fromRow(row) : undefined
  }

  /** Used to reject a duplicate email without tripping the DB constraint. */
  async findOtherByEmail(
    email: string,
    excludeId: string
  ): Promise<Patient | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(patientTable)
      .where(
        and(
          sql`lower(${patientTable.email}) = lower(${email})`,
          ne(patientTable.id, excludeId)
        )
      )
      .limit(1)
    return row ? Patient.fromRow(row) : undefined
  }

  async findOne(id: string): Promise<Patient | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(patientTable)
      .where(eq(patientTable.id, id))
      .limit(1)
    return row ? Patient.fromRow(row) : undefined
  }

  async findByUserId(userId: string): Promise<Patient | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(patientTable)
      .where(eq(patientTable.userId, userId))
      .limit(1)
    return row ? Patient.fromRow(row) : undefined
  }

  async create(item: Patient): Promise<Patient> {
    await db.insert(patientTable).values({
      id: item.id,
      userId: item.userId ?? null,
      firstName: item.firstName,
      lastName: item.lastName,
      email: item.email,
      phone: item.phone,
      dni: item.dni,
      birthDate: item.birthDate,
      bloodType: item.bloodType ?? null,
      createdAt: item.createdAt,
    })
    return item
  }

  async update(
    id: string,
    item: Partial<Patient>
  ): Promise<Patient | undefined> {
    await db.update(patientTable).set(item).where(eq(patientTable.id, id))
    return this.findOne(id)
  }

  async delete(id: string): Promise<{ id: string } | undefined> {
    await db.delete(patientTable).where(eq(patientTable.id, id))
    return { id }
  }
}

===== .\src\repository\ReviewRepository.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { desc, eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm"
import { db } from "@/lib/db"
import { Review, reviewTable, type RatingSummary } from "@/models/Review"

export class ReviewRepository {
  async findByDoctorId(doctorId: string): Promise<Review[]> {
    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(reviewTable)
      .where(eq(reviewTable.doctorId, doctorId))
      .orderBy(desc(reviewTable.createdAt))
    return rows.map(Review.fromRow)
  }

  async findByAppointmentId(
    appointmentId: string
  ): Promise<Review | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(reviewTable)
      .where(eq(reviewTable.appointmentId, appointmentId))
      .limit(1)
    return row ? Review.fromRow(row) : undefined
  }

  /** Ids de los turnos del paciente que ya tienen reseÃ±a. */
  async findAppointmentIdsByPatient(patientId: string): Promise<string[]> {
    const rows = await db
      .select({ appointmentId: reviewTable.appointmentId })
      .from(reviewTable)
      .where(eq(reviewTable.patientId, patientId))
    return rows.map((row) => row.appointmentId)
  }

  /**
   * Promedio y cantidad de un profesional. Es la Ãºnica fuente de verdad de las
   * estrellas: `doctors.rating` es una copia cacheada de este cÃ¡lculo.
   */
  async summaryForDoctor(doctorId: string): Promise<RatingSummary> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select({
        average: sql<number>`COALESCE(avg(${reviewTable.score}), 0)::float8`,
        total: sql<number>`count(*)::int`,
      })
      .from(reviewTable)
      .where(eq(reviewTable.doctorId, doctorId))

    return { average: row?.average ?? 0, total: row?.total ?? 0 }
  }

  async create(item: Review): Promise<Review> {
    await db.insert(reviewTable).values({
      id: item.id,
      appointmentId: item.appointmentId,
      doctorId: item.doctorId,
      patientId: item.patientId,
      score: item.score,
      comment: item.comment,
    })
    return item
  }
}

import "server-only"
import { and, asc, eq, ne, sql } from "drizzle-orm"
import { db } from "@/lib/db"
import { Specialty, specialtyTable } from "@/models/Specialty"
import type { Repository } from "./BaseRepository"

export class SpecialtyRepository implements Repository<Specialty> {
  async findAll(): Promise<Specialty[]> {
    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(specialtyTable)
      .orderBy(asc(specialtyTable.name))
    return rows.map(Specialty.fromRow)
  }

  async findByName(name: string): Promise<Specialty | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(specialtyTable)
      .where(sql`lower(${specialtyTable.name}) = lower(${name})`)
      .limit(1)
    return row ? Specialty.fromRow(row) : undefined
  }

  /** Used to reject a duplicate name without tripping the DB constraint. */
  async findOtherByName(
    name: string,
    excludeId: string
  ): Promise<Specialty | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(specialtyTable)
      .where(
        and(
          sql`lower(${specialtyTable.name}) = lower(${name})`,
          ne(specialtyTable.id, excludeId)
        )
      )
      .limit(1)
    return row ? Specialty.fromRow(row) : undefined
  }

  /** Keeps the "N mÃ©dicos" badge in the admin panel honest. */
  async setActiveDoctors(id: string, total: number): Promise<void> {
    await db
      .update(specialtyTable)
      .set({ activeDoctors: total })
      .where(eq(specialtyTable.id, id))
  }

  async findOne(id: string): Promise<Specialty | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(specialtyTable)
      .where(eq(specialtyTable.id, id))
      .limit(1)
    return row ? Specialty.fromRow(row) : undefined
  }

  async create(item: Specialty): Promise<Specialty> {
    await db.insert(specialtyTable).values({
      id: item.id,
      name: item.name,
      description: item.description,
      icon: item.icon,
      activeDoctors: item.activeDoctors,
    })
    return item
  }

  async createIfMissing(item: Specialty): Promise<void> {
    await db
      .insert(specialtyTable)
      .values({
        id: item.id,
        name: item.name,
        description: item.description,
        icon: item.icon,
        activeDoctors: item.activeDoctors,
      })
      .onConflictDoNothing()
  }

  async update(
    id: string,

  ): Promise<Specialty | undefined> {

    return this.findOne(id)
  }

    await db.delete(specialtyTable).where(eq(specialtyTable.id, id))
    return { id }
  }
}

===== .\src\repository\UserRepository.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { and, asc, eq, ne, sql } from "drizzle-orm"
import { db } from "@/lib/db"
import { userTable, type UserRow } from "@/models/User"

/**
 * Does not implement Repository<T>: `user` is Better Auth's authentication
 * table, so rows are created through `auth.api.signUpEmail` (which owns
 * password hashing) rather than with a plain insert here.
 */
export class UserRepository {
  async findAll(): Promise<UserRow[]> {
    return db.select().from(userTable).orderBy(asc(userTable.name))
  }

  async findById(id: string): Promise<UserRow | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(userTable)
      .where(eq(userTable.id, id))
      .limit(1)
    return row
  }

  async findByEmail(email: string): Promise<UserRow | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(userTable)
      .where(sql`lower(${userTable.email}) = lower(${email})`)
      .limit(1)
    return row
  }

  /** Used to reject a duplicate email without tripping the DB constraint. */
  async findOtherByEmail(
    email: string,
    excludeId: string
  ): Promise<UserRow | undefined> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select()
      .from(userTable)
      .where(
        and(
          sql`lower(${userTable.email}) = lower(${email})`,
          ne(userTable.id, excludeId)
        )
      )
      .limit(1)
    return row
  }

  async countByRole(role: string): Promise<number> {
    const [row] = await db
      .select({ total: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
      .from(userTable)
      .where(eq(userTable.role, role))
    return row?.total ?? 0
  }

  async updateRole(id: string, role: string): Promise<void> {
    await db
      .update(userTable)
      .set({ role, updatedAt: new Date() })
      .where(eq(userTable.id, id))
  }

  async updateProfile(
    id: string,
    attributes: { name: string; email: string; role: string }
  ): Promise<UserRow | undefined> {
    await db
      .update(userTable)
      .set({ ...attributes, updatedAt: new Date() })
      .where(eq(userTable.id, id))
    return this.findById(id)
  }

  /**
   * `session` and `account` cascade from the FK, and `doctors.userId` /
   * `patients.userId` are set to NULL, so the clinical record outlives the
   * login it was attached to.
   */
  async delete(id: string): Promise<{ id: string } | undefined> {
    await db.delete(userTable).where(eq(userTable.id, id))
    return { id }
  }
}

"use server"

import * as branchController from "@/controllers/BranchController"
import { runAction, type ActionResult } from "@/lib/actionResult"
import type { BranchDto, BranchFormInput } from "@/models/Branch"

export async function getBranches(): Promise<BranchDto[]> {
  return branchController.findAll()
}

export async function getActiveBranches(): Promise<BranchDto[]> {
  return branchController.findActive()
}

export async function saveBranch(
  input: BranchFormInput
): Promise<ActionResult<BranchDto | undefined>> {
  const { id, ...attributes } = input
  return runAction(() =>
    id
      ? branchController.update(id, attributes)
      : branchController.create(attributes)
  )
}

export async function deleteBranch(
  id: string
): Promise<ActionResult<{ id: string } | undefined>> {
  return runAction(() => branchController.remove(id))
}

  return notificationController.markRead(id)
}
