===== .\src\services\AppointmentService.ts =====

import "server-only"
import {
  Appointment,
  type AppointmentInput,
  type AppointmentStatus,
} from "@/models/Appointment"
import type { SessionUser } from "@/models/User"
import type { AppointmentRepository } from "@/repository/AppointmentRepository"
import type { MedicRepository } from "@/repository/MedicRepository"
import type { NotificationService } from "./NotificationService"
import type { PatientService } from "./PatientService"

export interface CreateAppointmentResult {
  ok: boolean
  error?: string
  appointment?: Appointment
}

/** PostgreSQL `unique_violation`. */
function isUniqueViolation(error: unknown): boolean {
  return (
    typeof error === "object" &&
    error !== null &&
    "code" in error &&
    (error as { code?: unknown }).code === "23505"
  )
}

export class AppointmentService {
  private repository: AppointmentRepository
  private medicRepository: MedicRepository
  private patientService: PatientService
  private notificationService: NotificationService

  constructor(
    repository: AppointmentRepository,
    medicRepository: MedicRepository,
    patientService: PatientService,
    notificationService: NotificationService
  ) {
    this.repository = repository
    this.medicRepository = medicRepository
    this.patientService = patientService
    this.notificationService = notificationService
  }

  /**
   * Scoped by role: an admin sees every appointment, a doctor only the ones
   * booked with them, and a patient only their own.
   */
  async findAllForUser(user: SessionUser): Promise<Appointment[]> {
    if (user.role === "admin") return this.repository.findAll()

    if (user.role === "doctor") {
      const medic = await this.medicRepository.findByUserId(user.id)
      return medic ? this.repository.findByDoctorId(medic.id) : []
    }

    const patient = await this.patientService.findByUserId(user.id)
    return patient ? this.repository.findByPatientId(patient.id) : []
  }

  /**
   * Un turno, pero solo si a esta persona le corresponde verlo.
   *
   * Misma regla de alcance que `findAllForUser`: el administrador ve todos, el
   * profesional los suyos y el paciente los propios. Devolver `undefined` en
   * lugar de lanzar deja que quien llama decida entre 403 y 404.
   */
  async findOneForUser(
    user: SessionUser,
    id: string
  ): Promise<Appointment | undefined> {
    const appointment = await this.repository.findOne(id)
    if (!appointment) return undefined

    if (user.role === "admin") return appointment

    if (user.role === "doctor") {
      const medic = await this.medicRepository.findByUserId(user.id)
      return medic && appointment.doctorId === medic.id ? appointment : undefined
    }

    const patient = await this.patientService.findByUserId(user.id)
    return patient && appointment.patientId === patient.id
      ? appointment
      : undefined
  }

  /**
   * Horarios ya ocupados de un profesional en una fecha.
   *
   * Sirve para apagar esos botones en el calendario: antes se ofrecÃ­an todos y
   * la persona se enteraba de que el horario estaba tomado reciÃ©n al confirmar,
   * despuÃ©s de haber recorrido los cuatro pasos.
   */
  /**
   * Horarios que el calendario tiene que mostrar deshabilitados.
   *
   * Son dos conjuntos: los que ya tiene tomados el profesional, y los que el
   * propio paciente tiene ocupados ese dÃ­a con CUALQUIER mÃ©dico. Sin los
   * segundos, el horario se ofrecÃ­a como libre y el rechazo reciÃ©n aparecÃ­a
   * al confirmar, que es la peor forma de enterarse.
   */
  async takenSlots(
    doctorId: string,
    date: string,
    user?: SessionUser
  ): Promise<string[]> {
    const appointments = await this.repository.findByDoctorAndDate(
      doctorId,
      date
    )

    const taken = new Set(
      appointments
        .filter((appointment) => appointment.status !== "cancelled")
        .map((appointment) => appointment.time)
    )

    if (user) {
      const patient = await this.patientService.findByUserId(user.id)
      if (patient) {
        const own = await this.repository.findByPatientAndDate(patient.id, date)
        for (const appointment of own) taken.add(appointment.time)
      }
    }

    return [...taken]
  }

  async create(
    user: SessionUser,
    input: AppointmentInput
  ): Promise<CreateAppointmentResult> {
    let patient
    try {
      patient = await this.patientService.findOrCreateForUser(
        user.id,
        user.name,
        user.email
      )
    } catch (error) {
      return { ok: false, error: (error as Error).message }
    }

    const medic = await this.medicRepository.findOne(input.doctorId)
    if (!medic) {
      return { ok: false, error: "El profesional seleccionado no existe." }
    }

    const clash = await this.repository.findClash(
      input.doctorId,
      input.date,
      input.time
    )
    if (clash) {
      return { ok: false, error: "Ese horario ya fue reservado. ElegÃ­ otro." }
    }

    // Una persona no puede estar en dos consultas a la vez. Sin este chequeo
    // se podÃ­a reservar con dos profesionales distintos el mismo dÃ­a y hora:
    // cada turno era vÃ¡lido por separado y ninguna de las otras dos guardas
    // â€”agenda del mÃ©dico, consultorioâ€” los veÃ­a.
    const ownClash = await this.repository.findPatientClash(
      patient.id,
      input.date,
      input.time
    )
    if (ownClash) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        error: `Ya tenÃ©s un turno ese dÃ­a a las ${input.time} con ${ownClash.doctorName}.`,
      }
    }

    // El consultorio es un recurso compartido: puede estar ocupado por otro
    // profesional aunque este mÃ©dico tenga la agenda libre.
    if (medic.branchId && medic.office) {
      const roomClash = await this.repository.findRoomClash(
        medic.branchId,
        medic.office,
        input.date,
        input.time
      )
      if (roomClash) {
        return {
          ok: false,
          error: `El consultorio ${medic.office} estÃ¡ ocupado en ese horario. ElegÃ­ otro.`,
        }
      }
    }

    const appointment = new Appointment({
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      patientId: patient.id,
      patientName: `${patient.firstName} ${patient.lastName}`,
      doctorId: medic.id,
      doctorName: medic.displayName,
      specialtyName: medic.specialtyName,
      // Copiados a propÃ³sito: si el mÃ©dico cambia de sede, este turno tiene que
      // seguir diciendo dÃ³nde se atendiÃ³ la persona.
      branchId: medic.branchId,
      branchName: medic.branchName,
      office: medic.office,
      date: input.date,
      time: input.time,
      status: "scheduled",
      reason: input.reason ?? null,
    })

    try {
      await this.repository.create(appointment)
    } catch (error) {
      // The clash check above and this insert are not atomic, so two requests
      // for the same slot can both get past it. The partial unique index
      // `appointments_slot_unique` is what actually decides, and the loser
      // lands here as a 23505.
      if (isUniqueViolation(error)) {
        return {
          ok: false,
          error:
            "Ese horario acaba de ser tomado por otra persona. ElegÃ­ otro.",
        }
      }
      throw error
    }

    await this.notificationService.appointmentBooked(appointment)

    return { ok: true, appointment }
  }

  /**
   * Mueve un turno a otra fecha y hora conservando su identidad.
   *
   * Cancelar y volver a crear era la Ãºnica salida hasta ahora, pero eso pierde
   * el historial: el turno original queda como "cancelado" y el nuevo no sabe
   * de dÃ³nde viene. Reprogramar mantiene el mismo registro.
   */
  async reschedule(
    user: SessionUser,
    id: string,
    date: string,
    time: string
  ): Promise<CreateAppointmentResult> {
    const appointment = await this.repository.findOne(id)
    if (!appointment) {
      return { ok: false, error: "El turno no existe." }
    }

    if (user.role === "patient") {
      const patient = await this.patientService.findByUserId(user.id)
      if (!patient || appointment.patientId !== patient.id) {
        return { ok: false, error: "Solo podÃ©s reprogramar tus propios turnos." }
      }
    }

    if (appointment.status !== "scheduled") {
      return {
        ok: false,
        error: "Solo se pueden reprogramar los turnos agendados.",
      }
    }

    if (appointment.date === date && appointment.time === time) {
      return { ok: false, error: "ElegÃ­ una fecha u hora distinta." }
    }

    const clash = await this.repository.findClash(
      appointment.doctorId,
      date,
      time,
      appointment.id
    )
    if (clash) {
      return { ok: false, error: "Ese horario ya fue reservado. ElegÃ­ otro." }
    }

    const ownClash = await this.repository.findPatientClash(
      appointment.patientId,
      date,
      time,
      appointment.id
    )
    if (ownClash) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        error: `Ya tenÃ©s un turno ese dÃ­a a las ${time} con ${ownClash.doctorName}.`,
      }
    }

    if (appointment.branchId && appointment.office) {
      const roomClash = await this.repository.findRoomClash(
        appointment.branchId,
        appointment.office,
        date,
        time,
        appointment.id
      )
      if (roomClash) {
        return {
          ok: false,
          error: `El consultorio ${appointment.office} estÃ¡ ocupado en ese horario. ElegÃ­ otro.`,
        }
      }
    }

    // Se copian ANTES del UPDATE. Leerlos despuÃ©s funciona solo mientras el
    // repositorio devuelva instancias nuevas en cada consulta; si alguna vez
    // devolviera la misma que se acaba de modificar â€”una cachÃ©, por ejemploâ€”,
    // el aviso informarÃ­a el horario nuevo como si fuera el anterior.
    const previousDate = appointment.date
    const previousTime = appointment.time

    let updated
    try {
      updated = await this.repository.update(id, { date, time })
    } catch (error) {
      if (isUniqueViolation(error)) {
        return {
          ok: false,
          error: "Ese horario acaba de ser tomado por otra persona. ElegÃ­ otro.",
        }
      }
      throw error
    }

    if (updated) {
      await this.notificationService.appointmentRescheduled(
        updated,
        previousDate,
        previousTime
      )
    }

    return {
      ok: true,
      appointment: updated,
    }
  }

  async cancel(user: SessionUser, id: string): Promise<void> {
    if (user.role === "patient") {
      const patient = await this.patientService.findByUserId(user.id)
      if (!patient) throw new Error("Unauthorized")
      await this.repository.updateStatusScopedToPatient(
        id,
        patient.id,
        "cancelled"
      )
    } else {
      await this.repository.update(id, { status: "cancelled" })
    }

    // Se relee para avisar solo si la cancelaciÃ³n realmente se aplicÃ³: la
    // versiÃ³n acotada al paciente no toca nada si el turno no era suyo.
    const cancelled = await this.repository.findOne(id)
    if (cancelled?.status === "cancelled") {
      await this.notificationService.appointmentCancelled(cancelled)
    }
  }

  async setStatus(
    user: SessionUser,
    id: string,
    status: AppointmentStatus
  ): Promise<void> {
    if (user.role === "patient") throw new Error("Unauthorized")
    const updated = await this.repository.update(id, { status })
    if (updated) {
      await this.notificationService.appointmentStatusChanged(updated)
    }
  }

  async saveNotes(user: SessionUser, id: string, notes: string): Promise<void> {
    if (user.role === "patient") throw new Error("Unauthorized")
    await this.repository.update(id, { notes })
  }
}

===== .\src\services\AuthService.ts =====

import "server-only"
import type { Role } from "@/models/User"
import type { UserRepository } from "@/repository/UserRepository"
import type { MedicService } from "./MedicService"
import type { PatientService } from "./PatientService"

export interface AssignRoleResult {
  ok: boolean
  error?: string
}

export class AuthService {
  private userRepository: UserRepository
  private medicService: MedicService
  private patientService: PatientService

  constructor(
    userRepository: UserRepository,
    medicService: MedicService,
    patientService: PatientService
  ) {
    this.userRepository = userRepository
    this.medicService = medicService
    this.patientService = patientService
  }

  /**
   * Persists the role chosen at sign-up and creates the matching domain record
   * so the account shows up in the admin listings.
   */
  async assignRole(userId: string, role: Role): Promise<AssignRoleResult> {
    const row = await this.userRepository.findById(userId)
    if (!row) return { ok: false, error: "Usuario no encontrado" }

    await this.userRepository.updateRole(userId, role)

    if (role === "patient") {
      await this.patientService.ensureForUser(userId, row.name, row.email)
    } else if (role === "doctor") {
      await this.medicService.ensureForUser(userId, row.name, row.email)
    }

    return { ok: true }
  }
}

===== .\src\services\BranchService.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { Branch, type BranchInput } from "@/models/Branch"
import type { BranchRepository } from "@/repository/BranchRepository"

export class BranchService {
  private repository: BranchRepository

  constructor(repository: BranchRepository) {
    this.repository = repository
  }

  findAll(): Promise<Branch[]> {
    return this.repository.findAll()
  }

  findActive(): Promise<Branch[]> {
    return this.repository.findActive()
  }

  findOne(id: string): Promise<Branch | undefined> {
    return this.repository.findOne(id)
  }

  async create(input: BranchInput): Promise<Branch> {
    const duplicate = await this.repository.findByName(input.name)
    if (duplicate) {
      throw new Error(`Ya existe una sede llamada "${duplicate.name}"`)
    }

    return this.repository.create(
      new Branch(
        crypto.randomUUID(),
        input.name,
        input.address,
        input.phone,
        input.active
      )
    )
  }

  async update(id: string, input: BranchInput): Promise<Branch | undefined> {
    const current = await this.repository.findOne(id)
    if (!current) throw new Error("La sede no existe")

    const duplicate = await this.repository.findOtherByName(input.name, id)
    if (duplicate) {
      throw new Error(`Ya existe una sede llamada "${duplicate.name}"`)
    }

    return this.repository.update(id, input)
  }

  /**
   * Borrar una sede con profesionales asignados los dejarÃ­a sin lugar donde
   * atender y sin consultorio vÃ¡lido, asÃ­ que se rechaza explicando por quÃ©.
   * Para sacarla de circulaciÃ³n sin perder la historia estÃ¡ el campo `active`.
   */
  async remove(id: string): Promise<{ id: string } | undefined> {
    const branch = await this.repository.findOne(id)
    if (!branch) throw new Error("La sede no existe")

    const assigned = await this.repository.countDoctors(id)
    if (assigned > 0) {
      throw new Error(
        `No se puede eliminar "${branch.name}": tiene ${assigned} ${
          assigned === 1 ? "mÃ©dico asignado" : "mÃ©dicos asignados"
        }. Reasignalos primero, o desactivÃ¡ la sede para que deje de ofrecerse.`
      )
    }

    return this.repository.delete(id)
  }
}

===== .\src\services\container.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { AppointmentRepository } from "@/repository/AppointmentRepository"
import { BranchRepository } from "@/repository/BranchRepository"
import { MedicRepository } from "@/repository/MedicRepository"
import { NotificationRepository } from "@/repository/NotificationRepository"
import { PatientRepository } from "@/repository/PatientRepository"
import { ReviewRepository } from "@/repository/ReviewRepository"
import { SpecialtyRepository } from "@/repository/SpecialtyRepository"
import { UserRepository } from "@/repository/UserRepository"
import { AppointmentService } from "./AppointmentService"
import { AuthService } from "./AuthService"
import { BranchService } from "./BranchService"
import { MedicService } from "./MedicService"
import { NotificationService } from "./NotificationService"
import { PatientService } from "./PatientService"
import { ProfileService } from "./ProfileService"
import { ReviewService } from "./ReviewService"
import { SeedService } from "./SeedService"
import { SpecialtyService } from "./SpecialtyService"
import { UserService } from "./UserService"

/**
 * Single place where the object graph is wired.
 *
 * Every controller used to rebuild its own services, so adding a dependency
 * meant editing each of them and the copies drifted apart. The instances are
 * stateless â€” they only hold repositories â€” so sharing them per server process
 * is safe.
 */

const appointmentRepository = new AppointmentRepository()
const branchRepository = new BranchRepository()
const medicRepository = new MedicRepository()
const notificationRepository = new NotificationRepository()
const patientRepository = new PatientRepository()
const reviewRepository = new ReviewRepository()
const specialtyRepository = new SpecialtyRepository()
const userRepository = new UserRepository()

export const branchService = new BranchService(branchRepository)

export const medicService = new MedicService(
  medicRepository,
  specialtyRepository,
  branchRepository,
  userRepository
)

export const patientService = new PatientService(
  patientRepository,
  userRepository
)

export const specialtyService = new SpecialtyService(
  specialtyRepository,
  medicRepository
)

export const userService = new UserService(
  userRepository,
  medicService,
  patientService
)

export const authService = new AuthService(
  userRepository,
  medicService,
  patientService
)

export const notificationService = new NotificationService(
  notificationRepository,
  appointmentRepository,
  patientRepository,
  medicRepository
)

export const appointmentService = new AppointmentService(
  appointmentRepository,
  medicRepository,
  patientService,
  notificationService
)

export const reviewService = new ReviewService(
  reviewRepository,
  appointmentRepository,
  medicRepository,
  patientService,
  notificationService
)

export const profileService = new ProfileService(
  patientRepository,
  medicRepository,
  specialtyRepository,
  appointmentRepository
)

export const seedService = new SeedService(
  userRepository,
  patientRepository,
  medicRepository,
  specialtyRepository,
  branchRepository
)

===== .\src\services\MedicService.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { Medic, type MedicInput } from "@/models/Medic"
import type { BranchRepository } from "@/repository/BranchRepository"
import type { MedicRepository } from "@/repository/MedicRepository"
import type { SpecialtyRepository } from "@/repository/SpecialtyRepository"
import type { UserRepository } from "@/repository/UserRepository"

interface ResolvedSpecialty {
  id: string
  name: string
}

export class MedicService {
  private repository: MedicRepository
  private specialtyRepository: SpecialtyRepository
  private branchRepository: BranchRepository
  private userRepository: UserRepository

  constructor(
    repository: MedicRepository,
    specialtyRepository: SpecialtyRepository,
    branchRepository: BranchRepository,
    userRepository: UserRepository
  ) {
    this.repository = repository
    this.specialtyRepository = specialtyRepository
    this.branchRepository = branchRepository
    this.userRepository = userRepository
  }

  findAll(): Promise<Medic[]> {
    return this.repository.findAll()
  }

  findOne(id: string): Promise<Medic | undefined> {
    return this.repository.findOne(id)
  }

  findByUserId(userId: string): Promise<Medic | undefined> {
    return this.repository.findByUserId(userId)
  }

  async create(input: MedicInput): Promise<Medic> {
    const duplicate = await this.repository.findByEmail(input.email)
    if (duplicate) {
      throw new Error("Ya existe un mÃ©dico con ese email")
    }

    const specialty = await this.resolveSpecialty(input.specialtyId)
    const branchId = await this.resolveBranch(input.branchId)

    const medic = new Medic({
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      firstName: input.firstName,
      lastName: input.lastName,
      email: input.email,
      phone: input.phone,
      specialtyId: specialty.id,
      specialtyName: specialty.name,
      branchId,
      office: input.office,
      avatar: input.avatar,
      license: input.license,
      yearsExperience: input.yearsExperience,
      workDays: input.workDays,
      startTime: input.startTime,
      endTime: input.endTime,
      breakStart: input.breakStart,
      breakEnd: input.breakEnd,
      slotMinutes: input.slotMinutes,
      // Un profesional reciÃ©n cargado no tiene reseÃ±as: arranca sin estrellas,
      // no con cinco.
      rating: 0,
      reviewCount: 0,
    })

    const created = await this.repository.create(medic)
    await this.refreshDoctorCount(specialty.id)
    return created
  }

  async update(id: string, input: MedicInput): Promise<Medic | undefined> {
    const current = await this.repository.findOne(id)
    if (!current) throw new Error("El mÃ©dico no existe")

    const duplicate = await this.repository.findOtherByEmail(input.email, id)
    if (duplicate) {
      throw new Error("Ya existe otro mÃ©dico con ese email")
    }

    const specialty = await this.resolveSpecialty(input.specialtyId)
    const branchId = await this.resolveBranch(input.branchId)

    // Se guarda antes del UPDATE: leerlo despuÃ©s darÃ­a la especialidad nueva y
    // el contador de la anterior nunca bajarÃ­a.
    const previousSpecialtyId = current.specialtyId

    const updated = await this.repository.update(id, {
      firstName: input.firstName,
      lastName: input.lastName,
      email: input.email,
      phone: input.phone,
      specialtyId: specialty.id,
      specialtyName: specialty.name,
      branchId,
      office: input.office,
      avatar: input.avatar,
      license: input.license,
      yearsExperience: input.yearsExperience,
      workDays: input.workDays,
      startTime: input.startTime,
      endTime: input.endTime,
      breakStart: input.breakStart,
      breakEnd: input.breakEnd,
      slotMinutes: input.slotMinutes,
    })

    // Ambos contadores se mueven cuando se reasigna un profesional.
    await this.refreshDoctorCount(previousSpecialtyId)
    await this.refreshDoctorCount(specialty.id)

    return updated
  }

  /**
   * Los turnos caen en cascada por la clave forÃ¡nea, y la cuenta de acceso se
   * borra junto con el profesional para no dejar credenciales huÃ©rfanas.
   */
  async remove(id: string): Promise<{ id: string } | undefined> {
    const medic = await this.repository.findOne(id)
    if (!medic) throw new Error("El mÃ©dico no existe")

    const result = await this.repository.delete(id)

    if (medic.userId) {
      await this.userRepository.delete(medic.userId)
    }
    await this.refreshDoctorCount(medic.specialtyId)

    return result
  }

  /**
   * Runs when a user picks the "doctor" role at sign-up: creates the linked
   * domain record if it does not exist yet. The specialty is left empty for an
   * admin to fill in later.
   */
  async ensureForUser(
    userId: string,
    fullName: string,
    email: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    const existing = await this.repository.findByUserId(userId)
    if (existing) return

    // Una ficha cargada por un admin antes de que la persona se registre se
    // adopta en lugar de duplicarse.
    const byEmail = await this.repository.findByEmail(email)
    if (byEmail) {
      await this.repository.update(byEmail.id, { userId })
      return
    }

    const parts = fullName.trim().split(" ")
    await this.repository.create(
      new Medic({
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        firstName: parts[0] ?? fullName,
        lastName: parts.slice(1).join(" "),
        email,
        userId,
      })
    )
  }

  /**
   * El formulario manda un id elegido del catÃ¡logo y el nombre se lee de la
   * especialidad, asÃ­ las dos columnas no pueden separarse.
   */
  private async resolveSpecialty(
    specialtyId: string
  ): Promise<ResolvedSpecialty> {
    if (!specialtyId) return { id: "", name: "" }

    const specialty = await this.specialtyRepository.findOne(specialtyId)
    if (!specialty) {
      throw new Error("La especialidad seleccionada no existe")
    }
    return { id: specialty.id, name: specialty.name }
  }

  /** La sede es opcional, pero si se indica una tiene que existir. */
  private async resolveBranch(branchId: string): Promise<string | null> {
    if (!branchId) return null

    const branch = await this.branchRepository.findOne(branchId)
    if (!branch) {
      throw new Error("La sede seleccionada no existe")
    }
    return branch.id
  }

  private async refreshDoctorCount(specialtyId: string): Promise<void> {
    if (!specialtyId) return
    const total = await this.repository.countBySpecialtyId(specialtyId)
    await this.specialtyRepository.setActiveDoctors(specialtyId, total)
  }
}

===== .\src\services\NotificationService.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { formatLongDate, toISODate } from "@/lib/format/date"
import type { Appointment, AppointmentStatus } from "@/models/Appointment"
import {
  Notification,
  type NotificationType,
} from "@/models/Notification"
import type { AppointmentRepository } from "@/repository/AppointmentRepository"
import type { MedicRepository } from "@/repository/MedicRepository"
import type { NotificationRepository } from "@/repository/NotificationRepository"
import type { PatientRepository } from "@/repository/PatientRepository"

/** CuÃ¡ntos dÃ­as antes del turno se avisa. */
const REMINDER_DAYS_AHEAD = 1

function place(appointment: Appointment): string {
  return [
    appointment.branchName,
    appointment.office && `consultorio ${appointment.office}`,
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(", ")
}

const statusMessages: Partial<
  Record<AppointmentStatus, { title: string; body: (a: Appointment) => string }>
> = {
  completed: {
    title: "Consulta registrada como atendida",
    body: (a) =>
      `Tu consulta con ${a.doctorName} del ${formatLongDate(a.date)} figura como atendida. Ya podÃ©s calificar la atenciÃ³n.`,
  },
  absent: {
    title: "FigurÃ¡s como ausente",
    body: (a) =>
      `El turno con ${a.doctorName} del ${formatLongDate(a.date)} quedÃ³ registrado como ausente.`,
  },
  cancelled: {
    title: "Turno cancelado",
    body: (a) =>
      `El turno con ${a.doctorName} del ${formatLongDate(a.date)} fue cancelado.`,
  },
}

export class NotificationService {
  private repository: NotificationRepository
  private appointmentRepository: AppointmentRepository
  private patientRepository: PatientRepository
  private medicRepository: MedicRepository

  constructor(
    repository: NotificationRepository,
    appointmentRepository: AppointmentRepository,
    patientRepository: PatientRepository,
    medicRepository: MedicRepository
  ) {
    this.repository = repository
    this.appointmentRepository = appointmentRepository
    this.patientRepository = patientRepository
    this.medicRepository = medicRepository
  }

  list(userId: string) {
    return this.repository.findByUserId(userId)
  }

  countUnread(userId: string) {
    return this.repository.countUnread(userId)
  }

  markAllRead(userId: string) {
    return this.repository.markAllRead(userId)
  }

  markRead(userId: string, id: string) {
    return this.repository.markRead(userId, id)
  }

  // --- Emisores ------------------------------------------------------------

  /**
   * Avisa al paciente y al profesional que se reservÃ³ un turno.
   *
   * Nada de esto puede tumbar la reserva: un aviso que no sale es una molestia,
   * perder el turno de una persona es un problema de verdad. Por eso cada envÃ­o
   * va aislado en `safely`.
   */
  async appointmentBooked(appointment: Appointment): Promise<void> {
    const { patientUserId, doctorUserId } = await this.participants(appointment)
    const where = place(appointment)

    await this.safely(() =>
      this.push(patientUserId, {
        type: "appointment_booked",
        title: "Turno confirmado",
        body: `${appointment.doctorName} Â· ${formatLongDate(appointment.date)} a las ${appointment.time} hs${where ? ` en ${where}` : ""}.`,
        dedupeKey: `booked:${appointment.id}`,
      })
    )

    await this.safely(() =>
      this.push(doctorUserId, {
        type: "appointment_booked",
        title: "Nuevo turno agendado",
        body: `${appointment.patientName} reservÃ³ para el ${formatLongDate(appointment.date)} a las ${appointment.time} hs.`,
        dedupeKey: `booked-doctor:${appointment.id}`,
      })
    )
  }

  async appointmentRescheduled(
    appointment: Appointment,
    previousDate: string,
    previousTime: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    const { patientUserId, doctorUserId } = await this.participants(appointment)
    const cambio = `del ${formatLongDate(previousDate)} ${previousTime} hs al ${formatLongDate(appointment.date)} ${appointment.time} hs`

    await this.safely(() =>
      this.push(patientUserId, {
        type: "appointment_rescheduled",
        title: "Turno reprogramado",
        body: `Tu turno con ${appointment.doctorName} se moviÃ³ ${cambio}.`,
        // La fecha entra en la clave: reprogramar dos veces genera dos avisos.
        dedupeKey: `rescheduled:${appointment.id}:${appointment.date}:${appointment.time}`,
      })
    )

    await this.safely(() =>
      this.push(doctorUserId, {
        type: "appointment_rescheduled",
        title: "Turno reprogramado",
        body: `${appointment.patientName} moviÃ³ su turno ${cambio}.`,
        dedupeKey: `rescheduled-doctor:${appointment.id}:${appointment.date}:${appointment.time}`,
      })
    )
  }

  async appointmentCancelled(appointment: Appointment): Promise<void> {
    const { patientUserId, doctorUserId } = await this.participants(appointment)

    await this.safely(() =>
      this.push(patientUserId, {
        type: "appointment_cancelled",
        title: "Turno cancelado",
        body: `Se cancelÃ³ tu turno con ${appointment.doctorName} del ${formatLongDate(appointment.date)}.`,
        dedupeKey: `cancelled:${appointment.id}`,
      })
    )

    await this.safely(() =>
      this.push(doctorUserId, {
        type: "appointment_cancelled",
        title: "Turno cancelado",
        body: `${appointment.patientName} cancelÃ³ el turno del ${formatLongDate(appointment.date)} a las ${appointment.time} hs.`,
        dedupeKey: `cancelled-doctor:${appointment.id}`,
      })
    )
  }

  async appointmentStatusChanged(appointment: Appointment): Promise<void> {
    const message = statusMessages[appointment.status]
    if (!message) return

    const { patientUserId } = await this.participants(appointment)

    await this.safely(() =>
      this.push(patientUserId, {
        type: "appointment_status",
        title: message.title,
        body: message.body(appointment),
        dedupeKey: `status:${appointment.id}:${appointment.status}`,
      })
    )
  }

  async reviewReceived(
    appointment: Appointment,
    score: number
  ): Promise<void> {
    const { doctorUserId } = await this.participants(appointment)

    await this.safely(() =>
      this.push(doctorUserId, {
        type: "review_received",
        title: "Recibiste una calificaciÃ³n",
        body: `${appointment.patientName} calificÃ³ con ${score} ${score === 1 ? "estrella" : "estrellas"} la consulta del ${formatLongDate(appointment.date)}.`,
        dedupeKey: `review:${appointment.id}`,
      })
    )
  }

  // --- Recordatorios -------------------------------------------------------

  /**
   * Crea los recordatorios de los turnos de maÃ±ana.
   *
   * Se llama desde dos lados: al abrir la app (para la persona que entrÃ³) y
   * desde `/api/cron/reminders` (para todos). La deduplicaciÃ³n por
   * `dedupeKey` hace que las dos vÃ­as convivan sin pisarse.
   */
  async generateReminders(onlyPatientId?: string): Promise<number> {
    const target = new Date()
    target.setDate(target.getDate() + REMINDER_DAYS_AHEAD)
    const targetDate = toISODate(target)

    const appointments =
      await this.appointmentRepository.findScheduledOn(targetDate)

    const pending = onlyPatientId
      ? appointments.filter((a) => a.patientId === onlyPatientId)
      : appointments

    let created = 0
    for (const appointment of pending) {
      const done = await this.safely(async () => {
        const { patientUserId } = await this.participants(appointment)
        const where = place(appointment)

        return this.push(patientUserId, {
          type: "appointment_reminder",
          title: "TenÃ©s un turno maÃ±ana",
          body: `${appointment.doctorName} Â· ${formatLongDate(appointment.date)} a las ${appointment.time} hs${where ? ` en ${where}` : ""}.`,
          dedupeKey: `reminder:${appointment.id}`,
        })
      })
      if (done) created++
    }

    return created
  }

  // --- Internos ------------------------------------------------------------

  /** Resuelve las cuentas de acceso detrÃ¡s del paciente y del profesional. */
  private async participants(appointment: Appointment): Promise<{
    patientUserId: string | null
    doctorUserId: string | null
  }> {
    const [patient, medic] = await Promise.all([
      this.patientRepository.findOne(appointment.patientId),
      this.medicRepository.findOne(appointment.doctorId),
    ])

    return {
      patientUserId: patient?.userId ?? null,
      doctorUserId: medic?.userId ?? null,
    }
  }

  private async push(
    userId: string | null,
    data: {
      type: NotificationType
      title: string
      body: string
      dedupeKey: string
    }
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    // Una ficha sin cuenta de acceso asociada no tiene a dÃ³nde recibir el aviso.
    if (!userId) return false

    return this.repository.createIfMissing(
      new Notification(
        crypto.randomUUID(),
        userId,
        data.type,
        data.title,
        data.body,
        data.dedupeKey
      )
    )
  }

  /** Un aviso que falla nunca debe voltear la operaciÃ³n que lo originÃ³. */
  private async safely(
    operation: () => Promise<boolean>
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      return await operation()
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[notificaciones] no se pudo registrar el aviso:", error)
      return false
    }
  }
}

===== .\src\services\PatientService.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { Patient, type PatientInput } from "@/models/Patient"
import type { PatientRepository } from "@/repository/PatientRepository"
import type { UserRepository } from "@/repository/UserRepository"
import { todayISODate } from "@/lib/format/date"

export class PatientService {
  private repository: PatientRepository
  private userRepository: UserRepository

  constructor(
    repository: PatientRepository,
    userRepository: UserRepository
  ) {
    this.repository = repository
    this.userRepository = userRepository
  }

  findAll(): Promise<Patient[]> {
    return this.repository.findAll()
  }

  findOne(id: string): Promise<Patient | undefined> {
    return this.repository.findOne(id)
  }

  findByUserId(userId: string): Promise<Patient | undefined> {
    return this.repository.findByUserId(userId)
  }

  async create(input: PatientInput): Promise<Patient> {
    const duplicate = await this.repository.findByEmail(input.email)
    if (duplicate) {
      throw new Error("Ya existe un paciente con ese email")
    }

    const patient = new Patient(
      crypto.randomUUID(),
      input.firstName,
      input.lastName,
      input.email,
      input.phone,
      input.dni,
      input.birthDate,
      todayISODate(),
      input.bloodType ?? null
    )
    return this.repository.create(patient)
  }

  async update(id: string, input: PatientInput): Promise<Patient | undefined> {
    const current = await this.repository.findOne(id)
    if (!current) throw new Error("El paciente no existe")

    const duplicate = await this.repository.findOtherByEmail(input.email, id)
    if (duplicate) {
      throw new Error("Ya existe otro paciente con ese email")
    }

    return this.repository.update(id, {
      ...input,
      bloodType: input.bloodType ?? null,
    })
  }

  /**
   * Appointments cascade from the FK, and the login account is removed with the
   * patient so no orphan credentials survive the deletion.
   */
  async remove(id: string): Promise<{ id: string } | undefined> {
    const patient = await this.repository.findOne(id)
    if (!patient) throw new Error("El paciente no existe")

    const result = await this.repository.delete(id)

    if (patient.userId) {
      await this.userRepository.delete(patient.userId)
    }

    return result
  }

  /**
   * Devuelve la ficha del usuario, creÃ¡ndola si falta.
   *
   * El alta de cuenta llama a `/api/set-role` desde el navegador y esa llamada
   * puede fallar en silencio â€”o correr antes de que la cookie de sesiÃ³n estÃ©
   * disponibleâ€”, dejando una cuenta con rol "patient" (el default de la
   * columna) pero sin ninguna fila en `patients`. Esa persona podÃ­a navegar
   * hasta el Ãºltimo paso de la reserva y ahÃ­ chocarse con "No se encontrÃ³ el
   * perfil del paciente". Recuperar la situaciÃ³n acÃ¡ es mÃ¡s robusto que confiar
   * en que aquel fetch haya salido bien.
   */
  async findOrCreateForUser(
    userId: string,
    fullName: string,
    email: string
  ): Promise<Patient> {
    await this.ensureForUser(userId, fullName, email)

    const patient = await this.repository.findByUserId(userId)
    if (!patient) {
      throw new Error("No se pudo crear el perfil del paciente.")
    }
    return patient
  }

  /**
   * Runs when a user picks the "patient" role at sign-up: creates the linked
   * domain record if it does not exist yet.
   */
  async ensureForUser(
    userId: string,
    fullName: string,
    email: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    const existing = await this.repository.findByUserId(userId)
    if (existing) return

    // A patient row loaded by an admin before the person signed up is adopted
    // instead of duplicated.
    const byEmail = await this.repository.findByEmail(email)
    if (byEmail) {
      await this.repository.update(byEmail.id, { userId })
      return
    }

    const parts = fullName.trim().split(" ")
    const patient = new Patient(
      crypto.randomUUID(),
      parts[0] ?? fullName,
      parts.slice(1).join(" "),
      email
    )
    patient.userId = userId
    await this.repository.create(patient)
  }
}

===== .\src\services\ProfileService.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { todayISODate } from "@/lib/format/date"
import type { ProfileSummary } from "@/models/Profile"
import type { SessionUser } from "@/models/User"
import type { AppointmentRepository } from "@/repository/AppointmentRepository"
import type { MedicRepository } from "@/repository/MedicRepository"
import type { PatientRepository } from "@/repository/PatientRepository"
import type { SpecialtyRepository } from "@/repository/SpecialtyRepository"

export class ProfileService {
  private patientRepository: PatientRepository
  private medicRepository: MedicRepository
  private specialtyRepository: SpecialtyRepository
  private appointmentRepository: AppointmentRepository

  constructor(
    patientRepository: PatientRepository,
    medicRepository: MedicRepository,
    specialtyRepository: SpecialtyRepository,
    appointmentRepository: AppointmentRepository
  ) {
    this.patientRepository = patientRepository
    this.medicRepository = medicRepository
    this.specialtyRepository = specialtyRepository
    this.appointmentRepository = appointmentRepository
  }

  async getSummary(user: SessionUser): Promise<ProfileSummary> {
    const base = {
      role: user.role,
      name: user.name,
      email: user.email,
      image: user.image,
    }

    if (user.role === "patient") return this.getPatientSummary(user, base)
    if (user.role === "doctor") return this.getDoctorSummary(user, base)
    return this.getAdminSummary(base)
  }

  private async getPatientSummary(
    user: SessionUser,
    base: Pick<ProfileSummary, "role" | "name" | "email" | "image">
  ): Promise<ProfileSummary> {
    const patient = await this.patientRepository.findByUserId(user.id)
    const appointments = patient
      ? await this.appointmentRepository.findByPatientId(patient.id)
      : []
    const today = todayISODate()

    return {
      ...base,
      patient: {
        dni: patient?.dni ?? "â€”",
        phone: patient?.phone ?? "â€”",
        birthDate: patient?.birthDate ?? "â€”",
        bloodType: patient?.bloodType,
        memberSince: patient?.createdAt ?? "â€”",
        total: appointments.length,
        upcoming: appointments.filter(
          (appointment) =>
            appointment.status === "scheduled" && appointment.date >= today
        ).length,
        completed: appointments.filter(
          (appointment) => appointment.status === "completed"
        ).length,
      },
    }
  }

  private async getDoctorSummary(
    user: SessionUser,
    base: Pick<ProfileSummary, "role" | "name" | "email" | "image">
  ): Promise<ProfileSummary> {
    const medic = await this.medicRepository.findByUserId(user.id)
    const todayAppointments = medic
      ? await this.appointmentRepository.findByDoctorAndDate(
          medic.id,
          todayISODate()
        )
      : []

    return {
      ...base,
      doctor: {
        specialtyName: medic?.specialtyName ?? "â€”",
        license: medic?.license ?? "â€”",
        rating: medic?.rating ?? 0,
        reviewCount: medic?.reviewCount ?? 0,
        branchName: medic?.branchName || "Sin asignar",
        office: medic?.office || "â€”",
        yearsExperience: medic?.yearsExperience ?? 0,
        phone: medic?.phone ?? "â€”",
        workDays: medic?.workDays ?? [],
        todayTotal: todayAppointments.length,
        todayCompleted: todayAppointments.filter(
          (appointment) => appointment.status === "completed"
        ).length,
        todayPending: todayAppointments.filter(
          (appointment) => appointment.status === "scheduled"
        ).length,
      },
    }
  }

  private async getAdminSummary(
    base: Pick<ProfileSummary, "role" | "name" | "email" | "image">
  ): Promise<ProfileSummary> {
    const [patients, medics, specialties, appointments] = await Promise.all([
      this.patientRepository.findAll(),
      this.medicRepository.findAll(),
      this.specialtyRepository.findAll(),
      this.appointmentRepository.findAll(),
    ])

    return {
      ...base,
      admin: {
        totalPatients: patients.length,
        totalDoctors: medics.length,
        totalSpecialties: specialties.length,
        totalAppointments: appointments.length,
        scheduled: appointments.filter(
          (appointment) => appointment.status === "scheduled"
        ).length,
      },
    }
  }
}

===== .\src\services\ReviewService.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { Review, type RatingSummary, type ReviewInput } from "@/models/Review"
import type { SessionUser } from "@/models/User"
import type { AppointmentRepository } from "@/repository/AppointmentRepository"
import type { MedicRepository } from "@/repository/MedicRepository"
import type { ReviewRepository } from "@/repository/ReviewRepository"
import type { NotificationService } from "./NotificationService"
import type { PatientService } from "./PatientService"

export class ReviewService {
  private repository: ReviewRepository
  private appointmentRepository: AppointmentRepository
  private medicRepository: MedicRepository
  private patientService: PatientService
  private notificationService: NotificationService

  constructor(
    repository: ReviewRepository,
    appointmentRepository: AppointmentRepository,
    medicRepository: MedicRepository,
    patientService: PatientService,
    notificationService: NotificationService
  ) {
    this.repository = repository
    this.appointmentRepository = appointmentRepository
    this.medicRepository = medicRepository
    this.patientService = patientService
    this.notificationService = notificationService
  }

  /**
   * ReseÃ±as de un profesional, de la mÃ¡s nueva a la mÃ¡s vieja.
   *
   * Devuelve las entidades; es el controller el que las convierte al DTO
   * pÃºblico, sin identidad del paciente.
   */
  async listForDoctor(doctorId: string): Promise<Review[]> {
    const reviews = await this.repository.findByDoctorId(doctorId)
    return [...reviews].sort(
      (a, b) => b.createdAt.getTime() - a.createdAt.getTime()
    )
  }

  /**
   * Promedio y cantidad, calculados desde la tabla y no desde la copia
   * cacheada en `doctors`: es la fuente de verdad.
   */
  async summaryForDoctor(doctorId: string): Promise<RatingSummary> {
    return this.repository.summaryForDoctor(doctorId)
  }

  /** Turnos del paciente que ya calificÃ³, para no volver a ofrecerlo. */
  async reviewedAppointmentIds(user: SessionUser): Promise<string[]> {
    const patient = await this.patientService.findByUserId(user.id)
    if (!patient) return []
    return this.repository.findAppointmentIdsByPatient(patient.id)
  }

  /**
   * Solo el paciente que fue atendido puede calificar, una sola vez, y
   * Ãºnicamente un turno ya completado: son las tres condiciones que hacen que
   * una estrella signifique algo.
   */
  async create(user: SessionUser, input: ReviewInput): Promise<Review> {
    const patient = await this.patientService.findByUserId(user.id)
    if (!patient) {
      throw new Error("No se encontrÃ³ el perfil del paciente.")
    }

    const appointment = await this.appointmentRepository.findOne(
      input.appointmentId
    )
    if (!appointment) throw new Error("El turno no existe")

    if (appointment.patientId !== patient.id) {
      throw new Error("Solo podÃ©s calificar tus propios turnos")
    }

    if (appointment.status !== "completed") {
      throw new Error(
        "Solo se pueden calificar los turnos que ya fueron atendidos"
      )
    }

    const existing = await this.repository.findByAppointmentId(appointment.id)
    if (existing) {
      throw new Error("Este turno ya fue calificado")
    }

    const review = await this.repository.create(
      new Review(
        crypto.randomUUID(),
        appointment.id,
        appointment.doctorId,
        patient.id,
        input.score,
        input.comment ?? null
      )
    )

    await this.refreshDoctorRating(appointment.doctorId)
    await this.notificationService.reviewReceived(appointment, input.score)

    return review
  }

  /**
   * Recalcula el promedio guardado en `doctors`. La fuente de verdad es la
   * tabla `reviews`; la columna es solo una copia para no rehacer el promedio
   * en cada listado.
   */
  async refreshDoctorRating(doctorId: string): Promise<void> {
    const summary = await this.repository.summaryForDoctor(doctorId)
    await this.medicRepository.setRating(
      doctorId,
      summary.average,
      summary.total
    )
  }
}

===== .\src\services\SeedService.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { auth } from "@/lib/auth/authServer"
import type { SeedAccount, SeedMedic } from "@/lib/seedData"
import { Branch, type BranchDto } from "@/models/Branch"
import { Medic } from "@/models/Medic"
import { Specialty, type SpecialtyDto } from "@/models/Specialty"
import type { BranchRepository } from "@/repository/BranchRepository"
import type { MedicRepository } from "@/repository/MedicRepository"
import type { PatientRepository } from "@/repository/PatientRepository"
import type { SpecialtyRepository } from "@/repository/SpecialtyRepository"
import type { UserRepository } from "@/repository/UserRepository"

export interface CatalogSeedResult {
  branches: number
  specialties: number
  medics: number
}

export class SeedService {
  private userRepository: UserRepository
  private patientRepository: PatientRepository
  private medicRepository: MedicRepository
  private specialtyRepository: SpecialtyRepository
  private branchRepository: BranchRepository

  constructor(
    userRepository: UserRepository,
    patientRepository: PatientRepository,
    medicRepository: MedicRepository,
    specialtyRepository: SpecialtyRepository,
    branchRepository: BranchRepository
  ) {
    this.userRepository = userRepository
    this.patientRepository = patientRepository
    this.medicRepository = medicRepository
    this.specialtyRepository = specialtyRepository
    this.branchRepository = branchRepository
  }

  async seedAccounts(accounts: SeedAccount[]): Promise<string[]> {
    const results: string[] = []

    for (const account of accounts) {
      results.push(await this.ensureAccount(account))
      await this.applyRoleAndLinks(account)
    }

    return results
  }

  async seedCatalog(
    branches: Omit<BranchDto, "doctorCount">[],
    specialties: SpecialtyDto[],
    medics: SeedMedic[]
  ): Promise<CatalogSeedResult> {
    // Las sedes van primero: los mÃ©dicos las referencian.
    for (const branch of branches) {
      await this.branchRepository.createIfMissing(
        new Branch(
          branch.id,
          branch.name,
          branch.address,
          branch.phone,
          branch.active
        )
      )
    }

    for (const specialty of specialties) {
      await this.specialtyRepository.createIfMissing(
        new Specialty(
          specialty.id,
          specialty.name,
          specialty.description,
          specialty.icon,
          specialty.activeDoctors
        )
      )
    }

    for (const medic of medics) {
      await this.medicRepository.createIfMissing(
        new Medic({
          id: medic.id,
          firstName: medic.firstName,
          lastName: medic.lastName,
          email: medic.email,
          phone: medic.phone,
          specialtyId: medic.specialtyId,
          specialtyName: medic.specialtyName,
          branchId: medic.branchId,
          office: medic.office,
          avatar: medic.avatar,
          license: medic.license,
          yearsExperience: medic.yearsExperience,
          workDays: medic.workDays,
          // Sin reseÃ±as todavÃ­a: las estrellas se ganan atendiendo.
          rating: 0,
          reviewCount: 0,
        })
      )
    }

    return {
      branches: branches.length,
      specialties: specialties.length,
      medics: medics.length,
    }
  }

  private async ensureAccount(account: SeedAccount): Promise<string> {
    const existing = await this.userRepository.findByEmail(account.email)
    if (existing) return `ya existÃ­a: ${account.email}`

    try {
      await auth.api.signUpEmail({
        body: {
          email: account.email,
          password: account.password,
          name: account.name,
        },
      })
      return `creado: ${account.email}`
    } catch (error) {
      return `error al crear ${account.email}: ${(error as Error).message}`
    }
  }

  private async applyRoleAndLinks(account: SeedAccount): Promise<void> {
    const row = await this.userRepository.findByEmail(account.email)
    if (!row) return

    await this.userRepository.updateRole(row.id, account.role)

    if (account.patientId) {
      await this.patientRepository.update(account.patientId, {
        userId: row.id,
      })
    }
    if (account.medicId) {
      await this.medicRepository.update(account.medicId, { userId: row.id })
    }
  }
}

===== .\src\services\SpecialtyService.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { Specialty, type SpecialtyInput } from "@/models/Specialty"
import type { MedicRepository } from "@/repository/MedicRepository"
import type { SpecialtyRepository } from "@/repository/SpecialtyRepository"

export class SpecialtyService {
  private repository: SpecialtyRepository
  private medicRepository: MedicRepository

  constructor(
    repository: SpecialtyRepository,
    medicRepository: MedicRepository
  ) {
    this.repository = repository
    this.medicRepository = medicRepository
  }

  findAll(): Promise<Specialty[]> {
    return this.repository.findAll()
  }

  findOne(id: string): Promise<Specialty | undefined> {
    return this.repository.findOne(id)
  }

  async create(input: SpecialtyInput): Promise<Specialty> {
    const duplicate = await this.repository.findByName(input.name)
    if (duplicate) {
      throw new Error(`Ya existe una especialidad llamada "${duplicate.name}"`)
    }

    const specialty = new Specialty(
      crypto.randomUUID(),
      input.name,
      input.description,
      input.icon,
      0
    )
    return this.repository.create(specialty)
  }

  async update(
    id: string,
    input: SpecialtyInput
  ): Promise<Specialty | undefined> {
    const current = await this.repository.findOne(id)
    if (!current) throw new Error("La especialidad no existe")

    const duplicate = await this.repository.findOtherByName(input.name, id)
    if (duplicate) {
      throw new Error(`Ya existe una especialidad llamada "${duplicate.name}"`)
    }

    // El nombre anterior se copia ANTES del UPDATE: compararlo despuÃ©s solo
    // funciona mientras el repositorio devuelva instancias nuevas en cada
    // consulta, y de eso depende que el renombre se propague a los mÃ©dicos.
    const previousName = current.name

    const updated = await this.repository.update(id, input)

    if (previousName !== input.name) {
      await this.medicRepository.renameSpecialty(id, input.name)
    }

    return updated
  }

  /**
   * Deleting a specialty that still has doctors would leave every one of them
   * pointing at an id that no longer resolves, so the operation is refused with
   * the reason instead.
   */
  async remove(id: string): Promise<{ id: string } | undefined> {
    const specialty = await this.repository.findOne(id)
    if (!specialty) throw new Error("La especialidad no existe")

    const assigned = await this.medicRepository.countBySpecialtyId(id)
    if (assigned > 0) {
      throw new Error(
        `No se puede eliminar "${specialty.name}": tiene ${assigned} ${
          assigned === 1 ? "mÃ©dico asignado" : "mÃ©dicos asignados"
        }. ReasignÃ¡ esos profesionales primero.`
      )
    }

    return this.repository.delete(id)
  }

  /** Recomputes the badge shown in the admin grid after a doctor changes. */
  async refreshDoctorCount(specialtyId: string): Promise<void> {
    if (!specialtyId) return
    const total = await this.medicRepository.countBySpecialtyId(specialtyId)
    await this.repository.setActiveDoctors(specialtyId, total)
  }
}

===== .\src\services\UserService.ts =====

import "server-only"
import { auth } from "@/lib/auth/authServer"
import type { Role, UserInput, UserRow } from "@/models/User"
import type { UserRepository } from "@/repository/UserRepository"
import type { MedicService } from "./MedicService"
import type { PatientService } from "./PatientService"

export class UserService {
  private repository: UserRepository
  private medicService: MedicService
  private patientService: PatientService

  constructor(
    repository: UserRepository,
    medicService: MedicService,
    patientService: PatientService
  ) {
    this.repository = repository
    this.medicService = medicService
    this.patientService = patientService
  }

  findAll(): Promise<UserRow[]> {
    return this.repository.findAll()
  }

  /**
   * Better Auth owns password hashing, so the account is created through its
   * sign-up API and only then promoted to the requested role.
   */
  async create(input: UserInput, password: string): Promise<UserRow> {
    const existing = await this.repository.findByEmail(input.email)
    if (existing) {
      throw new Error("Ya existe un usuario con ese email")
    }

    await auth.api.signUpEmail({
      body: { email: input.email, password, name: input.name },
    })

    const created = await this.repository.findByEmail(input.email)
    if (!created) {
      throw new Error("No se pudo crear la cuenta")
    }

    await this.applyRole(created.id, created.name, created.email, input.role)

    return (await this.repository.findById(created.id)) ?? created
  }

  async update(
    id: string,
    input: UserInput,
    currentUserId: string
  ): Promise<UserRow | undefined> {
    const target = await this.repository.findById(id)
    if (!target) throw new Error("El usuario no existe")

    const duplicate = await this.repository.findOtherByEmail(input.email, id)
    if (duplicate) {
      throw new Error("Ya existe otro usuario con ese email")
    }

    await this.assertAdminSeatSurvives(target, input.role, currentUserId)

    // El rol anterior se copia ANTES del UPDATE. Compararlo despuÃ©s solo
    // funciona mientras el repositorio devuelva instancias nuevas, y de eso
    // depende que al pasar a mÃ©dico o paciente se le cree la ficha.
    const previousRole = target.role

    const updated = await this.repository.updateProfile(id, input)
    if (input.role !== previousRole) {
      await this.applyRole(id, input.name, input.email, input.role)
    }
    return updated
  }

  async remove(
    id: string,
    currentUserId: string
  ): Promise<{ id: string } | undefined> {
    if (id === currentUserId) {
      throw new Error("No podÃ©s eliminar tu propia cuenta")
    }

    const target = await this.repository.findById(id)
    if (!target) throw new Error("El usuario no existe")

    if (target.role === "admin") {
      const admins = await this.repository.countByRole("admin")
      if (admins <= 1) {
        throw new Error(
          "No se puede eliminar al Ãºltimo administrador del sistema"
        )
      }
    }

    return this.repository.delete(id)
  }

  /**
   * Blocks the change that would leave the system with no administrator and
   * nobody able to reach the admin panel again.
   */
  private async assertAdminSeatSurvives(
    target: UserRow,
    nextRole: Role,
    currentUserId: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    if (target.role !== "admin" || nextRole === "admin") return

    const admins = await this.repository.countByRole("admin")
    if (admins <= 1) {
      throw new Error("Debe quedar al menos un administrador en el sistema")
    }
    if (target.id === currentUserId) {
      throw new Error("No podÃ©s quitarte a vos mismo el rol de administrador")
    }
  }

  /** Keeps the role column and the matching domain record in sync. */
  private async applyRole(
    userId: string,
    name: string,
    email: string,
    role: Role
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.repository.updateRole(userId, role)

    if (role === "patient") {
      await this.patientService.ensureForUser(userId, name, email)
    } else if (role === "doctor") {
      await this.medicService.ensureForUser(userId, name, email)
    }
  }
}

===== .\src\validations\AppointmentValidation.ts =====

import type { AppointmentInput, AppointmentStatus } from "@/models/Appointment"

const isoDatePattern = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/
const timePattern = /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/
const validStatuses: AppointmentStatus[] = [
  "scheduled",
  "completed",
  "cancelled",
  "absent",
]

export function sanitizeAppointmentInput(raw: unknown): AppointmentInput {
  const input = (raw ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>

  const doctorId =
    typeof input.doctorId === "string" ? input.doctorId.trim() : ""
  if (!doctorId) throw new Error("ElegÃ­ un profesional")

  const date = typeof input.date === "string" ? input.date.trim() : ""
  if (!isoDatePattern.test(date)) throw new Error("Fecha invÃ¡lida")

  const time = typeof input.time === "string" ? input.time.trim() : ""
  if (!timePattern.test(time)) throw new Error("Hora invÃ¡lida")

  const reason =
    typeof input.reason === "string" && input.reason.trim()
      ? input.reason.trim()
      : undefined

  return { doctorId, date, time, reason }
}

/** Fecha y hora nuevas al reprogramar: mismo formato que al reservar. */
export function sanitizeReschedule(
  rawDate: unknown,
  rawTime: unknown
): { date: string; time: string } {
  const date = typeof rawDate === "string" ? rawDate.trim() : ""
  if (!isoDatePattern.test(date)) throw new Error("Fecha invÃ¡lida")

  const time = typeof rawTime === "string" ? rawTime.trim() : ""
  if (!timePattern.test(time)) throw new Error("Hora invÃ¡lida")

  return { date, time }
}

export function sanitizeAppointmentStatus(raw: unknown): AppointmentStatus {
  if (
    typeof raw !== "string" ||
    !validStatuses.includes(raw as AppointmentStatus)
  ) {
    throw new Error("Estado de turno invÃ¡lido")
  }
  return raw as AppointmentStatus
}

export function sanitizeRole(raw: unknown): Role {
  if (typeof raw !== "string" || !validRoles.includes(raw as Role)) {
    throw new Error("Rol invÃ¡lido")
  }
  return raw as Role
}

import type { BranchInput } from "@/models/Branch"

export function sanitizeBranchInput(raw: unknown): BranchInput {
  const input = (raw ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>

  const name = typeof input.name === "string" ? input.name.trim() : ""
  if (!name) throw new Error("El nombre de la sede es obligatorio")

  const address = typeof input.address === "string" ? input.address.trim() : ""
  if (!address) throw new Error("La direcciÃ³n es obligatoria")

  return {
    name,
    address,
    phone: typeof input.phone === "string" ? input.phone.trim() : "",
    // Una sede se crea activa salvo que se indique lo contrario.
    active: input.active === undefined ? true : Boolean(input.active),
  }
}

===== .\src\validations\MedicValidation.ts =====

import {
  DEFAULT_SCHEDULE,
  generateTimeSlots,
  toMinutes,
} from "@/lib/bookingSlots"
import type { MedicInput } from "@/models/Medic"

const emailPattern = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/
const officePattern = /^[A-Za-z0-9\s.\-/]{1,12}$/
const defaultWorkDays = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]

function sanitizeWorkDays(value: unknown): number[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return defaultWorkDays
  const days = value
    .map((day) => Number(day))
    .filter((day) => Number.isInteger(day) && day >= 0 && day <= 6)
  return days.length > 0 ? days : defaultWorkDays
}

/** El consultorio es una etiqueta corta: "101", "3B", "PB-2". */
function sanitizeOffice(value: unknown): string {
  const office = typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : ""
  if (!office) return ""

  if (!officePattern.test(office)) {
    throw new Error(
      "El consultorio solo admite letras, nÃºmeros y guiones (hasta 12 caracteres)"
    )
  }
  return office
}

/** Hora "HH:MM" vÃ¡lida, o el valor por defecto si viene mal. */
function sanitizeTime(value: unknown, fallback: string): string {
  const texto = typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : ""
  return toMinutes(texto) === null ? fallback : texto
}

/**
 * Jornada de atenciÃ³n del profesional.
 *
 * Se valida como conjunto y no campo por campo: una hora de cierre anterior a
 * la de apertura, o una duraciÃ³n que no deja entrar ni un turno, dejan al
 * profesional sin horarios disponibles y nadie puede reservar con Ã©l. Es mejor
 * rechazarlo en el formulario que descubrirlo al no ver turnos.
 */
function sanitizeSchedule(input: Record<string, unknown>) {
  const startTime = sanitizeTime(input.startTime, DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.startTime)
  const endTime = sanitizeTime(input.endTime, DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.endTime)

  // El corte es opcional: vacÃ­o en los dos campos significa jornada continua.
  const breakStart =
    typeof input.breakStart === "string" && input.breakStart.trim()
      ? sanitizeTime(input.breakStart, DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.breakStart)
      : ""
  const breakEnd =
    typeof input.breakEnd === "string" && input.breakEnd.trim()
      ? sanitizeTime(input.breakEnd, DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.breakEnd)
      : ""

  const slotMinutes = Math.trunc(
    Number(input.slotMinutes ?? DEFAULT_SCHEDULE.slotMinutes)
  )

  if ((toMinutes(endTime) ?? 0) <= (toMinutes(startTime) ?? 0)) {
    throw new Error("El horario de cierre debe ser posterior al de apertura")
  }

  if (!Number.isFinite(slotMinutes) || slotMinutes < 5 || slotMinutes > 240) {
    throw new Error("La duraciÃ³n de la consulta debe estar entre 5 y 240 minutos")
  }

  if (breakStart && breakEnd && (toMinutes(breakEnd) ?? 0) <= (toMinutes(breakStart) ?? 0)) {
    throw new Error("El fin del descanso debe ser posterior a su inicio")
  }

  const schedule = { startTime, endTime, breakStart, breakEnd, slotMinutes }

  if (generateTimeSlots(schedule).length === 0) {
    throw new Error(
      "Con esa jornada no entra ningÃºn turno: revisÃ¡ los horarios y la duraciÃ³n"
    )
  }

  return schedule
}

export function sanitizeMedicInput(raw: unknown): MedicInput {
  const input = (raw ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>

  const firstName =
    typeof input.firstName === "string" ? input.firstName.trim() : ""
  if (!firstName) throw new Error("El nombre es obligatorio")

  const lastName =
    typeof input.lastName === "string" ? input.lastName.trim() : ""
  if (!lastName) throw new Error("El apellido es obligatorio")

  const email = typeof input.email === "string" ? input.email.trim() : ""
  if (!emailPattern.test(email)) throw new Error("Email invÃ¡lido")

  const branchId =
    typeof input.branchId === "string" ? input.branchId.trim() : ""
  const office = sanitizeOffice(input.office)

  // Un consultorio sin sede no identifica ningÃºn lugar: "Consultorio 4" no
  // dice nada si no se sabe en quÃ© edificio estÃ¡.
  if (office && !branchId) {
    throw new Error("Para indicar un consultorio hay que elegir una sede")
  }

  const yearsExperience = Number(input.yearsExperience ?? 1)

  return {
    firstName,
    lastName,
    email,
    phone: typeof input.phone === "string" ? input.phone.trim() : "",
    specialtyId:
      typeof input.specialtyId === "string" ? input.specialtyId.trim() : "",
    // Lo reescribe MedicService leyÃ©ndolo de la especialidad elegida.
    specialtyName: "",
    branchId,
    office,
    avatar: typeof input.avatar === "string" ? input.avatar.trim() : "",
    license: typeof input.license === "string" ? input.license.trim() : "",
    yearsExperience: Number.isNaN(yearsExperience)
      ? 1
      : Math.max(0, yearsExperience),
    workDays: sanitizeWorkDays(input.workDays),
    ...sanitizeSchedule(input),
  }
}

===== .\src\validations\PatientValidation.ts =====

import {
  DNI_MAX_DIGITS,
  DNI_MIN_DIGITS,
  normalizeDni,
} from "@/lib/format/dni"
import { parseISODate, todayISODate } from "@/lib/format/date"
import type { PatientInput } from "@/models/Patient"

const emailPattern = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/
const isoDatePattern = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/

/** Nadie documentado supera esta edad; sirve para atajar aÃ±os tipeados mal. */
const MAX_AGE_YEARS = 120

function sanitizeDni(raw: unknown): string {
  const digits = normalizeDni(typeof raw === "string" ? raw : "")

  if (!digits) throw new Error("El DNI es obligatorio")
  if (digits.length < DNI_MIN_DIGITS || digits.length > DNI_MAX_DIGITS) {
    throw new Error(
      `El DNI debe tener entre ${DNI_MIN_DIGITS} y ${DNI_MAX_DIGITS} dÃ­gitos`
    )
  }
  return digits
}

function sanitizeBirthDate(raw: unknown): string {
  const value = typeof raw === "string" ? raw.trim() : ""
  if (!value) throw new Error("La fecha de nacimiento es obligatoria")
  if (!isoDatePattern.test(value)) {
    throw new Error("La fecha de nacimiento no tiene un formato vÃ¡lido")
  }

  const birth = parseISODate(value)
  if (Number.isNaN(birth.getTime())) {
    throw new Error("La fecha de nacimiento no es una fecha real")
  }

  // parseISODate normaliza los desbordes (2026-02-31 pasa a marzo), asÃ­ que se
  // compara contra el texto original para detectar un dÃ­a que no existe.
  const [year, month, day] = value.split("-").map(Number)
  if (
    birth.getFullYear() !== year ||
    birth.getMonth() !== month - 1 ||
    birth.getDate() !== day
  ) {
    throw new Error("La fecha de nacimiento no es una fecha real")
  }

  if (value > todayISODate()) {
    throw new Error("La fecha de nacimiento no puede ser futura")
  }

  const oldest = new Date()
  oldest.setFullYear(oldest.getFullYear() - MAX_AGE_YEARS)
  if (birth < oldest) {
    throw new Error(`La fecha de nacimiento no puede superar los ${MAX_AGE_YEARS} aÃ±os`)
  }

  return value
}

export function sanitizePatientInput(raw: unknown): PatientInput {
  const input = (raw ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>

  const firstName =
    typeof input.firstName === "string" ? input.firstName.trim() : ""
  if (!firstName) throw new Error("El nombre es obligatorio")

  const lastName =
    typeof input.lastName === "string" ? input.lastName.trim() : ""
  if (!lastName) throw new Error("El apellido es obligatorio")

  const email = typeof input.email === "string" ? input.email.trim() : ""
  if (!emailPattern.test(email)) throw new Error("Email invÃ¡lido")

  return {
    firstName,
    lastName,
    email,
    phone: typeof input.phone === "string" ? input.phone.trim() : "",
    dni: sanitizeDni(input.dni),
    birthDate: sanitizeBirthDate(input.birthDate),
    bloodType:
      typeof input.bloodType === "string" && input.bloodType.trim()
        ? input.bloodType.trim()
        : undefined,
  }
}

===== .\src\validations\ReviewValidation.ts =====

import { MAX_SCORE, MIN_SCORE, type ReviewInput } from "@/models/Review"

const MAX_COMMENT_LENGTH = 500

export function sanitizeReviewInput(raw: unknown): ReviewInput {
  const input = (raw ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>

  const appointmentId =
    typeof input.appointmentId === "string" ? input.appointmentId.trim() : ""
  if (!appointmentId) throw new Error("Falta indicar el turno a calificar")

  const score = Number(input.score)
  if (!Number.isInteger(score) || score < MIN_SCORE || score > MAX_SCORE) {
    throw new Error(
      `La calificaciÃ³n debe ser un nÃºmero entero entre ${MIN_SCORE} y ${MAX_SCORE}`
    )
  }

  const rawComment = typeof input.comment === "string" ? input.comment.trim() : ""
  if (rawComment.length > MAX_COMMENT_LENGTH) {
    throw new Error(
      `El comentario no puede superar los ${MAX_COMMENT_LENGTH} caracteres`
    )
  }

  return {
    appointmentId,
    score,
    comment: rawComment || undefined,
  }
}

===== .\src\validations\SpecialtyValidation.ts =====

import {
  DEFAULT_SPECIALTY_ICON,
  specialtyIconOptions,
} from "@/lib/specialtyIconNames"
import type { SpecialtyInput } from "@/models/Specialty"

export function sanitizeSpecialtyInput(raw: unknown): SpecialtyInput {
  const input = (raw ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>

  const name = typeof input.name === "string" ? input.name.trim() : ""
  if (!name) throw new Error("El nombre es obligatorio")

  const description =
    typeof input.description === "string" ? input.description.trim() : ""
  if (!description) throw new Error("La descripciÃ³n es obligatoria")

  // An icon outside the catalogue would render as a blank card, so anything
  // unknown falls back to the default instead of being stored as-is.
  const rawIcon = typeof input.icon === "string" ? input.icon.trim() : ""
  const icon: string = (specialtyIconOptions as readonly string[]).includes(
    rawIcon
  )
    ? rawIcon
    : DEFAULT_SPECIALTY_ICON

  return { name, description, icon }
}

===== .\src\validations\UserValidation.ts =====

import type { Role, UserInput } from "@/models/User"

const emailPattern = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/
const validRoles: Role[] = ["patient", "doctor", "admin"]

/** Better Auth rejects anything shorter, so it is checked before the round trip. */
export const MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 8

export function sanitizeUserInput(raw: unknown): UserInput {
  const input = (raw ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>

  const name = typeof input.name === "string" ? input.name.trim() : ""
  if (!name) throw new Error("El nombre es obligatorio")

  const email =
    typeof input.email === "string" ? input.email.trim().toLowerCase() : ""
  if (!emailPattern.test(email)) throw new Error("Email invÃ¡lido")

  const role = input.role
  if (typeof role !== "string" || !validRoles.includes(role as Role)) {
    throw new Error("Rol invÃ¡lido")
  }

  return { name, email, role: role as Role }
}

export function sanitizePassword(raw: unknown): string {
  const password = typeof raw === "string" ? raw : ""
  if (password.length < MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH) {
    throw new Error(
      `La contraseÃ±a debe tener al menos ${MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH} caracteres`
    )
  }
  return password
}
